Existing approaches typically retrieve a textual counterpart for each image and then refine multimodal representations by directly enforcing cross-modal agreement, e.g., maximizing image-text similarity inherited from pretrained VLMs. However, such a strategy aligns heterogeneous representations across modalities without explicitly modeling the intrinsic structure within each modality and thus might yield unreliable alignment or distort modality-specific structures that are crucial for clustering. In this paper, we propose a simple but principled approach, termed deep modality-shared self-expressive model (DeepMORSE), which discovers cross-modal structures via a modality-shared self-expressive model and simultaneously learns structured representations that conform to a union of modality-specific subspaces. Moreover, we theoretically justify that the modality-shared self-expressive coefficients suppress inter-class noise towards a subspace-preserving solution, and show that mini-batch optimization procedure introduces an implicit regularization onto the self-expressive model. We evaluate our DeepMORSE on six widely used image clustering benchmarks and observe performance improvements exceeding 3% on the UCF-101, DTD-47, and ImageNet-Dogs datasets. In addition, we demonstrate the strong transferability of the learned representations by achieving state-of-the-art performance on downstream tasks such as image retrieval and zero-shot classification---without requiring any task-specific losses or post-processing. The code is available at: https://github.com/mengxianghan123/DeepMORSE.

</details>


### [100] [FreCast: Refining Radar Echo Intensity via Phase-Preserving Amplitude Residual Diffusion for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08436)
*Heping Fang,Zihuai Yin,Kaicheng Mao,Peiguang Zhang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreCast是一个两阶段的雷达回波预测框架。第一阶段生成初始的雷达回波预测，第二阶段利用初始预测的空间结构作为约束，进一步校正单个位置上的强度偏差。在三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在预测技能指标上取得了一致的提升，并在较长预测时段内更好地保持了雨带连续性和强降水结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型虽然能大致捕捉未来降水的整体空间分布，但在单个位置的雷达回波强度预测上仍存在显著偏差。这促使研究者采用一种更有针对性的策略来减少预测误差——不再无空间约束地重新生成整个雷达回波序列，而是利用预测的降水结构来引导回波强度在单个位置上的细化修正。

Method: FreCast采用两阶段框架：第一阶段生成未来雷达回波的初始预测；第二阶段以初始预测的空间结构作为约束，进一步修正第一阶段预测中单个位置的强度偏差。这种结构引导的细化方式直接针对回波强度偏差进行校正。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，FreCast在预报技能指标上取得了一致的改进。定性结果进一步显示，FreCast在较长的预测时段内更好地保持了雨带连续性和强降水结构。

Conclusion: FreCast通过结构约束引导的强度校正策略，有效减少了雷达回波强度在单个位置的预测偏差，在多种气象预报技能指标上优于现有方法，并改善了较长时效下的降水结构保持能力。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting predicts the spatiotemporal evolution of future radar echoes from historical radar echo sequences, thereby estimating the occurrence, development, and movement of precipitation over the near term. In recent years, deep learning has become an important approach to precipitation nowcasting. Although state-of-the-art models can generally capture the overall spatial distribution of future precipitation, their predictions still exhibit substantial biases in radar echo intensity at individual locations. This observation motivates a more targeted strategy for reducing forecast errors. Instead of regenerating an entire radar echo sequence without spatial constraints, the predicted precipitation structure can be used to guide the refinement of echo intensities at individual locations. This structure-guided refinement directly targets echo intensity biases. Accordingly, we propose FreCast, a two-stage framework for radar echo prediction. The first stage generates an initial forecast of future radar echoes. The second stage uses the spatial structure of the initial forecast as a constraint to further correct intensity biases at individual locations in the first-stage prediction. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that FreCast achieves consistent improvements across forecast skill metrics. Qualitative results further show that FreCast better preserves rainband continuity and intense precipitation structures at longer lead times.

</details>


### [101] [InstructionCrafter: Generating Consistent and High-Fidelity Visual Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08460)
*Shun Okamoto,Satoshi Iizuka,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InstructionCrafter，一个基于视频扩散模型的框架，用于根据文本指令生成逐步的视觉指令图像序列。核心思路是将时间/指令对齐优化与逐帧视觉质量优化分离，通过空间层冻结训练和两个轻量级适配器（Consistent Adapter和Context-Aware Temporal Adapter），在保持逐帧质量的同时实现步骤忠实度、跨图一致性和视觉质量三方面的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从文本指令生成逐步视觉指令图像序列需要同时满足三个关键性质：步骤忠实度（step faithfulness）、跨图像一致性（cross-image consistency）和逐帧视觉质量（per-frame visual quality）。然而现有文生图方法难以同时满足这三者：独立采样破坏一致性，在低质量视频上微调降低逐帧质量，冻结的骨干网络缺乏多步理解能力。

Method: InstructionCrafter基于预训练视频扩散骨干搭建，采用（1）空间冻结训练（spatial-freeze training）：冻结控制逐帧细节的空间层，仅更新时间层和文本条件通路，从而保留生成先验并减少约50%可训练参数；（2）指令感知适配器：包括Consistent Adapter，聚合整个指令序列和相邻步骤的文本线索以保持对象身份和属性一致；以及Context-Aware Temporal Adapter，将交叉注意力输出转化为时间自注意力的偏置，显式传播帧间关系。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，InstructionCrafter在步骤忠实度、跨图像一致性和逐帧视觉质量三个维度上均达到最先进的整体性能，同时显著减少了噪声、模糊和虚假字幕。

Conclusion: 通过将时间/指令对齐优化与逐帧视觉质量优化解耦，并引入指令感知适配器，可以有效解决视觉指令序列生成中多性质难以同时满足的难题。代码和训练模型将公开。

Abstract: Given textual task instructions, generating step-by-step visual instructions as an image sequence requires the simultaneous satisfaction of multiple properties, specifically step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality. Existing text-to-image generation approaches rarely meet all three properties, owing to independent sampling that breaks consistency, finetuning on low-quality video that degrades per-frame quality, and frozen backbones that lack multi-step understanding. In this work, we propose InstructionCrafter, a diffusion-based framework with the key idea of separating the optimization of temporal and instructional alignment from per-frame visual quality via (1) spatial-freeze training and (2) instruction-aware adapters. Built on a pretrained video diffusion backbone, InstructionCrafter freezes the spatial layers that control per-frame detail and updates only temporal and text-conditioning pathways to learn instruction semantics and inter-step relations, which preserves the generative prior for per-frame quality and reduces trainable parameters by about 50 percent compared with full finetuning. We also introduce two lightweight adapters that enhance the model's understanding of instructional context. The Consistent Adapter aggregates textual cues from the entire instruction sequence and from neighboring steps to keep object identity and attributes consistent across frames, and the Context-Aware Temporal Adapter converts cross-attention outputs into biases for temporal self-attention, explicitly propagating inter-frame relations. Extensive experiments on two benchmark datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art overall performance on step faithfulness, cross-image consistency, and per-frame visual quality while significantly reducing noise, blur, and spurious subtitles. Our code and trained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [102] [RayLift: Lifting Complementary Ray-Wise Evidence with 3D Geometry Priors for Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08476)
*Meng Wang,Hongxia Yu,Wenzhe He,Xingdong Song,Huilong Pi,Jiapeng Zhang,Ruihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayLift是一个基于立体几何作为度量参考并结合互补光线证据的自适应3D语义场景补全框架，通过三个核心模块（互补上下文编码器、深度光线证据提升器、语义感知体素积分器）有效缓解深度不确定性对体素表示的负面传播，在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的3D语义场景补全方法将立体深度估计视为确定性几何约束，导致深度不确定性和局部匹配误差直接传播到体素表示中，降低了场景理解的可靠性。

Method: RayLift包含三个核心组件：(1) 互补上下文编码器，从冻结的3D视觉基础模型中提取几何感知先验以丰富场景上下文；(2) 深度光线证据提升模块，联合建模几何差异性、深度置信度和空间不确定性，沿每条相机光线自适应采样并加权候选表面位置；(3) 语义感知体素积分器，通过显式建模空间支撑信息将光线证据注入体素特征。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和SSCBench-KITTI-360基准上，RayLift取得了具有竞争力的性能，并持续优于现有的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将立体几何仅作为度量参考而非确定性约束，并自适应地利用光线证据恢复可靠3D结构，RayLift有效地解决了深度不确定性传播问题，为相机式3D语义场景补全提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D semantic scene completion (SSC) provides comprehensive scene understanding for autonomous driving and robotics. However, existing methods often treat stereo depth estimates as deterministic geometric constraints, causing depth uncertainty and local correspondence errors to propagate directly into voxel representations. To address this issue, we propose RayLift, a framework that uses stereo geometry as a metric reference while incorporating complementary ray evidence to recover reliable 3D structures adaptively. RayLift first employs a Complementary Context Encoder that extracts geometry-aware priors from a frozen 3D vision foundation model, thereby enriching the scene context. It then introduces a Depth Ray Evidence Lifter module that jointly models geometric dissimilarity, depth confidence, and spatial uncertainty to adaptively sample and weight candidate surface locations along each camera ray. Finally, a Semantic-Aware Voxel Integrator injects the resulting ray evidence into voxel features by explicitly modeling their spatial support. Extensive experiments on SemanticKITTI and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate that RayLift achieves competitive performance and consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [103] [RenderMatte: Exact-Alpha Rendering and Group-Relative Alignment for Image Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08487)
*Zecheng Ren,Yafei Hu,Jianing Zhao,Ruichen Cong,Qun Jin,Yiren Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenderMatte是一个基于FLUX.1 Kontext的trimap引导图像抠图框架，通过全参数微调、alpha-edge目标函数和group-relative alpha alignment后训练，结合大规模合成数据集，在开放世界场景中实现了SOTA抠图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界场景中前景具有高度多样化的外观和透明度模式，现有方法在语义模糊和细粒度透明度变化上表现不佳，尤其在稀疏边界区域难以监督。需要一种能利用图像编辑先验进行结构保持的alpha预测方法。

Method: 1) 将FLUX.1 Kontext通过全参数微调适配为trimap引导的抠图模型；2) 引入alpha-edge目标函数，在保持潜在流匹配信号的同时加强像素空间边界监督；3) 提出group-relative alpha alignment后训练，在相同trimap条件下对多个采样matte使用抠图特定奖励进行优化；4) 构建RenderMatte数据集，结合3D渲染RGBA前景与多源资产，提供精确的strand级alpha标注和多样化背景合成。

Result: 在所有基准测试上均达到最先进的性能，验证了该方法的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 展示了通过结合生成模型先验、针对性训练目标和高质量合成数据，可实现开放世界场景中高保真抠图的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Image matting is an essential enabling technology for modern visual content production, where foreground extraction determines the realism and editability of downstream creation workflows. However, precise alpha estimation in open-world scenes remains challenging because real foregrounds exhibit highly diverse appearances and opacity patterns. This makes existing methods struggle with semantic ambiguity and fine-grained opacity variation, especially in sparse boundary regions that are fragile and difficult to supervise. To address this gap, we present RenderMatte, a trimap-guided matting framework that adapts FLUX.1 Kontext through full-parameter fine-tuning, leveraging image editing priors for structure-preserving alpha prediction. During supervised adaptation, an alpha-edge objective preserves the latent flow-matching signal while strengthening pixel-space boundary supervision. We further introduce group-relative alpha alignment for post-training. It compares multiple mattes sampled under the same trimap condition using matting-specific rewards for alpha accuracy, boundary fidelity, trimap compliance, and compositional consistency. To overcome the lack of precise edge annotations, we construct the RenderMatte dataset, a large-scale synthetic dataset combining 3D-rendered RGBA foregrounds with diverse multi-source assets. It features exact strand-level alpha annotations and diverse background composites. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks, demonstrating a scalable path toward high-fidelity matting in open-world scenes.

</details>


### [104] [Linguistically-Aligned and Visually-Grounded Preference Optimization for Clinically-Augmented Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08494)
*Qiang Hu,Yuxuan Luo,Yingjie Guo,Hao Wang,Qimei Wang,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DPO-Clin，一个面向医学报告生成的偏好优化后训练框架，通过实体级临床诊断、检索增强的多模态DPO和反事实修改策略，解决现有DPO方法在MRG中偏好构造粗糙、缺乏视觉-语言对齐的问题，在三个数据集上显著提升临床相关指标的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRG模型存在事实错误；基于DPO的后训练方法虽然有效，但采用朴素的偏好构造（直接将模型生成报告与真实报告配对），这会将临床关键发现与语言风格差异纠缠在一起，且缺乏明确的跨模态对齐，导致模型可靠性受限。

Method: DPO-Clin包含三个核心模块：(1) 实体级临床诊断（ECD）模块，进行精确的实体级事实诊断，指导生成语言对齐的报告偏好对，将临床差异与语言变异分离；(2) M²DPO，一种检索增强的多模态DPO变体，通过视觉上下文切换触发文本偏好反转实现细粒度跨模态对齐；(3) 对预测正确但高度不确定的实体进行反事实修改，构造针对性偏好数据以缓解潜在风险，进一步提升模型可靠性。

Result: 在两个公共胸部X射线数据集（MIMIC-CXR和IU X-Ray）以及一个内部内窥镜数据集上的大量实验表明，DPO-Clin在临床相关指标上显著优于SFT基线，并超越现有基于DPO的MRG方法，在不同基线架构和多种医学影像模态上展现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DPO-Clin通过将偏好优化聚焦于临床发现和跨模态对齐，有效解决了现有DPO-based MRG方法中偏好构造粗糙和缺乏视觉-语言对齐的问题，显著提升了医学报告生成的临床可靠性与跨模态泛化能力。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Medical Report Generation (MRG), the reliability remains constrained by the prevalence of factual errors. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm to enhance the performance of Supervised Fine-Tuned (SFT) MRG models, existing DPO-based MRG methods typically adopt a naive preference construction that directly pairs model-generated reports with ground truth reports. This strategy inadvertently entangles critical clinical findings with clinically irrelevant linguistic characteristics, and fundamentally lacks explicit vision-language alignment. To address these challenges, we propose DPO-Clin, a novel post-training framework that focuses preference optimization on clinical findings and cross-modal alignment. First, we introduce the Entity-level Clinical Diagnostic (ECD) module to perform a precise entity-level factual diagnosis. ECD guides the generation of linguistically-aligned report preference pairs, isolating clinical discrepancies from linguistic variations. Second, to achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment, we develop M$^2$DPO, a retrieval-augmented multi-modal DPO variant that enforces textual preference inversion triggered by visual context switches. Third, we locate correct yet highly uncertain predicted entities and apply counterfactual modifications to construct targeted preference data for latent risk mitigation, thereby further enhancing the model reliability. Extensive experiments on two public chest X-ray datasets (MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray) and an in-house endoscopy dataset demonstrate that DPO-Clin significantly improves the SFT baselines on clinical-aware metrics. Furthermore, it achieves superior performance over existing DPO-based MRG methods, exhibiting robust generalizability across distinct baseline architectures and diverse medical imaging modalities.

</details>


### [105] [Towards Adaptive Super-Resolution and Quality Assessment via Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08508)
*Ajeet Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于测试时自适应（TTA）的统一范式，用于真实世界条件下的自适应视频超分辨率（VSR）和感知质量建模，在不需重新训练或高质量监督的情况下提升鲁棒性和感知质量。论文包含三个核心贡献：基于TTA的无参考视频质量评估（VQA）框架、面向屏幕内容超分辨率的Transformer架构，以及区域感知的TTA策略，实验证明在多种基准上均有一致的感知质量和可读性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频超分辨率（VSR）方法在面对异构设备、编解码器和网络环境带来的未知退化（degradations）时难以泛化，亟需一种不依赖重新训练或高质量监督就能提升鲁棒性和感知质量的解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出基于TTA的无参考视频质量评估（VQA）框架，利用自适应的质量预测为VSR提供感知引导；2) 开发面向屏幕内容超分辨率的Transformer架构，保持文本清晰度和结构保真度；3) 提出区域感知的TTA策略，在无需高分辨率真值的情况下，有针对性地对文本区域和非文本区域进行选择性精炼。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的实验表明，该方法在感知质量和可读性方面均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 本文为真实世界条件下的视频超分辨率提供了不依赖监督的TTA解决方案，并展望了向完全自适应的视频增强系统发展的方向，以实现在未知领域的泛化。

Abstract: This paper presents doctoral research on adaptive video super-resolution and perceptual quality modeling under real-world conditions. Existing video super-resolution (VSR) methods struggle to generalize under unknown degradations arising from heterogeneous devices, codecs, and network environments. We address this challenge through test-time adaptation (TTA), a unified paradigm that improves robustness and perceptual quality without retraining or high-quality supervision. Specifically, we: 1) propose a TTA-based framework for no-reference video quality assessment (VQA), where adapted quality predictions provide perceptual guidance for VSR under unseen distortions; 2) develop a transformer-based architecture for screen-content super-resolution that preserves text clarity and structural fidelity; and 3) introduce a region-aware TTA strategy that selectively refines text and non-text regions without requiring high-resolution ground truth. Experimental results across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in perceptual quality and readability. We also outline ongoing work toward fully adaptive video enhancement systems capable of generalizing across unseen domains.

</details>


### [106] [eBIRD: Event-based Intensity Image Reconstruction Using Controllable Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08519)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Fabian Valderrama,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出eBIRD框架，将去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）与ControlNet条件机制相结合，用于从事件流重建强度图像。在N-MNIST（手写数字）和RGBE-Gaze（人脸）数据集上的33ms事件窗口实验中，通用模型在N-MNIST上表现最佳（MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB），而专用模型在RGBE-Gaze上表现最佳（MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB），结果表明可控扩散模型是事件引导强度图像重建的有效方法，但最佳学习策略取决于重建域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件流数据具有二进制、稀疏和异步的特性，使得从事件流重建强度图像成为一项挑战性问题。现有方法难以同时有效地利用事件数据的时间信息和空间结构，因此需要探索新的重建框架。

Method: 提出eBIRD框架，结合DDPM（去噪扩散概率模型）与ControlNet条件机制，实现事件引导的强度图像重建。对比分析通用扩散学习策略和专用扩散学习策略，在两个不同域（手写数字和面部图像）上进行评估。

Result: 在N-MNIST数据集上，通用模型取得最佳重建质量（MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB）；在RGBE-Gaze数据集上，专用模型表现最佳（MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB）。

Conclusion: 可控扩散模型是事件引导强度图像重建的有前景方法，但最佳学习策略（通用vs专用）取决于具体的重建域，需要根据任务特性选择合适的学习策略。

Abstract: Intensity-image reconstruction from event streams remains a challenging problem due to the binary, sparse, and asynchronous nature of event data. This work proposes eBIRD, an event-guided reconstruction framework that combines a DDPM with ControlNet-based conditioning. We analyze generic and specialized diffusion learning strategies for handwritten digit (N-MNIST) and face (RGBE-Gaze) reconstruction using 33ms event windows. On N-MNIST, the general model achieves the best reconstruction quality (MSE 0.0052, SSIM 0.8982, PSNR 23.34dB), whereas the specialized model performs best on RGBE-Gaze (MSE 0.0161, SSIM 0.7605, PSNR 19.08dB). These preliminary results suggest that controllable diffusion models are a promising approach for event-guided intensity-image reconstruction, while highlighting that the preferred learning strategy depends on the reconstruction domain.

</details>


### [107] [A Combined Feature-Based Framework for Disguise and Spoofing Detection in Face Recognition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08521)
*Sangiya Pararajasingham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出并比较了五种结合特征提取与分类的流水线（PM、LPM、HPM、SM、HM），在单一框架内同时应对人脸识别中的两个常见故障模式：欺骗（冒名者用照片或视频冒充授权用户）和伪装（合法用户因外观变化而被拒绝）。在115个受试者上训练，评估于六个测试条件，结果发现基于HOG的HPM流水线在整体一致性上最优（混合伪装准确率94.59%，欺骗检测91.67%），而基于LBP的LPM流水线在欺骗检测上最高（93.2%）但对姿态变化鲁棒性较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别系统普遍面临两个通常被分开处理的失败模式——欺骗（spoofing）和伪装（disguise）。现有方法往往只针对其中一种问题进行优化，缺乏在单一框架内同时解决这两种问题的系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索经典特征表示能否在统一框架内兼顾两类挑战。

Method: 作者提出并比较了五种融合特征提取与分类的流水线：PM（PCA+最小欧氏距离）、LPM（LBP+PCA+MED）、HPM（HOG+PCA+MED）、SM（SURF+MED）和HM（Harris角点+MED）。每条流水线遵循统一的二阶段流程：预处理、特征提取、特征过滤和分类。训练数据来自FEI、Disguised Faces Database和NUAA三个数据库共115个受试者，评估覆盖混合外观、正面人脸、暗光照、左右侧转姿态和照片欺骗等六个测试条件。

Result: HPM（基于HOG）在各类条件下表现最一致：混合伪装准确率94.59%，姿态和光照变化下81.5-93.2%，欺骗检测91.67%。LPM（基于LBP）的欺骗检测精度最高（93.2%），但对姿态变化的鲁棒性较弱。结果表明经典特征表示在欺骗敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间存在可量化的权衡。

Conclusion: 经典特征表示在统一框架内同时处理欺骗与伪装时，存在欺骗敏感性与伪装鲁棒性之间的固有权衡。这一发现为后续的深度学习方法和跨数据库扩展研究提供了动机和基线，表明需要更先进的表示学习方法来突破经典特征方法的性能上限。

Abstract: Face recognition systems face two distinct, commonly-separated failure modes: spoofing, where an impostor presents a photograph or video of an authorized user, and disguise, where a legitimate user is rejected because their appearance differs from their enrolled template due to accessories, facial hair, illumination, or pose. This paper proposes and compares five combined feature-extraction and classification pipelines that address both problems within a single framework: PM (PCA and Minimum Euclidean Distance, MED), LPM (Local Binary Patterns with PCA and MED), HPM (Histogram of Oriented Gradients with PCA and MED), SM (Speeded-Up Robust Features with MED), and HM (Harris corner features with MED). Each pipeline follows a common two-phase process comprising pre-processing, feature extraction, feature filtering, and classification. The methods were trained on 115 subjects drawn from the FEI, Disguised Faces Database, and NUAA databases and evaluated on six test conditions covering mixed appearances, frontal faces, dark illumination, left- and right-turned poses, and photo-spoofing attempts. The HOG-based pipeline (HPM) achieved the most consistent performance across conditions, with 94.59% accuracy on mixed-appearance disguise, 81.5-93.2% across pose and illumination variants, and 91.67% on spoofing, while the LBP-based pipeline (LPM) achieved the highest spoofing-detection accuracy (93.2%) but weaker robustness to pose change. These results reveal a measurable trade-off between spoof sensitivity and disguise robustness among classical feature representations, motivating the deep-learning and cross-database extensions discussed in the concluding sections.

</details>


### [108] [ERF-GS: Reconstructing Fast Motion from Disjoint Event-RGB Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08531)
*Xiaoyang Bai,Zhenyang Li,Weiwei Xu,Edmund Y. Lam,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ERF-GS框架，将事件信息融合进3D高斯溅射的优化和稠密化阶段，用于高速运动物体的动态三维场景重建。该方法在真实仿真设置下开发，实现事件学习与RGB输入解耦，在模糊RGB帧和多视角RGB-事件不重叠的真实自然视频场景中优于4DGS基线和同时期的E-D3DGS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的动态三维场景重建（如NeRF和3DGS）在快速运动物体场景下仍然表现不佳，因为传统基于帧式视频的方法在体育赛事、动物拍摄等高动态场景中面临帧率不足、运动模糊等问题，难以准确重建高速运动物体。

Method: ERF-GS将事件相机信息整合到高斯溅射管线的两个关键阶段：优化阶段（optimization）和稠密化阶段（densification）。采用接近真实的仿真设置进行开发，使事件信息的利用可以与RGB输入解耦，从而增强模型的泛化能力以适应复杂布局、低帧率和严重运动模糊的真实自然视频。

Result: 在Neu3D和Nvidia数据集的多个变体（包含模糊RGB帧及RGB-事件视角不重叠的情况）上，ERF-GS在重建精度上优于4DGS基线和同期方法E-D3DGS。

Conclusion: ERF-GS验证了事件相机数据与高斯溅射结合在高速运动动态场景重建中的有效性，通过真实仿真设置和事件-RGB解耦设计，突破了传统方法对合成数据的依赖，可扩展到自然视频中的复杂场景。

Abstract: Deep learning-driven representations such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) have revolutionized the field of dynamic 3D scene reconstruction with improved visual precision and scalability. However, the reconstruction of fast-moving objects remains a challenge; existing methods based on conventional frame-based videos often struggle in scenarios such as sports events and animal videography. We propose an event-RGB fusion Gaussian splatting (ERF-GS) framework that integrates event information into both optimization and densification stages of the Gaussian splatting pipeline, taking advantage of novel event sensors with high frame-rate. Unlike many other event-assisted scene reconstruction methods, ERF-GS was developed using realistic simulation settings and realizes event-based learning detached from RGB inputs. This design enables its application beyond straightforward synthetic data into the realm of natural video with complex layout, low frame rates and severe motion blur. Our experiments show that ERF-GS outperforms both the 4DGS baseline and the concurrent E-D3DGS on different variants of the Neu3D and Nvidia datasets which include blurry RGB frames and disjoint RGB-event viewpoints. Our code is available at https://github.com/andrewbxy/ERF-GS.

</details>


### [109] [Rethinking Attention Locality in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08541)
*Zeqi Zheng,Zizheng Zhu,Yuping Yan,Wenxuan Pan,Zhaofei Yu,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCLA-BCP方法，通过空间连续局部注意力与边界连续性通路来增强Spiking Transformer的空间局部性，并开发了分层局部性部署策略，在静态和神经形态数据集上实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spiking Transformer中的Softmax-free Spiking Self-Attention (SSA)难以建立空间上局部化的token交互。虽然已有的局部性增强SSA方法提升了精度，但作者通过Mean Attention Distance (MAD)分析发现：计算上的局部性并不一定转化为空间上的局部性，而且统一应用相同的局部性增强忽略了不同架构的部署需求差异。

Method: 作者提出两个核心组件：(1) SCLA——在空间相邻token的非重叠区域内计算注意力；(2) BCP——通过轻量级卷积通路促进跨边界信息交换。此外，还开发了一种分层局部性部署策略，将SCLA-BCP有效地应用于两类主要的Spiking Transformer架构中。

Result: 在七个静态和神经形态数据集上（涵盖分类、检测和分割任务）的一致改进，且参数和能量开销有限。在COCO 2017上mAP@50最高提升9.50%，在ADE20K上mIoU最高提升3.42%。

Conclusion: 通过可视化、MAD分析和消融实验验证了所提出方法的有效性，表明SCLA-BCP能够有效解决Spiking Transformer中计算局部性与空间局部性不一致的问题，并适应不同架构的部署需求。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers provide a promising paradigm for efficient visual processing with spike-driven computation, yet their Softmax-free Spiking Self-Attention (SSA) struggles to establish spatially localized token interactions. Although existing locality-enhanced SSA methods improve accuracy, it remains unclear whether they consistently induce spatial locality across layers and different Spiking Transformer architectures. Through Mean Attention Distance (MAD) analysis, we reveal that computational locality does not necessarily translate into spatial locality and show that uniformly applying the same locality enhancement overlooks architecture-dependent deployment requirements. Motivated by these observations, we propose Spatially Contiguous Local Attention with Boundary Continuity Pathway (SCLA-BCP). SCLA computes attention within non-overlapping regions of spatially adjacent tokens, while BCP facilitates cross-boundary information exchange through a lightweight convolutional pathway. Furthermore, we develop a hierarchical locality deployment strategy to effectively apply SCLA-BCP across the two major Spiking Transformer architectures. Extensive experiments on seven static and neuromorphic datasets covering classification, detection, and segmentation demonstrate consistent improvements with limited parameter and energy overhead. Notably, our approach improves mAP@50 by up to 9.50% on COCO 2017 and mIoU by up to 3.42% on ADE20K. Visualizations, MAD analysis, and ablation studies further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [110] [MotionCraft: Latent World Modeling with Sparse Attention for Visual Upscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08553)
*Rong Fu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Xiaowen Ma,Yongtai Liu,Wangyu Wu,Shuo Yin,Zijian Zhang,Sicheng Li,Yingrui Ji,Chenhao Wang,Simon Fong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionCraft是一个受世界模型启发的可控视频超分辨率框架，将超分辨率重构建模为运动感知的潜在状态预测，结合自适应稀疏注意力与显式用户控制接口，在流式约束下实现时间一致的高质量重建，并能在时间平滑度与重建保真度之间进行可预测的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频超分辨率方法在局部细节保真度、长程时空建模、感知真实感和效率之间存在权衡：卷积对齐方法在大运动或复杂退化下表现不佳；Transformer方法虽能捕获长程依赖但计算开销大；潜在空间或扩散生成方法虽能合成丰富纹理但需要专门的时间约束来保持一致性。因此需要一个能同时兼顾这些方面且可控的框架。

Method: MotionCraft将超分辨率重构形式化为受世界模型启发的运动感知潜在状态预测，包含三个核心组件：鲁棒运动融合模块、Latent World Transformer（在局部性与定向非局部交互之间取得平衡）、以及紧凑的条件解码器。此外集成了自适应稀疏注意力和显式用户可访问的控制接口。

Result: 经验评估表明MotionCraft在重建质量和感知性能方面均表现优异，同时能在时间平滑度与重建保真度之间实现可预测的权衡。

Conclusion: MotionCraft在流式约束下实现了时间一致的高质量视频重建，并且通过其控制接口允许用户在时间平滑度和重建保真度之间灵活调节，兼顾了性能与可控性。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) aims to recover high-fidelity high-resolution videos from low-resolution inputs and is central to applications ranging from mobile capture to streaming and archival restoration. Existing approaches trade off among local-detail fidelity, long-range spatio-temporal modeling, perceptual realism, and efficiency: convolutional alignment techniques preserve local structure but suffer when motion is large or degradations are complex; transformer-based methods capture long-range dependencies yet require architectural or algorithmic adaptations to remain computationally feasible; and recent latent or diffusion-based generators synthesize rich texture but require specialized temporal constraints to maintain coherence. We present MotionCraft, a controllable VSR framework that formulates restoration as motion-aware latent state prediction inspired by world models and integrates adaptive sparse attention with an explicit user-accessible control interface. MotionCraft combines robust motion fusion, a Latent World Transformer that balances locality and targeted non-local interactions, and a compact conditional decoder to deliver temporally consistent, high-quality reconstructions under streaming constraints. Empirical evaluations show that MotionCraft achieves strong reconstruction and perceptual performance while enabling predictable trade-offs between temporal smoothness and reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [111] [SC-Diff: Semantically Calibrated Diffusion for Visible-to-Infrared Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08555)
*Junyin Zhang,Siyu Huang,Jianxiong Ye,Haowei Gong,Ruicheng Zhang,Deyu Meng,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SC-Diff，一个语义校准的潜在扩散框架，用于可见光到红外的图像翻译。不同于仅将语义先验作为外部条件的方法，SC-Diff将语义先验同时用于条件引导和内部自注意力校准，通过语义引导的自注意力校准机制减少跨类别干扰，从而提升生成红外图像的语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见光到红外图像翻译可用于利用丰富的可见光图像扩充红外训练数据。扩散模型虽生成能力强，但现有方法仅将语义先验作为外部条件，未显式调控去噪网络内的token交互，难以保持目标的位置、形状和语义布局，限制了标注信息的可靠复用。

Method: 1) 使用带预定义文本提示的SAM3模型从可见光图像提取类别语义掩码，合并为语义图并与可见光图像融合作为输入条件；2) 将语义图转换为token级别语义标签，用于校准去噪网络中的自注意力；3) 提出语义引导自注意力校准（SGSC），对同类别query-key对自适应施加正向偏置，偏置强度由注意力在语义类别间的分散度及query自身类别上分配的注意力决定，原始注意力分数进一步调制偏置大小。

Result: 大量实验表明SC-Diff在感知质量上优于现有方法，且生成的合成红外训练数据对下游红外目标检测任务更有效。

Conclusion: 软校准方法在减少跨类别干扰的同时保留全局上下文交互，能显著提升生成红外图像的语义一致性，并验证了其在数据增强场景下的实用价值。

Abstract: Visible-to-infrared image translation provides a practical way to expand infrared training data using abundant visible images. Diffusion models are promising for this task because of their strong generative performance. However, existing diffusion-based methods typically use semantic priors only as external conditions, without explicitly regulating token interactions within the denoising network. Consequently, they struggle to preserve object locations, shapes, and semantic layouts required for reliable annotation reuse. We propose SC-Diff, a semantically calibrated latent diffusion framework that uses semantic priors for both conditional guidance and internal self-attention calibration. A pretrained SAM3 model with predefined text prompts first extracts category-specific semantic masks from visible images. These masks are merged into a semantic map and fused with the visible image as the input condition. The same map is converted into token-level semantic labels to calibrate self-attention in the denoising network. Based on these labels, we introduce Semantic-Guided Self-Attention Calibration (SGSC), which adaptively applies positive biases to query-key pairs of the same category. The query-wise calibration strength depends on the dispersion of attention across semantic categories and the attention assigned to the query's own category. The original attention scores further modulate the bias, giving greater calibration to same-category keys with stronger responses. This soft calibration reduces cross-category interference while retaining global contextual interactions, thereby improving semantic consistency in generated infrared images. Extensive experiments show that SC-Diff improves perceptual quality and produces more effective synthetic training data for downstream infrared object detection.

</details>


### [112] [On-Device Multi-Species Malaria Detection with Uncertainty-Calibrated Slide-Level Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08566)
*Idaya Seidu,Ahmed Tahiru Issah,Charles B. Delahunt,Carine Mukamakuza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向资源匮乏地区疟疾诊断的端到端移动检测系统，解决了机器学习疟疾文献中通常被忽略的六类临床约束（停止标准、人机协同、多物种判别、厚血膜检测、不确定性计算和边缘设备部署），系统基于YOLOv13n在TensorFlow Lite上全离线运行，可检测四种疟原虫物种及白细胞，并以WHO标准量化虫血症。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾在资源匮乏地区仍是主要致死原因，但专业镜检人员稀缺。虽然基于显微镜图像的自动诊断潜力巨大，但算法要实际部署必须满足一系列从ML视角看并不显然的临床约束，而这些约束在现有疟疾ML文献中往往被忽视。因此需要开发一个真正符合临床要求的诊断系统。

Method: 与国家级卫生中心紧密合作，构建了满足六项关键临床要求的疟疾诊断流水线：(i) 停止准则以减少图像采集和时间成本；(ii) 人机协同审查功能；(iii) 多物种判别（因治疗因物种而异）；(iv) 厚血膜检测（镜检标准）；(v) 高效不确定性计算辅助临床审查；(vi) 边缘设备平台（应对网络不稳定）。系统采用YOLOv13n模型经TensorFlow Lite部署，在Gimsa染色厚血涂片图像上检测四种疟原虫物种和白细胞，将逐图像检测聚合为玻片级虫血症，并按WHO标准量化。

Result: 在涵盖四个物种的2,739张标注图像上评估，系统达到mAP@0.5为0.863，逐图像寄生虫计数相关性r=0.812，玻片级（软计数，每玻片10张图像）r=0.951，完全离线运行，流水线处理时间为每张图像10.27±1.65秒。

Conclusion: 该系统验证了在真实临床约束下构建可部署疟疾诊断方案是可行的，为资源匮乏地区的自动化疟疾检测提供了兼顾准确性、临床合规性和部署可行性的完整解决方案，同时也为后续研究指明了临床需求导向的ML系统设计方向。

Abstract: Malaria remains a leading cause of mortality in resource-limited settings, where expert microscopists are scarce. Automated diagnosis based on microscopy images thus has strong potential to improve care delivery. But for an algorithm to deploy, a necessary requirement is that it meet a suite of non-obvious (from a machine learning (ML) perspective) clinical constraints. Therefore, in close consultation with a national health center we developed a malaria diagnosis pipeline which addresses key requirements listed by the health care center but typically ignored in the ML malaria literature. In particular, it includes: (i) stopping criteria (to reduce image acquisition and time-to-result); (ii) human-in-the-loop functionality (for review and accountability); (iii) multi-species discrimination (since treatment varies by species); (iv) thick film detection (standard for microscopy); (v) computationally-efficient uncertainty calculations (to aid clinician review); and (vi) an edge device platform (since internet can be spotty in this catchment area). The mobile system performs all inference on-device using YOLOv13n deployed via TensorFlow Lite. It detects four species and white blood cells from Giemsa-stained thick blood smear images, aggregating per-image detections into slide-level parasitemia with World Health Organization (WHO)-standard quantification. This paper highlights these various clinical constraints and offers methods to address them. Evaluated on 2,739 annotated images across all four species, the system achieves mAP@0.5 of 0.863, per-image parasite count correlation of r = 0.812, slide-level r = 0.951 (soft counting, 10 images/slide), and runs entirely offline with a pipeline time of 10.27 +- 1.65 s per image.

</details>


### [113] [CDGC-Net: 3D Medical Image Segmentation with Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention and Grouped Channel Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08575)
*Zheyang Jing,Qin Lu,Jianwang Li,Yujie Yang,Chen Yi,Shaofeng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDGC-Net，一种3D医学图像分割网络，通过协作双尺度空间注意力（CDSA）和分组层次通道注意力（GHCA）在相同特征层面同时捕获全局解剖上下文与局部边界细节，并在Synapse、ACDC、BraTS和LA四个数据集上取得优于现有方法的分割性能，同时大幅降低参数量和计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学图像分割方法通常将全局和局部特征建模在分离的模块或特征层级中，并独立进行通道重标定，导致全局上下文与局部边界之间存在语义不匹配、通道关系建模不足、空间-通道交互弱以及特征表达冗余等问题。

Method: CDGC-Net提出CDGC块，其核心包含两个组件：（1）协作双尺度自注意力（CDSA），将注意力头分配到并行的局部窗口分支和全局稀疏分支，在同一特征层级捕获精细空间细节和长程解剖上下文，输出拼接为N×C空间表示；（2）分组层次通道注意力（GHCA），将通道组织为r个组，同时建模组内和跨组依赖关系。CDSA和GHCA共享一个键（key）投影以保持一致的特征参考，并通过残差特征对齐将精炼特征与原始表示融合。

Result: 在Synapse、ACDC、BraTS和LA数据集上，CDGC-Net的平均DSC分别达到86.96%、92.91%、82.56%和93.52%，比次优结果分别高出0.39、0.47、0.17和0.32个百分点。模型参数量25.83M，FLOPs 28.62G（输入64×128×128），相比UNETR++减少39.87%和40.30%。

Conclusion: CDGC-Net通过在相同特征层级融合双尺度空间注意力与分组层次通道建模，有效消除了全局-局部语义错配，在分割精度和计算复杂度之间取得了良好的平衡，验证了其作为3D医学图像分割方案的优越性。

Abstract: Accurate 3D medical image segmentation requires the integration of long-range anatomical context with fine boundary detail. Existing methods often model global and local features in separate modules or feature levels and perform channel recalibration independently. This may cause semantic mismatch between global context and local boundaries, insufficient channel relationship modeling, weak spatial-channel interaction, and redundant representations. We propose CDGC-Net, a 3D medical image segmentation network that combines cooperative dual-scale spatial attention with grouped hierarchical channel modeling. With-in each CDGC block, Cooperative Dual-Scale Self-Attention (CDSA) assigns attention heads to parallel local-window and global-sparse branches. The two branches capture fine spatial details and long-range anatomical context at the same feature level. Their outputs are concatenated into an $N\times C$ spatial representation and directly passed to Grouped Hierarchical Channel Attention (GHCA). GHCA organizes the channels into $r$ groups and models both within-group and cross-group dependencies. CDSA and GHCA reuse a shared key projection to maintain a consistent feature reference. Residual feature alignment subsequently integrates the refined features with the original representation. On the Synapse, ACDC, BraTS, and LA datasets, CDGC-Net achieved mean DSC values of 86.96\%, 92.91\%, 82.56\%, and 93.52\%, respectively, exceeding the next-highest reported values by 0.39, 0.47, 0.17, and 0.32 percentage points. CDGC-Net contains 25.83M parameters and 28.62G FLOPs for an input size of $64\times128\times128$, reducing these quantities by 39.87\% and 40.30\%, respectively, relative to UNETR++. These results indicate a favorable trade-off between segmentation accuracy and computational complexity.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Unified Hallucination Fuzzing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07525)
*Pengfei Zhou,Jiajun Song,Zhiwei Tang,Yixing Ma,Xiaopeng Peng,Donghui Si,Yuhang Xu,Huiqi Song,Yiyuan Miao,Yichen Qian,Weihua Chen,Wangbo Zhao,Bohan Zhuang,Jiasheng Tang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合细粒度基准UniHall与自适应模糊测试SAMF的系统评估框架，用于动态揭示多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的幻觉问题。实验发现最先进模型在模糊测试下性能显著下降，暴露推理能力与事实锚定之间的脱节，并识别出遵循RL对齐导致的乐于助人-幻觉权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评估主要基于静态基准，存在两方面问题：一是分类覆盖狭窄，无法系统覆盖幻觉类型；二是快速性能饱和，模型在主流基准上已趋于上限，无法真实反映其在动态真实场景中的鲁棒性。因此需要构建更全面且能自我演进的评估框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出了两阶段评估框架：(1) 构建UniHall细粒度数据集，基于统一的分类体系，涵盖对象（Object）、指令（Instruction）和知识（Knowledge）三个维度；(2) 提出Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing（SAMF）自适应模糊测试框架，采用进化式变异策略探索模型幻觉边界，并通过基于多模态Oracle集成的结构化评估指标套件确保动态输入评估的可靠性。

Result: 大量实验表明，最先进的MLLMs在模糊测试环境下相比常规设置出现显著性能退化，揭示了推理能力与事实锚定之间的分离。此外，研究发现强化学习对齐会无意间加剧指令遵循任务中的献媚行为（sycophancy），呈现出帮助性-幻觉之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 静态基准无法充分评估MLLMs的幻觉鲁棒性，自适应模糊测试框架能更有效地揭示模型局限。研究揭示了推理能力与事实性的解耦，以及对齐训练可能以增加幻觉为代价提升帮助性的问题，为未来MLLM评估与对齐优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), severely limiting their reliability in high-stakes applications. Existing evaluations, predominantly based on static benchmarks, suffer from narrow taxonomical coverage and rapid performance saturation, failing to reflect model robustness in evolving real-world scenarios. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation framework integrating a comprehensive benchmark with self-evolving stress testing. First, we introduce UniHall, a fine-grained dataset grounded in a unified taxonomy spanning Object, Instruction, and Knowledge dimensions. Second, to address benchmark saturation, we propose Self-Adaptive Multimodal Fuzzing (SAMF), a self-adaptive framework that employs evolutionary mutation strategies to explore the boundaries of model hallucinations. Crucially, to ensure reliable assessment of dynamic inputs, SAMF incorporates a structured metric suite driven by an ensemble of multi-modal oracles. Our extensive experiments reveal that state-of-the-art MLLMs exhibit significant performance degradation under fuzzing compared to conventional settings, exposing a dissociation between reasoning capabilities and factual grounding. Furthermore, we identify a helpfulness-hallucination trade-off, where reinforcement learning alignment inadvertently exacerbates sycophancy in instruction-following tasks. The framework, code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/LanceZPF/EvalHall.

</details>


### [115] [DocAtlas: Long-Document Understanding as Mutable-State Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07527)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocAtlas是一个将长文档理解视为可变状态信息检索过程的系统，通过外部可变的文档工具环境（搜索、阅读、笔记、回顾）结合自改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆，在固定上下文预算下工作。在MMLongBench-Doc上，使用GPT-5.4达到71.4%（超过人类专家65.8%），使用Qwen3.5-4B VLM经端到端强化学习训练达到63.7%（超过直接输入基线54.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强系统通常在生成前从静态索引中选择证据，而智能体系统虽然支持多轮工具调用，但往往依赖冻结的专有模型后端，其行为由提示词决定，缺乏对文档信息检索和证据选择过程的灵活控制。长文档理解需要跨多页、多布局、表格、图形和图表查找并组合证据，需要一种将文档信息检索视为可变状态过程的方法。

Method: DocAtlas将长文档理解实现为"可变文档工具环境"（mutable document harness）：1）外部环境决定每一步搜索、读取、存储、审查并展示给模型的文档信息；2）针对给定文档和问题，暴露搜索、阅读、笔记和回顾工具；3）维护层级树和笔记存储，随智能体记录证据而动态更新；4）结合自改进检索、选择性证据访问和主动工作记忆，在固定上下文预算下运行；5）同一环境同时支持大规模VLM的推理时使用和紧凑VLM智能体的端到端强化学习训练。

Result: 在MMLongBench-Doc基准上：使用GPT-5.4，DocAtlas达到71.4%，超过人类专家参考线的65.8%；使用Qwen3.5-4B VLM在该环境中进行端到端RL训练后达到63.7%，相较于54.4%的直接输入基线提升显著，表明可变文档工具环境设计能大幅改进紧凑型文档智能体。

Conclusion: DocAtlas证明了将长文档理解建模为可变状态的信息检索过程是有效的，外部可变的文档工具环境设计不仅提升了大型VLM的推理性能（超越人类专家水平），还使紧凑型VLM智能体通过端到端强化学习获得大幅性能提升，为长文档理解系统设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires models to find and combine evidence across many pages, layouts, tables, figures, and charts. Existing retrieval-augmented systems usually select evidence from a static index before generation, while recent agentic systems add multi-turn tool use but often rely on frozen proprietary backbones whose behavior is set by prompts. We present DocAtlas, a system that treats long-document understanding as a mutable-state information-seeking process. We instantiate DocAtlas as a mutable document harness: an external environment that determines what document information is searched, read, stored, reviewed, and shown to the model at each step. Given a document and question, the harness exposes search, reading, note-taking, and review tools, maintains a hierarchical tree and note store, and updates both as the agent records evidence. DocAtlas combines self-improving retrieval, selective evidence access, and active working memory under a fixed context budget. The same harness supports inference-time use with large VLMs and end-to-end reinforcement learning for compact VLM agents. With GPT-5.4, DocAtlas reaches 71.4\% on MMLongBench-Doc, exceeding the human-expert reference of 65.8\%. A Qwen3.5-4B VLM trained with end-to-end RL in the DocAtlas environment reaches 63.7\%, compared with a 54.4\% direct-input baseline, showing that mutable document-harness design can improve compact document agents by a large margin.

</details>


### [116] [WuYuEval: A Multi-Level Benchmark for Large Language Models in Solid Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07529)
*Yi Zhang,Hongyang Wang,Zheng Hao Leong,Zihao Wu,Kaijun Lin,Zhixing Pan,Qixun Huangfu,Wei Ren,Wenyan Wu,Fangyun Wang,Wenting Yu,Hengyu Lin,Muling Yang,Zongguo Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WuYuEval是一个面向固体废物管理（SWM）领域的多层级大语言模型评估基准，包含4,590道基础模块选择题和247道专家模块开放题，覆盖33个LLM进行系统性评测，发现模型在工程约束下的专业推理能力存在显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准侧重于通用知识，难以评估模型在工程、环境和政策约束下的专业决策能力，而SWM领域亟需一个能检验模型专业素养的评测体系。

Method: 构建包含基础模块（6种任务类型、8个领域类别的4,590道选择题）和专家模块（247道情境化开放题，涉及多目标优化、约束权衡和系统设计）的双层基准；采用锚定校准的LLM-as-a-Judge评分结合Elo成对比较；对33个LLM进行多维度评测。

Result: 领先模型在基础模块达94.64%准确率，但平均值从简单题的84.14%骤降至难题的42.50%；计算、实验设计、城市规划和开放型专家任务表现较差；推理导向的Thinking模式在多数模型对上有所提升，但增益取决于基线能力且并非总是正向。

Conclusion: 可见的推理过程中只有锚定在单位、假设和工程约束时才有助于决策，否则可能偏离关键答案边界；WuYuEval为SWM领域基础模型的开发提供了评估资源和专业推理链、显式约束控制的实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as technical assistants, but their competence in solid waste management (SWM) remains difficult to assess because existing benchmarks emphasize general knowledge rather than professional decisions under engineering, environmental, and policy constraints. We introduce WuYuEval, a multi-level benchmark for evaluating LLMs in SWM across foundational knowledge, domain reasoning, and expert decision-making. After quality auditing, WuYuEval contains a Foundation Module with 4,590 closed-ended multiple-choice questions across six task types and eight domain categories, together with an Expert Module with 247 scenario-based open-ended questions involving multi-objective optimization, constraint trade-offs, and system design. For expert tasks, we combine anchor-calibrated LLM-as-a-Judge scoring with Elo-based pairwise comparison. Across 33 LLMs, performance varied widely. The leading model reached 94.64\% accuracy on the Foundation Module, but average accuracy still fell from 84.14\% on easy questions to 42.50\% on hard questions, with lower performance concentrated in calculation, experimental design, urban planning, and open-ended expert tasks. Reasoning-oriented Thinking modes improve most matched model pairs after auditing, but the gains depend on baseline capability and are not uniformly positive. These results suggest that visible deliberation helps only when it remains anchored to units, assumptions, and engineering constraints; otherwise, it may drift from decisive answer boundaries. WuYuEval therefore provides both an evaluation resource and an empirical basis for developing SWM-oriented foundation models with professional reasoning chains and explicit constraint control.

</details>


### [117] [Search-G1: Grounded Search Agents via Representation-Based Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07531)
*Cheng Ruoxi,Ma Haoxuan,Zhang Hongyi,Zhang Junming,Duan Ranjie,Xia Qiaolin,Wang Hao,Lu Yu,Shi Haibo,Ma Xingjun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Search-G1是一种基于表征的内在奖励框架，通过两个干预校准的读出来衡量智能体答案的操作性grounding：提示状态读出预测闭卷充足性（其补集定义检索必要性），答案承诺读出通过证据删除下的答案敏感性估计证据依赖度。该框架无需过程注释或LLM评判推理即可提供奖励信号，并在RL训练过程中周期性重拟合读出来与策略共同演化，在多个检索问答基准上改善了grounding-搜索成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索增强语言智能体的外部奖励要么是稀疏的结果监督，要么依赖昂贵的过程注释或LLM评判；而基于策略侧信号（熵、似然、信息增益）的内在奖励虽然廉价但主要反映模型置信度而非证据grounding。需要一个既廉价又可区分"有根据的检索"与"冗余搜索"的奖励信号。

Method: Search-G1提出两个干预校准的读出器：(1) prompt-state读出器预测闭卷知识是否充足，其补集定义为策略相对的检索必要性；(2) answer-commit读出器通过证据删除时答案的敏感性估计证据依赖度。奖励结合这两个读数：当检索被判定为必要时为正确的检索轨迹提供额外奖励，当闭卷知识足够时奖励正确直接回答，并对重复搜索进行惩罚。校准后奖励打分无需过程注释或LLM评判；由于RL会改变策略表征，Search-G1在最新checkpoint轨迹上周期性重拟合两个读出器，使奖励与策略共同进化。

Result: 在多个基于搜索的问答基准和两个模型规模上，Search-G1改善了grounding-搜索成本权衡，在保持竞争性任务准确率的同时产生了更短的响应侧轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过表征级内在奖励实现检索必要性与证据依赖度的可扩展且廉价的度量，Search-G1在不需要昂贵监督信号的前提下兼顾了检索grounding与搜索效率，验证了与策略共同演化的奖励设计的有效性。

Abstract: Search-augmented language agents should retrieve external information only when necessary and ground their answers in retrieved evidence. Existing external rewards provide either sparse outcome supervision or richer feedback from process annotations and LLM judges. Outcome rewards scale readily but cannot distinguish grounded retrieval from redundant search, whereas richer signals require costly annotation or inference during training. Internal rewards based on policy-side signals such as entropy, likelihood, or information gain are graded and inexpensive to evaluate, yet mainly reflect model confidence rather than evidence grounding. We propose Search-G1, a representation-based intrinsic reward framework that measures the operational grounding of an agent's answers through two intervention-calibrated readouts. A prompt-state readout predicts closed-book sufficiency, whose complement defines policy-relative retrieval necessity; an answer-commit readout estimates evidence reliance from answer-stage sensitivity to evidence deletion. Together, they provide additional credit to correct searched trajectories when retrieval is estimated necessary and the answer is evidence-sensitive, favor correct direct answers when closed-book knowledge suffices, and penalize repeated search. After calibration, reward scoring requires neither process annotations nor LLM-as-judge inference during policy optimization. Because reinforcement learning changes policy representations, Search-G1 periodically refits both readouts on trajectories from the latest checkpoint, allowing the reward to co-evolve with the policy. Experiments across multiple search-based question-answering benchmarks and two model scales show that Search-G1 improves the grounding--search-cost trade-off, producing shorter response-side trajectories at competitive task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/Rosy0912/Search-G1.

</details>


### [118] [Scaling Inherently Interpretable Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07594)
*Guide Labs Team,Andreas Madsen,Aya Abdelsalam Ismail,Giang Nguyen,Isaac Plant,Muawiz Chaudhary,Nathaniel Monson,Saqib Azim,Zhichen Guo,Julius Adebayo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战"可解释性是能力的代价"这一传统观点，提出将可解释性作为训练管线的约束条件与语言建模目标联合优化。在三个数量级的计算规模下，可解释性与能力同步提升，而非相互对抗。作者以Steerling-8B实例化该方案：这是一个带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，能够将输出归因于输入token、人类可理解的概念和训练数据，支持闭环干预（诊断-检索-纠正），且仅用1/2到1/16的计算量即可与开放同侪模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法往往在模型训练完成后进行逆向工程，其可靠性难以验证，且被视为能力的"税负"。本文旨在扭转这一范式，论证可解释性不应是事后的补偿，而是可以内建于训练过程中的约束条件，并与能力共同扩展。

Method: 将可解释性作为训练目标的一部分（与语言建模目标联合优化），而非事后分析手段；在自回归和扩散两种语言模型、跨越三个数量级计算规模下验证；具体实例化为Steerling-8B——一种带因果注意力掩码的扩散语言模型，支持token级概念归因和训练数据归因，从而允许无需重训的概念引导干预。

Result: 实验表明，随着模型规模扩大，表征变得更加解耦，并与人类可理解的概念对齐；可解释性随能力同步扩展。Steerling-8B在仅使用同侪模型1/2到1/16计算量的情况下仍保持竞争力，提示了一种新的扩展范式。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以作为一种设计原则融入训练过程，并随规模增长而改善；这开辟了"训练时内置可解释性"的新扩展范式，使模型在能力提升的同时保持透明和可干预，而无需牺牲性能或增加额外计算成本。

Abstract: Interpretability is often treated as a tax on capability: language models are trained as opaque systems, then explained after the fact, with methods whose reliability is difficult to establish. In this work, we challenge this premise. Rather than reverse-engineering a model, we make interpretability a constraint of the training pipeline, optimized alongside the language modeling objective. Across three orders of magnitude of compute, on both autoregressive and diffusion language models, interpretability scales with capability rather than against it. Surprisingly, model representations become more disentangled and aligned with human-understandable concepts with scale.
  We instantiate the training-time recipe with Steerling-8B, a diffusion language model with a causal attention mask. For any group of generated tokens, Steerling-8B attributes the output to relevant input tokens, human-understandable concepts, and training data. This enables closed-loop intervention: diagnose an output through its concept or feature attribution, retrieve similar training data, and correct the behavior through concept steering without retraining. Steerling-8B remains competitive with open peer models trained on substantially 2-16x more compute, suggesting a different scaling paradigm: interpretability can be designed into training, and it improves with scale.

</details>


### [119] [Embedding Initialization for Unseen Low-resource Languages in Multilingual NMT: A Case Study on Limbum-English Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07629)
*Samiratu Ntohsi,Neza David Tuyishimire,Anesu Kafesu,Marvin Ogore,Samuel Oluwajunwonlo Babalola,Oche Ankeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向未覆盖语言的嵌入初始化策略：将多个类型学相近语言的语言令牌嵌入取平均值，作为目标语言的初始令牌。在Limbum-to-English极低资源翻译任务上，该方法的chrF2++达到46.7，与最优单语言代理（Swahili，47.3）相当，且比从头训练的Transformer（14.5）和零样本NLLB-200（12.5）均高出32分以上。然而所有系统均无法保留声调变音符号，这是未来需解决的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言神经机器翻译模型（如NLLB-200）虽然覆盖200种语言，但仍无法支持包括喀麦隆Grassfields班图语在内的上千种语言。当针对未见语言进行微调时，实践者必须选择代理语言令牌，但当前缺乏系统化的选择方法。作者希望通过多语言嵌入平均的初始化策略，消除启发式代理选择的不确定性。

Method: 方法核心是嵌入初始化策略：将模型中已存在的多个类型学相关语言的嵌入取平均，作为新语言的初始语言令牌嵌入，然后对NLLB-200进行微调。实验使用8,837句对的平行语料（来自《新约》文本和双语词典），在Limbum-to-English方向上与多种基线对比：NLLB-200零样本、从头训练的Transformer、以及使用斯瓦希里语作为代理令牌的NLLB-200微调。

Result: 实验结果显示：零样本NLLB-200的chrF2++仅为12.5，从头训练的Transformer为14.5，而使用Swahili代理令牌的微调达到47.3，本文的嵌入平均初始化达到46.7。多语言平均初始化与最优单语言代理性能相当，两种NLLB-200微调变体相比从头训练基线提升了超过32个chrF2++点。但所有系统均无法保留声调变音符号。

Conclusion: 多语言迁移（而非单一代理选择）是极低资源班图语翻译中的主导因素。本文提出的多语言嵌入平均初始化方法可消除代理令牌的启发式选择负担，同时保持性能。但声调变音符号的保留仍是开放挑战，作者已公开数据集和代码以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multilingual neural machine translation models such as NLLB-200 cover 200 languages but leave thousands unsupported, including most Grassfields Bantu languages of Cameroon. When fine-tuning these models for an unseen language, practitioners must choose a proxy language token, yet no principled method exists for this selection. We implemented an embedding initialization strategy where a language token is the average of embeddings from multiple typologically related languages already in the mod el. We evaluate this approach on Limbum-to-English translation using a parallel corpus of 8,837 sentence pairs from New Testament text and a bilingual dictionary. We compare models: NLLB-200 zero-shot (chrF2++ = 12.5), a Transformer trained from scratch (chrF2++ = 14.5), NLLB-200 fine-tuned with a Swahili proxy token (chrF2++ = 47.3), and NLLB-200 with our averaged embedding initialization (chrF2++ = 46.7). We find that the multi-language initialization achieves performance comparable to the best single-language proxy. Both NLLB-200 variants improve over the from-scratch baseline by over 32 chrF2++ points. These results show that multilingual transfer is the dominant factor in extremely low-resource Bantu translation while eliminating the need for heuristic proxy selection. However, all systems fail to preserve tonal diacritics, highlighting an open challenge. We make our dataset and code available to support further research.

</details>


### [120] [Can We Optimize the Performance-Carbon Emission Break-Even Point?: The Quest for Greener LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08744)
*Sourav Das,Tanmay Joshi,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将可微分的能耗代理函数折叠进LLM微调目标中，通过联合损失机制在推理阶段实现零或近零碳排放的准确率提升（盈亏平衡配置）。作者在Gemma-2 2B、Llama-3.1 8B和Qwen-2.5 14B三个模型以及MMLU的三个子任务上验证了该方法，发现碳项根据任务结构不同表现为有害干扰或有益正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的碳足迹主要在推理阶段累积（远超过微调的一次性成本），但现有效率优化大多聚焦预训练规模或后训练压缩，缺乏针对推理阶段碳排放的微调层面干预。

Method: 提出联合损失机制，包含逐模型的碳排放参数、基于参数范数的线性代理、FLOP代理和内存代理，通过硬件能耗剖析拟合得到。对三个架构不同的大语言模型进行碳感知微调，在MMLU三个子任务上评估推理F1与CO2排放。

Result: 碳项在不同任务结构下表现出双重效应：有时作为有害干扰降低性能，有时作为有益正则化提升性能。存在非空但依赖于模型与任务的盈亏平衡区域。

Conclusion: 校准的碳感知微调可作为一种轻量级、即插即用的正则化器，但盈亏平衡区域受模型架构和任务结构双重影响，并非普遍有效。该工作为进行中研究。

Abstract: The carbon footprint of any deployed Large Language Model (LLM) accumulates during inference, where repeated use of the model substantially exceeds the one-time cost of fine-tuning. Yet most efficiency interventions target either pre-training scale or post-hoc compression. We ask whether folding a calibrated, differentiable energy surrogate into the fine-tuning objective can produce inference behavior that gains task accuracy at zero or near-zero carbon cost, a break-even configuration. We propose a joint loss mechanism with a per-model carbon-emission parameter, a linear surrogate over parameter norm, FLOP proxy, and a memory proxy, fit from on-hardware energy profiling. We fine-tune three architecturally distinct families: Gemma-2 2B, Llama-3.1 8B, and Qwen-2.5 14B, and evaluate inference F1 and CO$_2$ emissions on three MMLU subjects: abstract algebra, philosophy, and formal logic. We discover from several outcomes that the carbon term behaves as either harmful interference or beneficial regularization depending on the task structure. We position calibrated carbon-aware fine-tuning as a lightweight, drop-in regularizer with a non-empty but model and task-dependent break-even region. This is an ongoing work, and we will release our codebase soon.

</details>


### [121] [SurveyReview: A Reviewer-Aligned Benchmark for Survey Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07641)
*Yuheng Zhang,Yuanchun Wang,Fanjin Zhang,Ruyu Zhao,Juanzi Li,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SurveyReview，一个与审稿人对齐的多维度基准数据集，用于系统评估survey生成质量。该数据集包含675篇survey论文和1,630份审稿报告，将自由文本审稿意见转换为四个维度（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）的评分及理由。同时开发了SurveyAlign基线评估器，通过对Qwen3-32B进行LoRA微调并融合外部知识，在测试集上将与人类审稿人的对齐度大幅提升（MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM自动化生成survey论文的规模扩大，可靠评估成为瓶颈。现有方法大多直接使用现成的LLM-as-a-judge方法，未与人类审稿人进行系统对齐，且缺乏量化评估自动评估器与人类审稿人一致性的系统框架。

Method: 收集并标注675篇survey论文和1,630份审稿报告，将非结构化自由文本审稿意见转化为四维评分（可读性、批判性、全面性、结构）并附带支持理由；发布标准化的训练/测试划分及评测协议；开发SurveyAlign基线评估器，通过LoRA微调Qwen3-32B并在知识密集型维度上增强外部知识输入。

Result: 在测试集上，SurveyAlign相比基于GPT-5.2的提示式评判，在四个维度上平均MSE从2.28降至1.38，MAE从1.15降至0.69，显著提升了与人类审稿人的对齐度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个多维度、与审稿人对齐的survey评审数据集及可复现的评估框架，并开发了强基线评估器，为未来研究提供了具有竞争力的参考基准。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models has transformed survey writing from a months-long manual effort into an automated process. As generation scales, reliable evaluation becomes the bottleneck, and LLMs are increasingly used as survey evaluators. However, existing approaches largely rely on off-the-shelf LLM-as-a-judge methods without systematic alignment to human reviewers, and there remains a lack of systematic frameworks for quantifying alignment with human reviewers. To address this gap, we propose SurveyReview, a reviewer-aligned, multi-dimensional benchmark and dataset for survey evaluation. We collect and annotate 675 survey papers with 1,630 review reports. We structure authentic peer-review reports by converting free-form comments into four-dimensional scores (Readability, Criticalness, Comprehensiveness, Structure) paired with supporting rationales. We further release standardized train/test splits and an evaluation protocol to measure alignment between automatic evaluators and human reviewers. To validate the benchmark, we develop SurveyAlign, a strong baseline evaluator by fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with LoRA on our annotated data, augmented with external knowledge for knowledge-intensive dimensions. On the test set, SurveyAlign substantially improves reviewer alignment over prompt-based judging with GPT-5.2, reducing average MSE from 2.28 to 1.38 and MAE from 1.15 to 0.69 across all four dimensions. Our contributions are twofold: (1) we establish the first multi-dimensional, reviewer-aligned dataset with a reproducible evaluation framework for survey reviewing; (2) we develop a strong baseline evaluator that substantially improves alignment with human reviewers, providing a competitive reference for future research. Our code and data are available at https://surveyreview.github.io

</details>


### [122] [Evaluating Dedicated Monolingual and Joint Multilingual Causal Models for Dravidian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07727)
*Venkata Naga Sai Vishnu Rohit Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从头训练了五个GPT-2架构模型，比较四种达罗毗荼语（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语）的单语模型与一个共享分词器的多语模型，并在困惑度、比特每字节、分词器效率和微调任务上与mGPT对比。结果显示单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别上优于mGPT，且单语分词器效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达罗毗荼语系语言（泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语）在多语言预训练模型中仅占极少训练数据，因此这些模型对每种语言的真实能力存疑，有必要专门研究单语与多语模型在这些低资源语言上的性能差异。

Method: 从头训练五个GPT-2架构模型：四个单语模型（每种语言独立训练，各配32K词表大小的子词分词器）和一个多语模型（四种语言共享一个64K词表子词分词器）。所有模型均在清洗后的CC-100、Wikipedia和Samanantar数据上训练，并通过困惑度、比特每字节、分词器效率及微调结果与mGPT进行对比评估。

Result: 单语模型在情感分类和命名实体识别任务上均优于mGPT；单语分词器在所有测试语言上的分词效率都优于共享的多语分词器。

Conclusion: 针对达罗毗荼语系语言，从头训练单语模型相比使用共享分词器的多语模型（如mGPT）具有更优的性能和分词效率，表明多语言预训练模型对这些低资源语言的能力可能不足，单语专业化建模是更有效的方案。

Abstract: Dravidian languages, mainly Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam make up only a small part of the data used to train multilingual language models, so it's not clear how much per-language ability these models actually keep. I have trained five GPT-2 architecture models from scratch to compare four monolingual models (one each for Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam, each with its own 32K-vocabulary subword tokenizer) against one multilingual model sharing a 64K-vocabulary subword tokenizer across all four languages. All the 5 models are trained on cleaned CC-100, Wikipedia, and Samanantar data. I have tested the models on perplexity, bits-per-byte, tokenizer efficiency, and fine-tuning results which are compared against mGPT. The monolingual models outperform mGPT on sentiment classification and named entity recognition, and their tokenizers proved more efficient than the shared multilingual model across all the languages tested.

</details>


### [123] [The No-Meaning Falsity: The Structural Impossibility of the Arbitrary Sign in Classical Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07737)
*Elnaserledinellah Mahmoud Abdelwahab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过形式化的数学模型证明：基于索绪尔任意符号假说的后现代"无限制语义不确定性"主张与古典阿拉伯语的结构架构不相容。研究者建立了阿拉伯语非连接形态学的数学模型，证明每个词项由"词根-模式"对唯一生成（形态对应定理），词汇意义在派生层面即已确定（语义定位定理）。同时用条件Kolmogorov复杂度形式化相对任意性，证明一般相对任意性不可判定，而阿拉伯语相对任意性可判定且小于1，从而确立了阿拉伯语与印欧语之间的严格系统复杂度不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验后现代主义关于语义不受限制的不确定性主张及其哲学基础——索绪尔的任意符号公理——是否与古典阿拉伯语的结构特性相兼容，从语言结构角度对哲学主张进行数学化证伪或证实。

Method: 建立阿拉伯语非连接形态学的形式数学模型，其中词汇意义由不变量词根与形态句法模式之间的交互决定；提出形态对应定理与语义定位定理；用条件Kolmogorov复杂度将索绪尔的相对任意性形式化为"无规则性质"（no rule property）；区分W级和M级动机性能指。

Result: 证明了一般相对任意性在算法意义上是不可判定的（形式化不可判定）；阿拉伯语的相对任意性是可判定的，且其动机性能指（W和M级）的相对任意性严格小于1；阿拉伯语与印欧语之间在系统复杂度上存在严格的不对称性。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯语的结构架构（非连接形态学）使得其语义确定性远高于后现代无限制不确定性主张所预设的水平，且阿拉伯语相比印欧语具有更强的形态动机性，从而对索绪尔任意性假说和后现代语义不确定性主张提供了形式化的挑战与限定。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether the postmodern claim of unrestricted semantic indeterminacy, and its foundational Saussurean axiom of the arbitrary sign, are compatible with the structural architecture of Classical Arabic. We develop a formal mathematical model of Arabic non concatenative morphology in which lexical meaning is determined by the interaction between an invariant root and a morphosyntactic pattern. Within this framework, we establish a Morphological Correspondence Theorem, demonstrating that every lexical item is uniquely generated by a root pattern pair, and a Semantic Localization Theorem, proving that lexical meaning is determined at the derivational level prior to surface realization. To address Saussurean weaker notion of relative arbitrariness, we formalize it via conditional Kolmogorov complexity, defining arbitrariness algorithmically as the no rule property. We prove that general relative arbitrariness is formally undecidable, while Arabic relative arbitrariness is decidable and provably less than 1 for its motivated signifiers (Levels W and M), establishing a strict system complexity asymmetry over Indo-European languages.

</details>


### [124] [Jako Tako or Fluent? Presenting PoVisLE: A Polish Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07763)
*Anna Kołos,Grzegorz Statkiewicz,Karolina Seweryn,Katarzyna Kowol,Karolina Piosek,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PoVisLE，一个面向波兰语的单文化视觉语言基准数据集，包含1,117张图像和2,366个手工标注的VQA对，用于评估视觉语言模型在文化情境下的深层理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型主要基于英语中心化数据训练，在解释区域特定含义、象征性内容和上下文相关视觉线索时存在局限。已有的文化能力基准多为模板驱动且聚焦表面识别，无法评估更深层的语言和语用理解能力。

Method: 构建了PoVisLE基准数据集，包含1,117张图像和2,366个手工标注的VQA对，采用"落地评估"（grounded evaluation）范式，即在视觉上下文中交互解释语言，专门针对波兰文化场景设计。

Result: PoVisLE提供了一个受控且具有挑战性的资源，能够评估超越表面识别的文化情境视觉语言理解能力。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了非英语、非表层文化能力评估的空白，为评估VLM在文化情境下的多模态理解提供了可靠的基准资源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on tasks such as image captioning, visual question answering, and image-to-text generation. However, they are predominantly trained on English-centric data, which limits their ability to handle culturally grounded visual understanding and leads to failures in interpreting region-specific meanings, symbolic content, and context-dependent visual cues. Existing benchmarks for cultural competence are often template-driven and focused on surface-level recognition, making them insufficient for evaluating deeper linguistic and pragmatic understanding in culturally situated settings. We introduce PoVisLE, a monocultural vision-language benchmark for Polish designed to evaluate culturally grounded multimodal understanding under a grounded evaluation paradigm, where language is interpreted in interaction with visual context. The dataset contains 1,117 images and 2,366 manually annotated VQA pairs. Overall, our dataset provides a controlled and challenging resource for assessing culturally grounded vision-language understanding beyond surface-level recognition.

</details>


### [125] [On the use of foundation models in cognitive science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07812)
*Raj Sanjay Shah,Alex Warstadt,Michael Frank,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四阶段的推断框架，用于评估基础模型（FMs）作为认知与发展模型的适用性，强调行为对齐本身不足，需要理论承诺、诊断性任务和对比性评估来使对齐具有科学意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来大量研究评估了基础模型与人类认知及儿童发展的一致性，但将这些模型用作认知模型面临重大方法论和概念挑战。核心问题是：在什么条件下，行为对齐才能证明将FMs视为认知解释模型的合理性？

Method: 提出了一个四阶段推断框架：（1）将人类实验任务改编为模型兼容格式；（2）指定连接假设（linking hypotheses），将模型输出映射到人类行为测量；（3）评估行为对应性；（4）在候选模型或操作之间进行比较。同时明确了连接假设在映射模型输出到人类指标中的角色，识别了制约对齐主张的挑战，并提出了理论驱动和比较性评估的原则。

Result: 论文指出行为拟合单独不足以验证模型有效性，对齐只有在嵌入明确的理论承诺、理论诊断性任务以及跨候选模型的系统对比评估时，才具有科学意义。

Conclusion: 作者主张对FMs作为认知模型进行评估时必须超越单纯的行为对齐，需要结合理论框架和系统性比较评估，才能真正赋予对齐主张以科学解释力。

Abstract: A host of recent studies have evaluated the cognitive and developmental alignment of Foundation Models (FMs). These investigations include evaluations of their correspondence to adult performance across a range of cognitive domains, as well as whether aspects of model training track children's cognitive development. However, using FMs as candidate cognitive models poses significant methodological and conceptual challenges. A key question underlies this effort: under what conditions does behavioral alignment justify treating FMs as explanatory models of cognition? In this paper, we articulate a four-stage inferential framework for evaluating FMs as cognitive and developmental models: adapting human experimental tasks to model-compatible formats, specifying linking hypotheses that map model outputs to human measures, evaluating behavioral correspondence, and comparing across candidate models or manipulations. We clarify the role of linking hypotheses in mapping model outputs to human behavioral measures, identify challenges that constrain alignment claims, and propose principles for theory-driven and comparative evaluation. Throughout, we argue that behavioral fit alone is insufficient. Alignment becomes scientifically meaningful only when embedded within explicit theoretical commitments, theory-diagnostic tasks, and systematic contrastive evaluation across candidate models.

</details>


### [126] ["Many Are My Names": The Anatomy of the Assistant and Its Personas via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07852)
*Adelaide Danilov,Aria Nourbakhsh,Oleksandr Marchenko Breneur,Salima Lamsiyah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型内部如何表征说话者（Assistant、角色扮演人设、故事角色），通过稀疏自编码器提取特征，发现Assistant与人设并非独立，人设保留Assistant核心特征并逐层分化；故事角色则缺乏该核心，且存在"沉浸式模拟模式"区分不同说话者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何内部表征"谁在说话"（Assistant、人设角色或故事角色）这一问题尚未被充分探索，理解这一机制有助于揭示模型的人格化与角色切换的内部原理。

Method: 使用用户情绪文本及对应模型响应的数据集，将三种生成设置（Assistant、Roleplay、Story）分解为稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征，在话轮边界和代词token位置提取特征，通过过滤管道筛选不同深度的特征，并通过操纵效应（steering effects）和激活分布来刻画每个幸存特征。

Result: 主要发现：（1）Assistant与角色扮演人设并非独立选项，人设保留Assistant关联的特征核心，并自浅至深逐层分化（从操作机制到行为与风格特征）；（2）故事角色缺少Assistant关联核心；（3）Story和Roleplay均可通过"沉浸式模拟模式"与Assistant区分；（4）Assistant在默认设置下有时也会进入或缓慢漂移进入该模式。

Conclusion: 模型的说话者表征存在层级化的特征结构：Assistant核心特征是基础，角色扮演在此基础上逐步分化，而故事角色则独立于该核心；"沉浸式模拟模式"可作为区分不同说话者的关键机制，但Assistant也可能默认状态下漂移进入该模式。

Abstract: How a language model internally represents who is speaking, the Assistant, an assigned roleplay persona, or a narrated story character, remains underexplored. We study speaker representations using a dataset of user-expressed emotional text and corresponding model responses. We decompose three generation settings (Assistant, Roleplay, and Story) into sparse autoencoder features extracted at turn-boundary and pronoun-token positions and selected through a filtering pipeline for different depths. We characterize each surviving feature through its steering effects and activation distribution. Our main finding is that the Assistant and roleplay personas are not independent alternatives: personas retain the Assistant-associated feature core while progressively differentiating from it across layers, starting from operational machinery towards behavioral and stylistic features. Meanwhile, generated story characters lack the Assistant-associated core. Both Story and Roleplay can be distinguished from the Assistant with Immersive Simulation Mode. However, the Assistant can sometimes enter or slowly drift into it even in the default setting.

</details>


### [127] [SurakshaEval: An Indic Safety Benchmark for Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07862)
*Debopriyo Banerjee,Kapil Rajesh Kavitha,Angana Borah,Xudong Han,Yuxia Wang,Parameswari Krishnamurthy,Utkarsh Agarwal,Atharva Kulkarni,Swaran Lata,Ayush Munot,Dhruv Sahnan,Aaryamonvikram Singh,Preslav Nakov,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SurakshaEval是一个专为十种主要印度语言和英语设计的新型安全基准数据集，由人工编写的真实场景提示构成，涵盖通用提示和区域/语言特定提示。基准测试表明，即使是强大的多语言大语言模型在印度语言（尤其本地文字）场景下也难以满足细粒度的安全要求，存在过度拒绝、隐含偏见检测不足和区域敏感场景上下文意识不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全评估数据集主要集中于英语和西方语境，忽视了其他语言中的语言多样性和文化相关的安全风险。亟需一个覆盖印度多语言环境的安全基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建SurakshaEval数据集，包含覆盖十种主要印度语言（阿萨姆语、孟加拉语、古吉拉特语、印地语、坎纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语、马拉地语、旁遮普语、泰米尔语和泰卢固语）及英语的人工撰写安全提示，同时包含跨印度通用的提示和区域/语言特定的本地化社会文化敏感性提示。对多种最先进的大语言模型进行基准测试并分析失败模式。

Result: 建立了这些模型在SurakshaEval上的基线安全性能，识别出三大反复出现的失败模式：过度拒绝（over-refusal）、隐含偏见检测漏检、以及在区域敏感场景中缺乏上下文意识。结果显示即使强大的多语言LLM在印度语言环境下也难以可靠地满足细致的安全要求，尤其是在本地文字（native scripts）下表现更差。

Conclusion: 研究强调了将区域特定数据和结构化评估协议纳入安全评估框架的紧迫性，这有助于开发和部署安全、合伦理且符合多元社会价值观的AI系统。

Abstract: Existing safety evaluation datasets for large language models (LLMs) predominantly focus on English and Western contexts, often overlooking the linguistic diversity and culturally grounded safety risks present in other languages. To address this gap, we introduce SurakshaEval, a novel safety benchmark composed of human-written prompts spanning real-world scenarios, explicitly designed for ten major Indian languages - Assamese, Bengali, Gujarati, Hindi, Kannada, Malayalam, Marathi, Punjabi, Tamil, and Telugu, along with English. SurakshaEval includes both generic prompts common across India and region- and language-specific prompts that capture localized sociocultural sensitivities. We benchmark a broad range of state-of-the-art LLMs on SurakshaEval, establish baseline safety performance, and identify recurring failure modes, including over-refusal, missed detection of implicit bias, and insufficient contextual awareness in regionally sensitive settings. Our results show that even strong multilingual LLMs struggle to reliably meet nuanced safety requirements when operating in Indic languages, particularly in native scripts. These findings highlight the urgent need for safety evaluation frameworks that incorporate region-specific data and structured assessment protocols, enabling the development and deployment of AI systems that operate securely, ethically, and in alignment with diverse societal values. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/SurakshaEval. Warning: This paper contains text that may be offensive or unsafe.

</details>


### [128] [Detection of Self-Introductions in Legislative Testimony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07891)
*Sofija Dimitrijevic,Pallavi Das,Kasey Liu,Foaad Khosmood*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用机器学习检测立法委员会证词中自我介绍并提取发言人姓名的流水线。使用154万条话语构建训练集，结合人工启发式自动标注，训练决策树、随机森林和XGBoost三种分类器，并进一步引入BERT概率特征进行增强。XGBoost表现最佳（F1=0.9747），加入BERT特征后F1提升至0.9782。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在政府会议中，自我介绍是立法委员会证词中的常见现象，成功检测并提取发言人姓名对于发言人识别任务极为有用，有助于提升会议记录处理的自动化和准确性。

Method: 构建包含154万话语（覆盖五个州立法会期）的训练集；采用姓名匹配启发式生成自动标签；设计融合词袋、位置上下文、结构信号、介绍短语指示符和话语上下文特征的特征集；训练决策树、随机森林和XGBoost三种分类器；进一步使用微调BERT对全量候选集评分，将BERT概率输出作为额外特征增强XGBoost模型。

Result: XGBoost以F1=0.9747和最少总错误数位列三种分类器之首；加入BERT概率特征后F1从0.9747提升至0.9782，总测试错误从241降至207。与决策树基线（F1=0.9323）相比，主要增益来自话语上下文特征和Boosting集成策略，BERT提供适度的互补信号。假阳性分析表明其中少数是源数据姓名不一致导致的真实自我介绍误标，说明真实性能略优于测量值。

Conclusion: 所提出的BERT增强XGBoost流水线在立法委员会自我介绍检测任务中实现了高精度性能（F1=0.9782），验证了机器学习方法在该任务中的有效性，并指出话语上下文特征和集成策略是关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Self-introductions are common in legislative committee testimonies. Successfully detecting them and extracting the speaker's name is enormously helpful in the task of speaker identification in the context of government meetings. In this paper, we present a pipeline for detection of self-introductions in legislative committee testimony using machine learning. We construct a training dataset from 1.54 million utterances spanning five state legislative sessions, apply a name-matching heuristic to generate automatic labels, and train three classifiers: a decision tree, random forest, and XGBoost to find self-introductions and extract the speaker's name. We construct a feature set combining bag-of-words, positional context, structural signals, introductory phrase indicators, and discourse context features. Among the three classifiers, XGBoost achieves the best performance with an F1 score of 0.9747 and the fewest total errors; adding fine-tuned BERT probability features improves this further. As an extension, we score the full candidate dataset with a fine-tuned BERT classifier and add BERT probability outputs as features. This BERT-augmented XGBoost model improves F1 from 0.9747 to 0.9782 and reduces total test errors from 241 to 207. The primary gain over the decision tree baseline (F1 0.9323) is driven by discourse context features and the boosting ensemble strategy; BERT provides a modest complementary signal. Analysis of false positives reveals that a minority are genuine self-introductions mislabeled due to name inconsistencies in the source data, indicating that measured metrics modestly understate true performance.

</details>


### [129] [Thinking Hard, Not Smart: Reasoning Models Fail to Ration Test-Time Compute Across Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07968)
*Chenrui Fan,Yize Cheng,Ming Li,Yongyuan Liang,Tianyi Zhou,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"考试式"评估框架，研究推理语言模型如何在多个问题之间分配有限的推理计算预算（token预算），以最大化总分。研究发现，当前多个开源和前沿推理模型都无法在全局预算下进行战略性分配，而是表现为贪婪的序贯求解器：按呈现顺序优先处理、在早期问题上投入过多精力、对题目分值不敏感，且这些问题随题目数量增加而加剧。这一行为模式从数学推理延伸到代码推理，表明全局预算分配是一个现有逐题评估无法捕捉、当前推理模型仍需克服的独立能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理语言模型评估通常一次只研究一个问题上的测试时计算分配，但在实际场景中，多个问题往往共享端到端的成本或延迟约束，模型需要决定如何在它们之间分配有限的推理计算资源。这种全局预算分配能力尚未被系统研究。

Method: 作者引入了"考试式"评估框架，模型需要在一个共享的token预算下，在不同难度和分值的多个问题之间进行分配以最大化总分。作者在多个开源和前沿推理模型上进行了实验评估，分析了模型的分配策略、对分值/难度的敏感性，并测试了显式规划提示的效果，同时将测试范围从数学推理扩展到代码推理。

Result: 模型未能战略性地在全局预算下分配计算资源：它们表现为贪婪的序贯求解器，按呈现顺序优先处理问题，在早期问题上投入过多计算，对题目分值不敏感，且这些倾向随问题数量增加而更加显著。显式的规划提示虽然能使计算分布更均匀，但并未产生基于分值或难度的优先排序。相同的模式在数学和代码推理任务中均被观察到。

Conclusion: 全局预算分配是当前推理语言模型尚未掌握的一项独立能力，传统的逐题评估方式无法捕捉这一缺陷。这一发现为推理模型在真实多任务场景下的评估和训练提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Reasoning language models increasingly use test-time compute to improve performance, but existing evaluations typically study this compute one question at a time. Yet when multiple problems share an end-to-end cost or latency constraint, models must decide how to divide limited inference compute among them. We introduce an exam-style evaluation framework for studying this setting, in which a model must distribute one shared token budget across questions with different difficulty and point values to maximize its total score. Across several open and frontier reasoning models, we find that models fail to allocate a shared budget strategically across questions of varying difficulties and values. Models behave largely as greedy sequential solvers: they prioritize questions by presentation order, front-load effort on early questions, and remain insensitive to value, with these tendencies becoming more pronounced as the number of questions grows. Explicit planning prompts spread compute more evenly but do not produce value- or difficulty-aware prioritization. The same behavioral pattern extends from mathematical to code reasoning. These findings establish global budget allocation as a distinct capability that is not captured by conventional per-question evaluation and remains a challenge for current reasoning models.

</details>


### [130] [Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP): Hallucination Detection in LLMs from Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08024)
*Zakhar Mrykhin,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了提示嵌入探针（Prompt Embedding Probes, PEP），一种用于大语言模型回答级幻觉检测的白盒方法。PEP通过在标准线性探针的输入中引入少量可学习的提示嵌入来增强检测能力，在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA数据集上使用不同规模的Qwen3模型进行验证，结果显示PEP在分布内检测中优于标准线性探针，并在生成前预测和跨模型迁移场景中保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能生成流畅且有用的回复，但容易产生幻觉（hallucination），即生成不符合事实的内容。现有方法中，基于隐状态的白盒幻觉检测手段仍有提升空间，需要一种更有效的检测方法来提高可靠性。

Method: PEP扩展了标准线性探针方法，通过在探针输入中附加少量可学习的提示嵌入（prompt embeddings）来增强从冻结LLM隐状态中进行幻觉检测的能力。该方法保持基础模型参数完全冻结，仅新增少量可训练参数。

Result: PEP在TriviaQA、GSM8K和MedQA三个数据集上，使用不同规模的Qwen3模型进行评估。在主要的分布内（in-distribution）设置中，PEP相比标准线性探针显著提升了基于隐状态的幻觉检测性能。在生成前预测和跨模型迁移设置中PEP依然有效，但跨数据集迁移的稳健性仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 基于提示的适配（prompt-based adaptation）能够在不微调骨干网络且仅增加少量可训练参数的情况下，有效增强隐状态探针的幻觉检测能力，为白盒幻觉检测提供了一种轻量且高效的改进方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and useful responses but remain prone to hallucinations. We introduce Prompt Embedding Probes (PEP), a white-box method for answer-level hallucination detection from the hidden states of a frozen LLM. PEP extends standard linear probes by augmenting the input with a small number of learnable prompt embeddings. We evaluate PEP on TriviaQA, GSM8K, and MedQA using Qwen3 models at multiple scales. PEP improves hidden-state-based detection over standard linear probes in the main in-distribution setting. We further evaluate PEP for pre-generation prediction, cross-model transfer, and out-of-distribution generalization. PEP remains effective in the pre-generation and cross-model settings, whereas robust cross-dataset transfer remains difficult. These results show that prompt-based adaptation can strengthen hidden-state probing while keeping the backbone frozen and adding only a small number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [131] [APEX-VW: A Document-Level English-Spanish Post-Editing Dataset in the Healthcare Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08059)
*Marie Escribe,Tharindu Ranasinghe,Amal Haddad Haddad,Hansi Hettiarachchi,Damith Premasiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了APEX-VW语料库，一个基于NHS虚拟病房文档构建的开放英语-西班牙语文档级自动后期编辑（APE）语料库，由专业译员在Trados Studio中对四种机器翻译系统输出进行后期编辑，保留文档序和CAT工具上下文，用于研究术语规范化、修正传播及人机协同翻译辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APE语料库大多在句子层面运作，或为合成数据，无法真实反映专业文档中重复性词汇和术语纠正在CAT工作流中的传播规律。需要一个保留文档级上下文和CAT工具环境的高质量语料库来支撑传播感知的APE研究。

Method: 从NHS虚拟病房文档中选取七个文档连贯的源文本共42k词，使用四种不同范式的MT系统翻译，由专业译员在Trados Studio中完成后期编辑，并设置受控的MT、术语和质量保证条件。语料保留文档顺序和CAT工具上下文，区别于WMT APE、eSCAPE、MLQE-PE和LangMark等现有资源。

Result: 构建了APEX-VW开放EN-ES语料库，覆盖七个文档源文本、四种MT系统与专业后期编辑，提供了语料设计、数据准备、PE设置和初始统计信息，作为文档级APE和传播感知辅助工具的基准资源。

Conclusion: APEX-VW填补了文档级、真实CAT工作流下APE语料库的空白，为术语规范化、修正传播和人在回路的翻译支持研究提供了新的基准平台，有助于推动传播感知型APE系统的发展。

Abstract: Post-Editing (PE) of Machine Translation (MT) output often involves repeating the same lexical and terminological corrections across many segments, especially in specialised and highly repetitive documents. Despite substantial work on Automatic Post-Editing (APE), most available corpora operate at the sentence level, others are synthetic, and overall not designed to study how corrections propagate in realistic Computer-Assisted Translation (CAT) workflows. This paper presents the APEX-VW (Automatic Post-Editing eXperiments on Virtual Wards) Corpus, a new open English-Spanish (EN-ES) dataset built from recent NHS virtual-ward documents and professional PE in Trados Studio, with controlled MT, terminology, and quality assurance settings. The corpus contains seven document-coherent source texts totalling 42k words, translated with four MT systems representing different paradigms and then post-edited by professional translators. Unlike prior resources such as WMT APE corpora, eSCAPE, MLQE-PE, or LangMark, the dataset preserves document order and CAT-tool context, making it suitable for research on terminology normalisation, correction propagation, and human-in-the-loop translation support. The paper describes the corpus design, data preparation, PE setup, and initial corpus statistics, and positions the resource as a benchmark for document-level APE and propagation-aware assistive tools.

</details>


### [132] [DialectS2S: End-to-End Speech Dialogue Modeling for Low-Resource Chinese Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08067)
*Yi Shu,Tianyu Peng,Yingzhuo Deng,Wen Yang,Jun Lin,Changming Xie,Xinyu Yu,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL
