We further separate intra-sample prefix conditioning from inter-sample objective mixing, allowing us to identify the local alignment signal separately from the optional batch-level mixing knob. Across LLaDA2-Mini and Qwen-1.7B backbones, PCD consistently improves over same-family native dLLM stable baselines, reaching a 4.2% relative gain on the main LLaDA2-Mini six-benchmark average (+2.56 points) and a 14.2% relative gain in the primary Qwen mechanism comparison (+4.86 points). These results suggest that aligning the pretraining context distribution with prompt-conditioned generation can recover a measurable part of the dLLM continuation gap without changing inference.

</details>


### [197] [ZetaGPT: A Reference Implementation of Positional--Encoding--Free State--Space--Attention Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09432)
*Róisín Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ZetaGPT，一种无需显式位置编码的紧凑型混合语言模型。其核心思想是在每个模型块中，于自注意力计算之前先应用因果状态空间方程，利用循环状态动态将序列顺序信息隐式编码到token表示中。该模型是完全开源的，提供从数据集构建、tokenizer训练、预训练、SFT、RLHF到CoT推理的端到端训练流水线，是首个无显式位置编码的开源小语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的自注意力计算是置换等变的，缺乏内在的token顺序表示机制。现有架构（如RoPE、学习式位置嵌入）都将位置信息视为架构后天获取的能力而非模型固有属性。作者追求无位置编码的架构，希望通过因果状态空间方程隐式编码位置信息，从而消除对显式位置编码的依赖。

Method: 每个模型块在自注意力之前集成一个因果状态空间方程，通过循环状态动态将序列顺序信息编码进token表示。这样后续的注意力层可以在不依赖显式位置编码的情况下操作位置感知的表示，同时保留自注意力的表达建模能力。此外，ZetaGPT提供完整开源训练流水线，涵盖数据构建、tokenizer训练、预训练、SFT、RLHF和纯强化学习的CoT推理。

Result: ZetaGPT成为首个无显式位置编码的开源小语言模型，建立了紧凑、可复现的参考实现，可用于位置编码无关语言模型的开发与实证研究。

Conclusion: 通过将因果状态空间方程嵌入注意力之前，语言模型可以在无需显式位置编码的情况下获得位置感知能力，同时保持自注意力的表达能力。ZetaGPT作为开源参考实现，为研究无位置编码架构和快速原型验证提供了完整的基础设施。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models rely on self-attention, whose computation is permutation-equivariant and therefore lacks an intrinsic mechanism for representing token order. Existing architectures address this limitation by explicitly incorporating positional information through learned positional embeddings or hand-crafted positional encodings, such as rotary positional encoding (RoPE), treating positional information as an architecturally acquired capability rather than an inherent property of the model. Motivated by the pursuit of positional-encoding-free architectures, this work explores a language model architecture that integrates causal state-space equations to implicitly encode positional information before attention computation. Specifically, each model block applies a causal state-space equation before self-attention, allowing recurrent state dynamics to encode sequential information into token representations. Consequently, subsequent attention layers operate on position-aware representations without requiring explicit positional encodings while retaining the expressive modeling capacity of self-attention. We present \textsc{ZetaGPT}, a compact hybrid language model designed for research, rapid prototyping, algorithm verification, and educational applications. In addition to the proposed architecture, \textsc{ZetaGPT} provides a fully open-source, end-to-end training pipeline encompassing dataset construction, tokenizer training, pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning via pure reinforcement learning. To the best of our knowledge, \textsc{ZetaGPT} is the first open-source small language model without explicit positional encoding and establishes a compact, reproducible reference implementation for the development and empirical study of positional-encoding-free language models.

</details>


### [198] [Learning Preference Adaptation for Large Language Model Personalization via Verbal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09507)
*Yuting Liu,Wei Wu,Jianzhe Zhao,Guibing Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AlignXada，一种无需训练的元学习框架，通过语言强化学习迭代优化文本精炼策略，将通用用户偏好摘要自适应为任务特定的偏好表示，在保留决策相关证据的同时删除冗余上下文，平均提升3.82分且仅保留22.8%的原始令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言用户偏好为LLM个性化提供了可解释接口，但通用偏好摘要包含大量与特定下游任务无关的信息。直接将完整偏好摘要提供给模型会浪费上下文容量并引入跨任务干扰，而人工设计任务特定的偏好视图难以规模化。因此需要一种自动化的任务特定偏好适配方法。

Method: 提出AlignXada，一个训练免费的元学习框架。它诱导可复用的文本精炼策略，将通用偏好摘要适配为任务特定版本。精炼策略通过元学习器以语言强化学习（verbal reinforcement learning）方式迭代优化，无需额外训练。

Result: 在13个任务和3个下游模型（共39个任务-模型组合）上，AlignXada平均提升3.82分，改善33个组合，仅保留原始偏好摘要22.8%的令牌，并在36个组合中优于RAG基线。扩展的忠实度分析显示精炼后的偏好文件在很大程度上保持对源偏好的忠实性，同时保留任务相关的个性化信号。

Conclusion: 偏好侧适配可以作为通用记忆构建的实用补充，用于终身个性化智能体。精炼后的偏好文件既能保持与源偏好的忠实性，又能保留任务相关的个性化信号，表明任务特定偏好适配是一种有效且可扩展的个性化策略。

Abstract: Natural language user preferences provide an interpretable interface for LLM personalization. However, universal preference summaries often contain information irrelevant to a particular downstream task. Directly supplying the full preference summary therefore wastes context capacity and introduces cross-task distraction, while manually designing task-specific preference views is difficult to scale. In this work, we study \emph{task-specific preference adaptation}: given a universal user preference summary and a downstream task, derive a task-conditioned representation that preserves sufficient decision-relevant evidence while removing redundant context. To this end, we propose \textsc{AlignXada}, a training-free meta-learning framework that induces reusable textual refinement policies for adapting universal preference summaries to task-specific ones. The refinement policy is iteratively optimized by a meta learner through verbal reinforcement learning. Across 13 tasks and three downstream models (39 task--model cells), \textsc{AlignXada} achieves an average gain of 3.82 points, improving 33 cells while retaining only 22.8\% of the original profile tokens and outperforming RAG in 36 cells. An extended faithfulness analysis further shows that the refined profiles remain largely grounded in the source preferences while preserving task-relevant personalization signals, suggesting that profile-side adaptation serves as a practical complement to universal memory construction for lifelong personalized agents.

</details>


### [199] [Build it, Break it, Repeat: Benchmarking and improving LLM-manipulated disinformation detection in social media posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09510)
*Kevin Thomas,Milosz Kasprzyk,Reuel C Igbokwe Onuigbo,Elliott Pert,Cameron Tovey,João A. Leite,Olesya Razuvayevskaya,Carolina Scarton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Build it, Break it, Repeat (BiBiR)框架，通过五轮迭代对抗性压力测试来评估社交媒体上机器生成虚假信息检测器的鲁棒性。结果表明，最强的攻击方法（回译+基于LLM角色重写）实现了95%的标签翻转率，而最佳检测器（DASS三元组对比模型）平均准确率达72.68%，比强基线高出15个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型使得生成和改写误导性内容变得更加容易，社交媒体上检测机器生成的虚假信息越来越困难。静态基准评估无法捕捉检测器在面对故意设计以逃避分类的内容转换时的行为表现，因此需要一种迭代式的对抗性压力测试框架来评估检测器的鲁棒性。

Method: 将Build it, Break it, Fix it框架适配为Build it, Break it, Repeat (BiBiR)，通过五轮迭代会话对检测器进行对抗性压力测试。该框架包括两个角色：攻击方（breakers）系统性地转换虚假信息帖文以逃避分类，以及构建方（builders）开发检测模型抵御攻击。

Result: 最佳攻击方法结合了回译和基于LLM角色重写，实现95%的标签翻转率且保留原始帖文语义。最佳检测模型为采用动态锚点切换（DASS）架构的三元组对比模型，平均准确率72.68%，在最具挑战性的攻击集上比微调e5-small-LoRA强基线高出15个百分点。

Conclusion: 迭代式框架最能暴露检测器的弱点并推动鲁棒性改进；但仍需语义保留分析来区分有效的对抗性规避与改变原始虚假信息主张语义的转换。

Abstract: Detecting machine-generated disinformation on social media is increasingly difficult as large language models (LLMs) make it easier to generate and rewrite misleading content at scale. Static benchmark evaluations, measuring detector performance on fixed held-out datasets, do not capture how detectors behave when posts are deliberately transformed to evade classification. This paper adapts the Build it, Break it, Fix it framework into Build it, Break it, Repeat (BiBiR): iterative sessions designed to stress-test detectors' robustness under iterative adversarial conditions, evaluating whether models remain reliable when disinformation posts are systematically transformed to evade classification. Across five iterations, the findings show that the best adversarial breakers' transformations came from a combination of back-translation and LLM persona-based rewriting, with the best performing technique achieving a 95% label flip rate (LFR), whilst still preserving the meaning of the original posts. The best builders' model was a triplet contrastive model with a dynamic anchor switching (DASS) architecture, which achieved an average accuracy of 72.68%, outperforming the strong baseline (a fine-tuned e5-small-LoRA) by 15 percentage points on the most robust set of breakers' adversarial attacks. The results demonstrate that an iterative framework best exposes detector weaknesses and pushes robustness improvements; however, it may still require semantic preservation analysis to distinguish valid adversarial evasion from transformations that changed the original disinformation claims' meaning.

</details>


### [200] [TCS-BENCH: Benchmarking State-of-the-Art Generative AI Theoretical Computer Science Research Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09538)
*Vincent Cohen-Addad,Dimitris Paparas,Ernest van Wijland,Max Springer,Julien Canitrot-Paradis,Honghao Lin,David Woodruff,Adarsh Kumarappan,Rajesh Jayaram,Rudrajit Das,Lalit Jain,Ola Svensson,Silvio Lattanzi,Mislav Balunovic,Theophane Weber,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TCS-Bench，一个用于评估大型语言模型在理论研究级计算机科学（TCS）定理证明生成能力的基准测试。该基准包含来自STOC、FOCS和SODA等顶级会议论文中的定理证明任务，并通过验证智能体（准确率超过90%）来检查生成证明的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估基准主要集中在常规推理任务上，缺乏面向研究级理论计算机科学定理证明的评估基准。TCS领域需要真实的、自包含的定理证明任务来评估LLM在该领域的实际能力。

Method: 从STOC、FOCS和SODA顶级会议论文中提取定理证明任务，为每个任务提供必要的上下文以确保能够生成自包含的证明。使用一个验证智能体来检查生成证明的正确性，并在专家标注的数据集上对该验证器进行基准评估。

Result: 提出的参考验证器在专家标注的证明判断数据集上达到了超过90%的准确率，验证了该验证方法在评估LLM生成的定理证明方面的可靠性。

Conclusion: TCS-Bench为评估LLM在理论研究级TCS领域定理证明能力提供了一个可靠的基准框架，通过高准确率的验证器确保评估结果的可信度。

Abstract: We introduce TCS-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) on research-level Theoretical Computer Science (TCS) proof generation. TCS-Bench consists of theorem-proving tasks from papers published at top theoretical computer science venues (STOC, FOCS, and SODA). Each task provides the necessary context to derive a self-contained proof for a target result. We evaluate state-of-the-art models on this benchmark. We verify the correctness of generated proofs via a verification agent, and further benchmark the verifier against human-expert proof judgements on a set of target statements and generated proofs pairs. Our reference verifier achieves over 90% accuracy on the expert labeled set.

</details>


### [201] [Mawqif-v2: An Arabic Benchmark Dataset for Cross-Target Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09539)
*Rasha Albalawi,Nuha Albadi,Hamzah Luqman,Maram Kurdi,Saad Ezzini,Asma Yamani,Ahmed Ashraf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mawqif-v2扩展数据集，包含996条手动标注的阿拉伯语推文，涵盖三个公开目标（女性驾驶、电动汽车、学期制），每条推文标注了立场、情感和讽刺标签。该扩展作为保留测试集，用于评估模型在跨目标（语义相关和未见目标）上的泛化能力，并提供了多种阿拉伯语/多语言Transformer模型及零样本大语言模型的基准结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公开阿拉伯语目标特定立场检测数据集有限，特别是缺乏用于评估跨目标泛化能力的基准，因此需要构建一个专门的保留评估集来推动该方向的研究。

Method: 构建了Mawqif-v2扩展数据集：从三个公开目标收集996条阿拉伯语推文并手动标注立场、情感和讽刺标签，遵循原始Mawqif标注方案。原始Mawqif数据集用于训练和开发，扩展集作为保留测试集。同时使用多种阿拉伯语和多语言Transformer模型以及零样本LLM建立基线结果。

Result: 提供了Mawqif-v2扩展数据集及多种模型的基线性能结果，与原始Mawqif数据集共同构成评估阿拉伯语立场检测跨目标泛化能力的基准。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了阿拉伯语跨目标立场检测评估资源的空白，为相关领域研究提供了可复现的评估基准。

Abstract: Publicly available Arabic datasets for target-specific stance detection remain limited, particularly for evaluating cross-target generalization. This paper presents the Mawqif-v2 Extension, consisting of 996 manually annotated Arabic tweets collected from three public targets: Women Driving, E-Cars, and Trimester System. Each tweet is annotated with stance, sentiment, and sarcasm labels following the original Mawqif annotation scheme. The released extension is intended as a held-out evaluation set for assessing model generalization to both semantically related and previously unseen targets, while the original Mawqif dataset is used for training and development. In addition, we establish baseline results using several Arabic and multilingual transformer models, as well as zero-shot large language models (LLMs), to facilitate reproducible evaluation. Together with the original Mawqif dataset, the Mawqif-v2 Extension provides a benchmark for evaluating cross-target generalization in Arabic stance detection.

</details>


### [202] [ELBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Education-Facing Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09548)
*Yilin Jiang,Xiaorong Zhu,Fei Tan,Zicheng Zhang,Kaiyi Huang,Yang Yu,Zexuan Fei,Yiming Luo,Keqian Li,Hao Hao,Aimin Zhou,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ELBench，首个在同一协议下同时评估教育场景四大需求（通用能力、安全可信、基础教育、高阶培养）的基准。通过对9个模型（7个前沿通用模型+2个教育专用模型）的评估发现：分模块画像比单一总分更具信息量；国产模型在安全模块领先；教育专用模型在两大教育模块中均未领先，且所有模型在高阶培养的结构化判断任务上存在系统性盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在教育场景（如助教、教学助理、内容生成）中的部署对模型提出了远超普通问答的综合要求，包括准确性、敏感提示下的安全性、教学实用性与教学目标对齐。然而现有基准彼此割裂，无法评估模型在教育场景下的整体适宜性，缺乏一个在同一协议下综合考核各项需求的统一基准。

Method: 构建ELBench基准，在统一协议下对同一批模型评估四个模块：通用能力、安全与可信、基础教育、高阶培养。数据来源结合精选的公共数据集与新合成的安全和培养数据。评估对象为9个模型，其中7个为前沿通用模型、2个为教育专用变体。

Result: 研究发现：1）模块级画像比单一总分更有区分度，总分前六的模型统计上无显著差异，但模块领先者各不相同，且安全与教学实用性显著负相关（r=-0.83）；2）中国开发的模型在安全模块领先（该模块区分度最高），在区域规范性内容上优势最大，在通用危害内容上优势缩小但未消失；3）两个教育专用模型在两大教育模块中均未领先，且所有模型在高阶培养的结构化判断任务上存在系统性盲区——均选择同一非参考选项，偏向教学风格而非目标契合度，导致该模块分数普遍偏低且无法区分模型。

Conclusion: ELBench表明教育场景下的模型评估应采用多模块画像而非单一分数；安全能力与实用教学能力之间存在明显张力；教育专用模型的领域后训练可能并未跟上前沿通用模型的教育任务表现，此问题值得进一步探究但本研究尚不能给出明确结论。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in education as tutors, teaching assistants, and content generators. These roles place demands that ordinary question answering does not: a usable education-facing model is supposed to be accurate, safe under sensitive prompts, instructionally useful, and aligned with pedagogical goals at the same time. Existing benchmarks evaluate these requirements largely in isolation, so none assesses education-facing suitability as an integrated profile. We introduce ELBench, the first benchmark to evaluate all four requirements (General Capability, Safety and Trustworthiness, Basic Education, and High-Level Cultivation) on the same models under a common protocol, combining curated public sources with newly synthesized safety and cultivation data. We evaluate nine models, seven frontier general-purpose systems and two education-specialized variants, and report three findings. First, module-level profiles are more informative than a single aggregate: the top six models are statistically indistinguishable on overall score, yet their module leaders differ substantially, and safety is anti-correlated with practical teaching (r = -0.83). Second, the Chinese-developed models lead the safety module, the most discriminative in the suite; this advantage is largest on region-specific normative content and narrows, but does not vanish, on universal-harm content. Third, the two education-specialized models lead neither education module, and on High-Level Cultivation all models share a systematic blind spot: on the structured judgment task they converge on the same non-reference option, favoring pedagogical style over fit to the stated goal, so the module scores uniformly low and does not separate models. This raises, but does not resolve, whether domain post-training keeps pace with frontier systems on education tasks.

</details>


### [203] [Pragmatic Attack Surface: Vulnerabilities of Implicit Context in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09551)
*Bocheng Chen,Han Zi,Roucheng Ou,Yawei Liu,Minyue Chen,Zimo Qi,Rongrong Wang,Guangliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"语用攻击面"（pragmatic attack surface）这一新型LLM安全漏洞概念：现有安全对齐机制依赖显式语言线索进行防护，但人类语言本质上依赖隐式的语用上下文（如世界知识、社会规范）来理解，这些上下文未在对齐中被充分利用，攻击者可通过利用这类隐性语用信息绕过LLM安全机制，实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注攻击者操纵自然语言中的显式线索来诱导LLM产生有害输出，这类攻击可被已有的安全对齐算法缓解。然而作者指出，人类语言理解本质上依赖语用学（pragmatics），即需要借助隐式的世界知识、社会规范等上下文来进行解释。这些隐式上下文未被安全对齐有效利用，形成了语言理解与安全对齐之间的根本性错配，构成一个尚未被充分研究的安全漏洞。

Method: 本文通过系统地分析语言理解与安全对齐之间的差异，阐明了语用攻击面的形成机理，并提出了一种利用该攻击面进行攻击的方法。该方法在多个开源和闭源模型上进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的攻击方法在多个开源和闭源模型上均显著优于基线攻击方法，取得了高攻击成功率，验证了语用攻击面这一漏洞的真实性和严重性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一个此前未被认识到的LLM安全漏洞类别——语用攻击面，指出安全对齐需要超越显式语言线索层面的防御，将人类的语用推理能力纳入对齐机制的设计中，以更好地防御此类攻击。

Abstract: In the era of large language models (LLMs), attackers often manipulate natural language to elicit unsafe or harmful outputs, creating a new natural language attack surface unique to LLM-based systems, where attacks directly exploit explicit linguistic cues in user prompts to bypass the safety mechanism of LLMs. However, such attacks can often be mitigated by existing safety alignment algorithms. On the other hand, human language is inherently grounded in pragmatics, necessitating typical context to interpret language, e.g., world knowledge, social norms. However, such contexts are often implicit because they are not directly expressed in human language and are not sufficiently leveraged in safety alignment, creating a fundamental mismatch between human language interpretation and safety alignment approaches. In this paper, we demonstrate that this mismatch exposes vulnerabilities in LLMs. We refer to this vulnerability as the pragmatic attack surface, which can be exploited to achieve high attack success rates. The experimental results demonstrate that our proposed approach outperforms baseline attack methods across various open-source and closed-source models by a substantial margin.

</details>


### [204] [Se-DPO: Self-Evolving Token Credit for Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09568)
*Wenxiao Zhao,Shu Wang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Se-DPO（自进化令牌信用DPO），针对DPO中所有token等权贡献偏好信号的问题，引入动态token信用机制，根据每个token对偏好结果的贡献大小调节其KL正则化强度，无需外部模型，仅增加轻量校准网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DPO通过对token级log概率比进行均匀求和，隐式假设所有token对偏好信号的贡献相等。但实际中不同token的贡献差异显著，且有效token信用与隐式奖励的幅度成正比，该值在训练过程中会大幅变化，导致静态token信用随训练推进越发失配。

Method: Se-DPO从模型自身演化中的内部信号动态推导token信用，同时考虑每个token贡献的强度与置信度来校准信用值。该方法不依赖外部模型，仅引入一个轻量级的校准网络，计算开销极小。

Result: 实验表明Se-DPO在AlpacaEval 2上较DPO提升最多9.8个点，在Arena-Hard上提升最多12.2个点。

Conclusion: 通过基于模型内部演化信号动态校准token信用，Se-DPO有效解决了DPO中token等权假设带来的对齐偏差问题，以极低的额外开销显著提升了偏好优化性能。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aggregates token-level log-probability ratios via uniform summation, implicitly treating all tokens as contributing equally to the preference signal. However, the contribution of individual tokens to the preference signal varies. We introduce token credit, which modulates each token's KL regularization based on its contribution to the preference outcome. We derive that effective token credit is proportional to the magnitude of each token's implicit reward, and observe that this quantity evolves substantially during training. This implies that static token credit becomes increasingly misaligned as training progresses. In this work, we propose Se-DPO (Self-Evolving Token Credit for DPO), a live mechanism that derives token credit from the model's own evolving internal signals during DPO training. Since the reward signal varies in reliability across positions, Se-DPO calibrates token credit based on both the strength and the confidence of each token's contribution. Se-DPO requires no external models, adding only a lightweight calibration network with minimal computational overhead. Experiments show that Se-DPO improves over DPO by up to 9.8 points on AlpacaEval~2 and 12.2 points on Arena-Hard.

</details>


### [205] [MDB-Link: Hierarchical Schema Linking for Multi-Database Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09588)
*Beiyu Xu,Zhenyu Wu,Jiaoyan Chen,Riza theresa Batista-navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MDB-Link，一个面向多数据库场景的分层模式链接框架，通过全局索引检索列、聚合证据筛选数据库、并使用预算感知的LLM进行数据库重排、表选择和列对齐，显著提升了数据库定位和模式子集构建的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Text-to-SQL研究假设目标数据库已知，忽略了需要在大型异构数据库集合中路由查询的真实场景。现有方法无法处理多数据库下的模式链接问题，因此需要先定位目标数据库，再构建紧凑的SQL相关模式用于生成。

Method: MDB-Link采用分层模式链接框架：首先从全局索引检索问题相关列，聚合检索证据以筛选候选数据库，然后利用预算感知的大语言模型进行数据库重排序、表选择和列对齐（grounding），最终生成紧凑的SQL相关模式子集。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-14B，MDB-Link在MMQA、Spider2-Snow和BIRD-dev上均优于LinkAlign的数据库定位和列选择性能，模式子集大小接近黄金模式。精确匹配分数在MMQA上从16.88提升到51.41，Spider2-Snow上从2.50提升到9.17，BIRD-dev上从12.52提升到38.01；运行速度也快于LinkAlign和AutoLink。

Conclusion: MDB-Link通过分层模式缩减策略有效解决了多数据库场景下的目标数据库定位和紧凑模式构建问题，实验证明其在准确性、效率及下游SQL生成效果上均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Traditional Text-to-SQL research and benchmarks assume a known target database, overlooking settings in which a query must be routed within a large, heterogeneous database collection. We therefore study schema linking in a multi-database setting, where the system must first locate the target database and then construct a compact, SQL-relevant schema for generation. We propose MDB-Link, a hierarchical schema-linking framework that retrieves question-relevant columns from a global index, aggregates retrieval evidence to shortlist databases, and uses a budget-aware large language model (LLM) for database reranking, table selection, and column grounding. With Qwen2.5-14B, MDB-Link outperforms LinkAlign on MMQA, Spider2-Snow, and BIRD-dev in database localization and column selection while producing schema subsets close in size to the gold schemas. Exact match improves from 16.88 to 51.41 on MMQA, 2.50 to 9.17 on Spider2-Snow, and 12.52 to 38.01 on BIRD-dev. MDB-Link also runs faster than LinkAlign and AutoLink, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical schema reduction for downstream SQL generation.

</details>


### [206] [Measuring the Wrong Thing: Internal Harmfulness Scores Anti-Rank Successful Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09624)
*Mingyu Luo,Ming Deng,Zilang Qiu,Yiming Cheng,Ci Tao,Xue Tan,Sijin Sun,Yangfu Li,Ping Chen,Jun Dai,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了"内部安全分数通过提示词有害性分离来预测攻击成功"这一推断。作者引入Active Attention Probing（固定内容无关的测量坐标），发现包装攻击会使有害生成率上升（0.05→0.27）而有害意图AUROC下降（0.936→0.803），攻击变得更危险但提示词在安全分数下显得更安全。被包装的有害提示中，结果AUROC仅为0.220，即成功攻击反而排在失败攻击之下。该逆转在三种目标模型、七类攻击家族和两个独立评判器上持续存在，且分布偏移首先破坏校准和阈值迁移，然后才破坏排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全过滤器使用内部安全分数在生成前判断提示词，其有效性依赖于"分数能区分有害/良性提示"与"分数能识别成功攻击"之间的隐含推断。但有害意图是提示词本身的属性，而越狱成功是由目标模型、解码策略和评判器共同决定的结果。若分数测量的量不对，误报预算将浪费在本来就会失败的攻击上。作者旨在审计这一推断是否成立。

Method: 引入Active Attention Probing（AAP），提供固定的内容无关测量坐标，避免包装改变被评判内容和信号提取位置的问题。将每个基础目标配对普通版和包装版提示，从目标模型生成真实完成结果。在Llama上测量有害意图AUROC与有害生成率；对包装后的有害提示单独计算结果AUROC；使用稀有token、被动通道和检测器派生通道在相同匹配设计上验证；跨三种目标模型、七类攻击家族和两个独立评判器检验稳健性。

Result: 在Llama上，包装使有害生成率从0.05升至0.27，而有害意图AUROC从0.936降至0.803，攻击更危险但分数认为提示更安全。包装的有害提示中结果AUROC为0.220，成功攻击排在失败攻击之下。该逆转在多种通道、三种模型、七类攻击家族和两个评判器上均复现。分布偏移下校准和阈值迁移先于排序退化。

Conclusion: 内部安全分数在"有害意图分离"与"攻击成功预测"之间存在根本性错位：包装攻击被错误地判断为更安全，且成功攻击在分数上反而低于失败攻击。这否定了用内部分数过滤提示词的有效性推断，警示安全过滤器的评估方式需要以实际攻击结果为准而非单纯的意图分离，且分布偏移对校准/阈值的破坏先于对排序的破坏，进一步影响实际部署的可靠性。

Abstract: Internal safety scores judge a prompt before any text is generated, and they are validated by how well they separate harmful prompts from benign ones. That separation is then read as evidence that the score will also catch the attacks that succeed. Harmful intent is a property of the prompt. Jailbreak success is an outcome produced later by a particular target model, decoding policy, and judge. A filter tuned on a score that measures the wrong quantity spends its false positive budget on attacks that would have failed anyway. In this paper we audit that inference. Attention based measurements are usually read from prompt dependent locations, so a wrapper changes both the content being judged and the place the signal is taken from. We therefore introduce Active Attention Probing, which supplies a fixed content independent measurement coordinate. We pair every base goal with a plain and a wrapped version and generate real completions from the target models. On Llama, wrapping raises harmful generation from 0.05 to 0.27 while harmful intent AUROC falls from 0.936 to 0.803, so the attacks grow more dangerous while the prompts look safer to the score. Among wrapped harmful prompts the outcome AUROC is 0.220, which places the attacks that succeeded below the attacks that failed. Rare token, passive, and detector derived channels reproduce the reversal on the same matched design, and the reversal itself persists across three target models, seven attack families, and two independent judges. Distribution shift then degrades calibration and threshold transfer before it degrades ranking.

</details>


### [207] [How Do Large Language Models Judge Social Attraction? Evidence from Theory-Grounded Persona Ratings Across Multiple LLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09717)
*Hasan Mahmud,Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh,Jamison Heard,Prabu David*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过三项研究检验大型语言模型（LLMs）作为社会吸引力评判者的有效性。结果表明LLMs评分具有高稳定性、一致的三层排序结构和对性别呈现不敏感，且其相对排序与人类判断一致，但LLMs在极端评分类别上比人类更极端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地被用于执行传统上由人类完成的主观评估，但其作为社会评判者的有效性尚不明确。本文旨在检验LLMs是否能够从基于理论的性格档案中评估社会吸引力，以及其判断是否与人类判断一致。

Method: 本文设计了基于十个心理学和关系构念的三种层级（社交吸引、社交混合、社交不吸引）的人格档案，并通过三项研究进行验证：(1) 34个LLMs对12个档案进行三次评分，检验稳定性、层级排序和一致性；(2) 通过六对匹配的姓名和代词档案以及单独代词测试，检验LLMs对性别呈现的敏感性；(3) 198名人类参与者评估相同的档案，与LLMs评分进行比较。

Result: Study 1发现LLMs评分在次数间高度稳定，呈现一致的三层排序和高度的相对排序一致，但不同模型存在整体评分偏移。Study 2未发现性别呈现的显著影响。Study 3发现人类评分复现了三层结构且与LLMs的相对排序一致，但LLMs在吸引力高和低的极端档案上比人类评分更极端，两组均未表现出显著的性别呈现效应。

Conclusion: LLMs能够在一定程度上有效评估社会吸引力，其相对判断与人类一致，但在绝对评分上存在极端化偏差。LLMs的性别判断不存在明显偏差。这为LLMs作为社会评判者的合理性提供了部分支持，但也提示在使用时需要注意其与人类在评分尺度上的差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to perform subjective evaluations traditionally made by humans, yet their validity as social judges remains unclear. This paper examines whether LLMs can assess social attraction from theory-grounded persona profiles constructed from ten psychological and relational constructs and organized into three tiers: socially attractive, socially mixed, and socially unattractive. We examine LLM ratings in two studies and compare them with human judgments in a third study. In Study 1, 34 LLMs rated 12 profiles across three repeated runs. Although some models tended to give higher or lower ratings overall, they showed strong stability across runs, consistent three-tier ordering, and high agreement in relative profile ordering. Study 2 examined sensitivity to gender presentation using six matched name-and-pronoun profile pairs and a separate pronoun-only test with a gender-neutral name, finding no significant effects in either analysis. In Study 3, 198 human participants evaluated the six matched profiles from Study 2. Their ratings reproduced the three-tier structure and followed a profile ordering consistent with that of the LLMs. However, LLMs rated attractive profiles more positively and unattractive profiles more negatively than humans, while neither group showed a significant overall effect of gender presentation.

</details>


### [208] [REFRAMED: Towards Realistic Audio Description Generation for Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09765)
*Igor Sterner,Mirella Lapata,Alex Lascarides,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出REFRAMED，一个包含2,023个视频、3,302个场景、来自206部电影的高质量音频描述（AD）数据集，并重新定义了AD生成任务为需要模型同时决定"描述什么"和"何时描述"的联合决策问题，不同于以往将AD简化为片段级字幕生成的设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AD生成方法存在三个关键缺陷：1）将AD任务人为设定为片段级字幕生成，描述内容和时间点都预先指定，忽略了AD作为结构化编辑任务（需在对话空隙中插入）的本质；2）依赖噪声较大的转录和对齐流程；3）缺乏包含叙事上下文所需的丰富平行数据。这些问题使得现有方法无法真实反映AD生成的实际挑战。

Method: 作者重新定义了AD生成问题，要求模型联合决策"描述什么"和"何时描述"。为此构建REFRAMED数据集，包含206部电影的2,023个视频、3,302个场景，配有专业AD转录文本（美式和英式双版本）、专业字幕和对齐的剧本。此外还提供人工策划的挑战集，将完整电影与多个AD参考配对，并设计了利用对话空隙和多参考比较的评估协议。

Result: 实验表明，现有的SOTA AD系统和多模态大语言模型虽优于简单基线，但远未达到专家人工表现水平，揭示当前技术在该任务上的显著不足。

Conclusion: REFRAMED数据集和基准测试为视频理解研究建立了新的基础，通过重新定义AD任务并提供高质量数据与评估协议，推动了该领域的深入研究。

Abstract: Audio Description (AD) is a verbal narration of key visual content in videos, enabling access for visually impaired audiences. Unlike standard video captioning, AD is a structured editorial task: descriptions must be inserted into gaps in dialogue and must convey only what is needed to understand the narrative being told. However, existing approaches formulate AD generation in an artificial setting where both the content and timing of descriptions are pre-specified, reducing the task to clip-level captioning. They further rely on noisy transcription and alignment pipelines, and lack the rich parallel data required for modeling narrative context. We introduce a new formulation of AD generation in which models must jointly decide what to describe and when to do it. To support this, we present REFRAMED, a high-quality dataset of 2,023 videos that span 3,302 scenes from 206 movies, with professional AD transcripts (both American and British versions), professional subtitles and aligned screenplays. We also provide a manually curated challenge set that pairs full movies with multiple AD references, together with evaluation protocols that leverage dialogue gaps and multi-reference comparisons. Experiments with state-of-the-art AD systems and multimodal LLMs show that they outperform trivial baselines but fall far short of expert human performance. Our dataset and benchmark establish a new foundation for research on video understanding.

</details>


### [209] [Cultivar: A Contrastive and Locale-Oriented Translation Benchmark for Investigating Contamination and Localisation Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09766)
*Pinzhen Chen,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Xiaoya Xu,David Tan,Doreen Osmelak,Ona de Gibert,Ariun-Erdene Tumurchuluun,Ashok Urlana,Fedor Sizov,Hale Sirin,Jesujoba Alabi,Karrar Talib Abed,Mateusz Klimaszewski,Nikolay Bogoychev,Niyati Bafna,Patricia Schmidtova,Preksha Manjunath Shanbhag,Sherrie Shen,Vilem Zouhar,Vivek Iyer,Yasser Hamidullah,Yusser Al Ghussin,Zheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出源对比评估方法，构建了FLORES的本地化子集Cultivar，用于语言环境特定的翻译评估，并对32个开放权重模型进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言翻译基准通常以英语为源语言翻译成其他语言，以语言对为评估单元，这种设计容易随时间受到数据污染，且忽略了地域和文化因素。因此需要一种考虑源语言环境差异的评估方式。

Method: 提出源对比评估（source-contrastive evaluation）方法，构建Cultivar——FLORES数据集的本地化子集，支持地域特定翻译评估。通过将本地化样本与其未本地化的对应样本配对，利用性能差异来探测数据污染和本地化鲁棒性。

Result: 对32个开放权重模型的基准测试发现：MT专用模型鲁棒性较差，部分模型可能过拟合FLORES数据集，且无论语言如何，模型翻译美国内容的性能普遍优于其他地域内容。

Conclusion: 源对比评估是一种更可靠的多语言翻译评估范式，能够更好地揭示数据污染和本地化能力问题，对翻译模型的公平评估具有重要价值。

Abstract: Multilingual translation benchmarks are typically sourced in English and translated into other languages, treating language pairs as the unit of evaluation---a design that is prone to contamination over time and overlooks locale and cultural considerations. We therefore advocate for source-contrastive evaluation and instantiate it with Cultivar, a localised subset of FLORES, which enables locale-specific translation evaluation. When paired with unlocalised counterparts, performance discrepancy allows the probing of data contamination and localisation robustness. We benchmark 32 open-weight models and find that MT-specialised models are less robust, a few models potentially overfit FLORES, and models tend to translate US content better than that of other locales, regardless of language.

</details>


### [210] [Structured Phonological Representations for Audio-Articulatory rtMRI Speech Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09767)
*Abner Hernandez,Tomás Arias Vergara,Daiqi Liu,Andreas Maier,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了将基于音频训练的语音特征模型PhonoQ的表示应用于音频-发音联合建模，发现其特征能提升发音轮廓对音系类别（方式、部位、清浊、元音特征）的分类性能，并能从音频向发音模型部分迁移音系信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时MRI可以观测说话时的发音运动，但将发音运动模式映射到音系/语音学类别仍然困难。作者希望探索音频模型PhonoQ提供的结构化音系特征表示是否有助于音频-发音联合建模。

Method: 从PhonoQ的Conformer模块提取表示（其训练受方式、部位、清浊和元音特征监督），结合发音轮廓与同步的音频特征，与WavLM-large和HuBERT-large基线对比，在未见语音和未见说话人两种设置下评估。同时测试仅用发音轮廓的推理设置及教师监督迁移。

Result: PhonoQ特征在方式、部位、清浊、元音高度、元音后位性等音系目标以及39音素细粒度分类上均提升macro-F1。在仅发音轮廓推理时，音频教师监督带来适度但一致的增益。后验分析显示可解释的语音表面模式，如/t/闪音化、/t/-/r/后缩或塞擦化、鼻音部位同化。

Conclusion: PhonoQ的结构化音系特征表示对音频-发音联合建模有实用价值，不仅能提升音系类别预测性能，还能实现音系信息从音频到发音模型的部分迁移，并提供可解释的语音学模式。

Abstract: Real-time MRI makes it possible to observe vocal-tract articulation during speech, but mapping these articulatory patterns to phonetic and phonological categories remains challenging. We investigate whether PhonoQ, an audio-based model trained to recognize structured phonological features, provides useful information for audio--articulatory modeling. Specifically, we extract representations from PhonoQ's Conformer module, whose training is shaped by supervision for manner, place, voicing, and vowel features. Using articulatory contours with synchronized audio-derived features, we compare WavLM-large and HuBERT-large baselines with models that incorporate PhonoQ-derived representations. Across unseen-speech and unseen-subject settings, these features improve macro-F1 for phonological targets including manner, place, voicing, vowel height, and vowel backness, and also improve fine-grained 39-phoneme classification. In a contour-only inference setting, audio-derived teacher supervision yields modest but consistent gains over contour-only training, indicating that phonological information from synchronized audio can be partially transferred to articulatory models. Finally, posterior analyses show interpretable surface-sensitive patterns consistent with flapping-like /t/ realizations, /t/-/r/ retraction or affrication, and nasal place assimilation.

</details>


### [211] [PragMatch: Separating Pragmatic Incongruity from Cross-Modal Mismatch in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09772)
*Zhanna Mukhametsharip,Vera Demberg,Varsha Suresh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PragMatch基准（3,000对图文对），通过系统性掩码和注入实验揭示大型视觉语言模型在多模态讽刺检测中依赖词汇、OCR和风格等表面线索（捷径学习），而非进行真正的图文关系推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在基准上表现优异，但尚不清楚其是否真正推理图像与文本之间的关系，还是依赖表面相关性（捷径学习）。在多模态讽刺检测中，成功预测依赖于识别语用不协调而非简单的图文不匹配，因此需要揭示LVLMs在深层语用推理上的真实能力。

Method: 从MMSD2.0构建PragMatch基准，包含3,000对图文对（原始讽刺示例、构造的字面配对和困难负样本）；通过系统性掩码识别有影响的捷径线索，并通过定向注入实验评估这些线索对模型预测的影响。

Result: LVLM预测对词汇、OCR提取和风格线索敏感，注入表面信号会在图文底层关系不变的情况下引起模型预测的显著变化。

Conclusion: 当前LVLMs存在局限性，倾向于依赖表面线索而非进行深层的多模态语用推理；PragMatch可作为评估超越表面对齐的多模态语用推理的系统性测试平台。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong performance on multimodal benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether they genuinely reason about relationships between images and text or rely on superficial correlations, known as shortcut learning. This question is particularly important for multimodal sarcasm detection, where successful prediction depends on recognizing pragmatic incongruity rather than treating sarcasm as simple image-text mismatch. We introduce PragMatch, a controlled benchmark of 3,000 image-text pairs derived from MMSD2.0, including original sarcastic examples and constructed literal and hard-negative pairs. We identify influential shortcut cues through systematic masking and evaluate their impact through targeted injection experiments. Our results show that LVLM predictions are sensitive to lexical, OCR-derived and stylistic cues, with injected surface signals causing substantial changes in model predictions despite unchanged underlying image-text relationships. Our findings reveal limitations in current LVLMs while PragMatch provides a systematic testbed for evaluating multimodal pragmatic reasoning beyond surface-level image-text alignment.

</details>


### [212] [KGCaRe: Explainable Complex Conditional Question Answering using Automatic Knowledge Graph Construction and Context Retrieval with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09779)
*Ghanshyam Verma,Simanta Sarkar,Devishree Pillai,Hotaka Shiokawa,Yourong Xu,Fiona Veazey,Peter Hubbert,Hui Su,Paul Buitelaar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KGCaRe，一种结合神经检索与基于LLM生成的知识图谱（KG）符号推理的混合方法，用于解答领域特定的复杂条件性问题。KGCaRe从文档中构建KG并存入图数据库，同时将文档嵌入向量库实现神经检索，通过LLM引导的迭代图遍历提取相关三元组、剪枝无关信息，必要时利用线索实体再次遍历，最终将路径形式的三元组与语义检索的文本片段共同输入定制提示词生成带解释的答案。在两个复杂条件性QA数据集上的实验表明，KGCaRe在Mistral、Mixtral、GPT-3.5和GPT-4o等多个LLM上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM和RAG在领域特定的复杂条件性问答任务上表现不佳，作者假设通过整合来自文档和知识图谱的结构化与非结构化知识，可以增强RAG的推理能力和答案准确性，从而解决这一挑战。

Method: KGCaRe采用混合架构：(1) 使用多提示提取策略从文档构建KG并存储到图数据库；(2) 同时将文档嵌入向量存储实现神经检索；(3) 执行由LLM引导的迭代图遍历，提取相关三元组并剪枝无关信息，若初次遍历未提供足够上下文，则利用额外线索实体再次遍历；(4) 将KG路径形式的相关三元组与语义检索的文本片段共同输入定制KGCaRe提示词生成带解释的答案。

Result: 在两个复杂条件性QA数据集上，KGCaRe在Mistral、Mixtral、GPT-3.5和GPT-4o等多个LLM上均始终优于Vanilla LLM、Code Prompt、Text Prompt、Think-on-Graph、Vanilla RAG和HybridContextQA等现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 将LLM生成的KG与神经检索结合，通过迭代图遍历和线索实体引导的符号推理，能有效提升复杂条件性问题回答的准确性和推理能力。作者公开了实现该方法的软件流程。

Abstract: Answering complex conditional questions using Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) remains a challenge, particularly in domain-specific contexts where general-purpose LLMs and RAG tend to underperform. We hypothesize that augmenting RAG with unstructured and structured knowledge, extracted from both documents and knowledge graphs (KGs), can improve reasoning and answer accuracy for such tasks.
  To test this, we propose KGCaRe, a hybrid approach that combines neural retrieval with symbolic reasoning over LLM-generated KGs. KGCaRe constructs a KG from documents using a multi-prompt extraction strategy and stores it in a graph database. Simultaneously, the documents are embedded into a vector store to enable neural retrieval. KGCaRe performs innovative iterative graph traversal guided by the LLM to extract relevant triples, prune irrelevant information, and uses additional clue entities to traverse the graph again if the initial traversal does not provide satisfactory context to generate the answer. The relevant triples extracted from the KG in path form, along with semantically retrieved text passages, are then fed into custom KGCaRe prompts to generate answers to the complex conditional questions with explanations.
  We evaluate KGCaRe on two complex conditional QA datasets. Our results on these datasets show that KGCaRe consistently outperforms existing baselines, including Vanilla LLM, Code Prompt, Text Prompt, Think-on-Graph, Vanilla RAG, and HybridContextQA, across multiple LLMs such as Mistral, Mixtral, GPT-3.5, and GPT-4o. We publicly release the software pipeline that we developed to implement the proposed KGCaRe approach.

</details>


### [213] [Comparing British and American Audio Description of Movies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09792)
*Igor Sterner,Alex Lascarides,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了英国与美国音频描述（为盲人和视障人士制作的电影视觉叙事）之间的差异，基于两国撰写指南和实践者的见解提出假设，并在包含206部电影的双语音频描述语料库上进行验证，定量证据支持了所有假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频描述是一种为盲人和视障人士服务的叙事技术，但其受限于对话间隙和地区性规范。此前缺乏对英国和美国音频描述差异的系统性定量研究，理解这些差异对于改进音频描述质量、指导实践和推动跨地区标准制定具有重要意义。

Method: 研究者首先利用英国和美国各自地区的音频描述编写指南，结合该领域从业者的经验，提出关于两地差异的具体假设（涉及词汇、进行体、被动结构、主观形容词和修饰词、人物命名时机、场景提示方式以及与电影对话和音乐重叠程度等）。随后在一个预先构建的语料库上验证，该语料库包含206部电影，每部均有由人类撰写的美式和英式音频描述。

Result: 所有测试假设均获得定量证据支持，包括词汇差异、进行体使用差异、被动结构使用差异、主观形容词和修饰词差异、人物命名时机差异、场景提示方式差异，以及与电影对话和音乐的重叠程度差异。

Conclusion: 该工作为音频描述这一叙事技术提供了一种定量的分析视角，证实了地区性指南对音频描述实践的系统性影响，并展示了跨地区音频描述在语言和叙事层面存在的显著且可量化的差异。

Abstract: Narrating the visual component of movies is known as audio description. It is a narrative technique designed to enable blind and visually impaired individuals to follow the story. However, it is far more constrained than most narratives: the descriptions not only need to convey the story in the movie, but they must also fit into gaps between dialogue and they need to conform to guidelines that exist in each region. In this work, we compare audio description created in the United Kingdom against audio description created in the United States. We use guidelines written for these two regions, alongside the impressions from a practitioner in the field, to motivate specific hypotheses about the differences. We test these hypotheses against our pre-existing corpus, which provides both human-authored American and British audio description for each of 206 movies. Results provide quantitative evidence to uphold all tested hypotheses, including differences in lexicon, the use of the progressive aspect, the use of passive constructions, the use of subjective adjectives and modifiers, when characters are named, how scenes are cued, and degree of overlap with movie dialogue and music. Our work offers a quantitative lens into the narrative technique of audio description.

</details>


### [214] [SWE-Bench ProMax: Benchmarking Agents on Large-Scale Multilingual Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09802)
*Yuling Shi,Jinghan Xu,Kelin Fu,Wenhao Zeng,Shilin He,Lei Zhang,Yue Liu,Zelin Zhao,Terry Yue Zhuo,Jialun Cao,Siyu Ye,Tianyu Liu,Kai Cai,Shing-Chi Cheung,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Bench ProMax，一个专家精选的多语言代码重构基准，包含170个来自七个编程语言真实提交的实例。该基准针对现有基准（如SWE-bench Verified）的测试质量缺陷（约60%的未解实例存在有缺陷的测试）进行了严格的多阶段筛选：重写问题描述以确保规格明确，人工审查测试套件以移除过窄或过宽的测试，并过滤低复杂度或跨文件范围不足的任务。平均每个实例涉及11.4个文件和261.6行代码，远超现有基准规模。实验表明，在最先进的两个agent框架下，最佳模型解出率仅为41.2%，证明了该基准对当前AI编码智能体构成了有意义且未饱和的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体基准（如SWE-bench Verified）正迅速饱和，且审计发现约60%未解实例包含有缺陷的测试（过窄测试拒绝正确解法，过宽测试检查未陈述需求），前沿模型甚至能从训练数据中逐字复现gold patch。代码重构任务需要跨多文件的协调行为保持修改，对智能体能力构成更大、更真实的考验，但现有基准在此方面覆盖不足。

Method: 作者构建了SWE-Bench ProMax基准，包含170个从七个编程语言（Python、Java、TypeScript、Go、C、C++、Rust）真实提交中提取的实例。采用多阶段专家策展流程：（1）从头重写问题描述，提供精确无歧义的规格；（2）人工审查测试套件，移除过窄和过宽的测试；（3）过滤复杂度不足或跨文件范围有限的任务。最终基准平均每个实例涉及11.4个修改文件和261.6行代码。

Result: 在两种agent脚手架下对前沿模型进行实验，最佳模型仅在41.2%的实例上成功解决，远低于现有基准的饱和水平，证实SWE-Bench ProMax为当前AI编码智能体提供了有意义的挑战，且尚未饱和。

Conclusion: SWE-Bench ProMax通过严格的质量控制流程解决了现有基准的测试质量缺陷，提供了一个规模更大、更具挑战性的代码重构基准，可作为衡量AI编码智能体在长周期复杂软件工程任务上真实能力的重要评估工具。

Abstract: As AI coding agents take on increasingly complex, long-horizon software engineering tasks, existing benchmarks are rapidly saturating and their evaluation quality has come under serious scrutiny: a recent audit found that nearly 60% of unsolved SWE-bench Verified instances contain flawed tests -- either overly narrow tests that reject correct solutions or overly broad tests that check unstated requirements -- and that frontier models can verbatim reproduce gold patches from training data. Code refactoring, which requires coordinated, behavior-preserving changes across many files, offers a substantially harder and more realistic test of agent capability, yet remains underserved by current benchmarks. We introduce SWE-Bench ProMax, an expert-curated, multilingual code refactoring benchmark of 170 instances drawn from real commits across seven programming languages (Python, Java, TypeScript, Go, C, C++, and Rust). Every instance undergoes rigorous, multi-stage curation that directly addresses the quality problems identified in prior benchmarks: issue descriptions are rewritten from scratch to provide precise, unambiguous specifications, and test suites are manually reviewed to remove overly narrow and overly broad tests. Tasks with insufficient complexity or limited cross-file scope are filtered out, yielding a benchmark of challenging, large-scale refactoring tasks that average 11.4 modified files and 261.6 lines of code per instance, substantially exceeding the scale of existing benchmarks. Experiments with frontier models under two agent scaffolds show that the best model achieves only 41.2% resolve rate, confirming that SWE-Bench ProMax presents a meaningful and unsaturated challenge for current AI coding agents. Our benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/swe-bench-promax/SWE-Bench-ProMax.

</details>


### [215] [RA-FinBERT: Rule-aware LoRA adaptation for low-resource financial sentiment classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09834)
*Fan Zhang,Jiaming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-FinBERT，一个参数高效的金融情感分析框架，将FinBERT的上下文表示与VADER规则派生的情感信号及来源元数据特征相结合，仅增加1,024个可训练权重即在金融新闻标题和描述的三分类情感任务上显著超越纯文本FinBERT（准确率69.89% vs 63.44%，宏F1 0.634 vs 0.526），中性类召回率从18.18%提升至45.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源高效的金融NLP研究主要集中于压缩或适配预训练语言模型，较少关注将上下文表示与轻量级规则派生特征相结合。作者希望探索规则派生情感信息和源元数据能否为预训练模型的上下文表示提供互补信号，以在有限计算资源下提升金融情感分类性能。

Method: RA-FinBERT采用参数高效框架：使用LoRA对FinBERT进行低秩适配；提取VADER派生的三个连续情感比例（积极、消极、中性）与一个来源级元数据特征，构成标准化四维特征向量；将该特征向量直接与FinBERT最后一层768维[CLS]表示拼接，通过轻量级分类头进行分类，仅引入1,024个额外可训练参数。

Result: 在金融新闻标题和描述的三分类情感基准上，RA-FinBERT达到69.89%准确率和0.634宏F1，显著优于纯文本FinBERT（63.44%准确率、0.526宏F1）和DistilBERT基线；中性类召回率从18.18%提高到45.45%；支持CPU和GPU执行。

Conclusion: 规则派生情感信息与来源元数据能为FinBERT上下文表示提供互补信号，以极小的额外模型复杂度显著提升金融情感分类性能，在计算资源受限场景下提供了轻量实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis converts unstructured financial news into quantitative signals that can support market analysis and decision-making. Existing work on resource-efficient financial NLP has largely focused on compressing or adapting pretrained language models, with less attention to combining contextual representations with lightweight rule-derived features. This study develops Rule-Aware FinBERT (RA-FinBERT), a parameter-efficient framework that integrates low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with three continuous VADER-derived sentiment proportions (positive, negative, and neutral) and a source-level metadata feature. The standardized four-dimensional feature vector is directly concatenated with the 768-dimensional final-layer FinBERT [CLS] representation and passed through a lightweight classification head. This design introduces only 1,024 additional trainable weights relative to a structurally matched text-only FinBERT model. RA-FinBERT was evaluated against text-only FinBERT and a lightweight DistilBERT baseline for three-class sentiment classification of financial-news titles and descriptions. On the held-out test set, RA-FinBERT achieved 69.89% accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.634, compared with 63.44% and 0.526 for text-only FinBERT. Neutral-class recall increased from 18.18% to 45.45%. The framework supports both CPU and GPU execution, offering a lightweight and practical approach to financial sentiment classification under constrained computational resources. These findings indicate that rule-derived sentiment information and source metadata can provide complementary signals to contextual FinBERT representations and improve performance with minimal additional model complexity.

</details>


### [216] [Fusion Training for Mathematical Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09893)
*Congfeng Cao,Pengyu Zhang,Jelke Bloem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统地研究了思维模式融合（TMF）的训练动力学，聚焦于思考模式与非思考模式之间的数据比例和训练调度。通过数学问题求解基准实验，发现两种模式之间存在不对称交互：增加非思考监督数据会降低思考模式精度，且不同训练调度会调节这一权衡，最优调度依赖于数据比例。研究还量化了两者监督之间的负相关关系，揭示了固有张力，为TMF训练设置提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMF使大语言模型能够在单一模型中同时支持简洁回复和长形式推理，但其训练动力学（包括两种模式间的数据比例和训练调度）尚未被充分探索。这种缺失使得实际训练TMF模型时缺乏系统性的指导依据。

Method: 围绕数学问题求解任务，构建了一个包含多种思考到非思考数据比例的基准测试集，并设计了三种训练调度方案。通过系统性地改变数据比例和训练调度，分析二者对TMF模型两种模式性能的影响。

Result: 实验揭示了两种模式间的不对称交互：增加非思考监督数据的比例会导致思考模式精度下降。不同训练调度会调节这种权衡，且最优调度取决于具体的数据比例。此外，通过量化分析发现非思考模式与思考模式的监督之间存在负相关关系，说明两者之间存在内在张力。

Conclusion: 这些发现为设计有效的TMF训练设置提供了实践经验指导，并发布了全部代码和数据（Fusion Bench）以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Thinking Mode Fusion (TMF) enables large language models to support both concise responses and long-form reasoning by unifying a non-thinking mode and a thinking mode within a single model. However, its training dynamics, including the \emph{data ratio} and \emph{training schedule} between the two modes, remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic study of TMF by analyzing the effects of the training schedule and data ratio between thinking and non-thinking modes. Focusing on mathematical problem solving, we construct a benchmark with multiple thinking-to-non-thinking data ratios and three training schedules. Our results reveal an asymmetric interaction between the two modes: increasing the ratio of non-thinking supervision reduces the accuracy of the thinking mode. We further show that different training schedules modulate this trade-off and that the optimal schedule depends on the data ratio. Finally, we quantify a negative correlation between non-thinking and thinking mode supervision, highlighting an inherent tension between these two modes. These findings provide practical guidance for designing effective TMF training settings. All code and data are released to support further research at: \href{https://github.com/caocongfeng/Fusion-Bench.git}{\textbf{Fusion Bench}}.

</details>


### [217] [Consilience for Verifier-Free Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09898)
*Lecheng Kong,Like Hui,Haitao Mao,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有基于置信度的无验证器测试时扩展（VF-TTS）方法在复杂任务上会灾难性失效，因为均匀高置信度往往意味着搜索未充分探索，容易给出自信但错误的答案。作者提出consilience框架，通过显式评估推理过程中置信度的时间不对称性——惩罚初始高置信度、要求最终高确定性——来提升测试时扩展的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展通常依赖外部验证器（如编译器、测试用例或训练好的价值函数），但在许多真实应用中无法获得高质量验证器。无验证器的测试时扩展（VF-TTS）方法通过替代信号评估候选rollout，其中基于置信度的方法因近零开销和最小内部状态访问而尤为有吸引力。然而，本文发现这类方法在复杂任务上存在根本性缺陷：均匀高置信度常常意味着缺乏探索，导致置信但错误的答案，亟需一种更鲁棒的选择机制。

Method: 提出consilience选择框架，核心洞察是鲁棒的认知搜索需要特定的置信度轨迹模式：推理初期应表现为低置信度（表明进行了探索性分支），最终收敛到高置信度的解。该框架通过一个组合度量显式地惩罚初始高置信度，同时严格要求最终确定性，从而实现对推理过程置信度时间不对称性的量化评估。

Result: 在研究生级别的数学问题和开放式代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，consilience一致性地优于现有基线方法，验证了关于完成置信度的新视角的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了基于置信度的VF-TTS方法在复杂任务上的关键局限——高置信度可能恰恰是搜索不足的信号，并提出了一个轻量且有效的consilience框架来纠正这一问题，为无验证器场景下的测试时扩展提供了新的研究方向和实用方法。

Abstract: Test-time scaling often uses an external verifier, such as compilers and test cases in coding or trained value functions in robotics applications, to obtain high-quality rollouts. Verifier-free test-time scaling (or VF-TTS) is gaining extensive attention as a mechanism to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, primarily because we do not have access to such high-quality verifiers in many real-world applications. Among existing VF-TTS methods, confidence-based VF-TTS methods, which compute and rank rollouts solely by confidence, are particularly promising. Such methods introduce near-zero overhead for sample evaluation and require minimal access to internal model states, making the methods highly flexible across models and tasks.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a critical limitation of existing confidence-based VF-TTS methods by showing that such methods catastrophically break down on complex tasks. We observe a very interesting phenomenon: uniformly high confidence frequently indicates a failure to explore, favoring confidently wrong answers. To address this, our core insight is that robust cognitive search requires a specific confidence trajectory pattern: such methods perform exploratory branching at the beginning, as manifested by low initial confidence, and converge to a high final confidence solution. To implement this insight, we introduce consilience, a novel selection framework that explicitly evaluates the temporal asymmetry of confidence in reasoning. We operationalize this via a combinatorial metric that actively penalizes high initial confidence while strictly demanding final certainty. Extensive experiments covering both graduate-level mathematics problems and free-form code generation demonstrate that consilience effectively outperforms existing baselines, validating our novel perspective on completion confidence.

</details>


### [218] [Decoding-Level Taboo: A Diagnostic Stress Test for LLM Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09900)
*Tadanobu Chuyo Kamijo,Ori Rottenstreich,Javier Conde,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Decoding-Level Taboo，一种零提示的诊断性压力测试方法，通过在logit空间动态屏蔽首要候选token，强制模型脱离其最优生成路径进行迂回表达，以揭示基准分数与实际部署性能之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型评估通常只在标称条件下进行，造成了能力幻觉——模型在高度优化的生成走廊上表现良好，但真实部署中复杂系统提示、安全护栏和结构约束会迫使模型偏离标称路径，导致基准分数与部署性能脱节。

Method: Taboo在运行时直接干预logit空间，在词边界处动态屏蔽首要候选token，强制模型进行机器式迂回表达（machine circumlocution），且无需任何提示词即可触发压力测试。

Result: 在多个开放权重模型家族上的评估表明，离路径鲁棒性受参数规模和后训练指令对齐的显著影响，模型规模和对其程度越大，鲁棒性通常越好。

Conclusion: Taboo不仅为模型离路径鲁棒性提供了新的诊断工具，还提供了生成多样化合成数据集、压力测试运行时安全护栏及部署前模型可靠性审计的通用基元。

Abstract: Large language model evaluations typically focus on performance under nominal conditions, creating an illusion of capability where models comfortably walk a narrow, highly optimized generation corridor. In real-world deployments, however, complex system prompts, safety guardrails, and structural constraints continuously force models off this nominal path, driving a divergence between benchmark scores and deployment performance. To address this issue, we introduce Decoding-Level Taboo, a zero-prompt diagnostic stress test that intervenes directly in logit space at runtime, forcing models out of their nominal paths. By dynamically masking primary candidate tokens at word boundaries, Taboo forces machine circumlocution.
  Evaluating Taboo across several open-weight model families reveals that off-path robustness is heavily influenced by both parameter scale and post-training instruction alignment, with robustness generally improving with model size and alignment. Beyond the results presented in this paper, Taboo provides a novel primitive for generating diverse synthetic datasets, stress-testing runtime safety guardrails, and auditing model reliability prior to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [219] [From Values to Benchmarks: Evaluating Large Language Models for Governmental Use in Dutch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09925)
*Laurens Samson,Iva Gornishka,Gossa Lô,Yuki M. Asano,Sennay Ghebreab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Grip on LLMs"框架，一个专为荷兰政府场景设计的大语言模型系统化评估套件，涵盖六大评估维度（事实性、诚实性、社会偏见、能耗、成本和训练数据透明度），对30多个多语言和荷兰语专属模型进行评测。结果显示没有单一模型在所有维度表现优异，质量提升必然伴随更高的环境与财务成本，偏见与质量相对独立，且事实性与诚实性由不同属性决定。论文发布了一个面向非技术用户的公共模型概览工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正日益部署于政府场景，但现有评估框架很少同时反映公共行政的价值取向和非英语语境的语言要求。荷兰政府机构在选用LLM时缺乏系统化、可操作的评估工具，亟需一个兼顾公共部门价值观与荷兰语语言特性的评估体系。

Method: 研究方法包括：与荷兰某大型市政组织的领域专家协作，通过顾问委员会流程、用户研究和公务员聊天机器人用户调查来确定需求；据此识别出六大评估维度（事实性、诚实性、社会偏见、能耗、成本、训练数据透明度）；将这些维度操作化为一个覆盖30多个多语言及荷兰语专属模型的基准测试套件。

Result: 研究发现：没有单一模型能在所有维度上表现最佳，各维度之间存在不可避免的权衡——更高质量的模型总是带来更大的环境影响和财务成本，而偏见水平与质量和成本相对独立；事实性（能否正确回答）与诚实性（是否承认不知道）由不同属性决定，高事实性并不必然意味着高诚实性。

Conclusion: 论文发布了一个公开访问、用户友好的模型概览工具，面向从工程师到政策制定者的各类利益相关方，将研究发现转化为非技术受众可操作的决策依据，为政府部门选择LLM提供系统化支持。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being deployed in governmental settings, yet few existing evaluation frameworks jointly reflect the values of public administration and the linguistic requirements of non-English contexts. We present the "Grip on LLMs" framework, a systematic evaluation suite for Dutch governmental use developed in collaboration with domain experts from a major Dutch municipal organisation. Through an advisory board process, user research, and a survey of the users of a civil-servant chatbot, we identify six evaluation dimensions (factuality, honesty, social bias, energy consumption, cost, and training data transparency) and operationalise them into a benchmark suite covering more than 30 multilingual and Dutch-specific models. Our results reveal that no single model excels across all dimensions, and that trade-offs are unavoidable: higher quality consistently comes at greater environmental impact and financial cost, while bias remains largely independent of both. We further find that factuality (whether a model answers correctly) and honesty (whether a model acknowledges what it does not know) are governed by distinct properties, with high factuality not implying high honesty. To make these findings actionable for non-technical audiences, we release a publicly accessible, user-friendly model overview designed for the full range of stakeholders involved in governmental LLM selection, from engineers to policymakers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [AirFlow: Context Preserving and Multi-Rate State Modeling for Air Quality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09775)
*Fan Yang,Nan Chen,Yijie Dong,Yuchen Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AirFlow是一个面向空气质量预测的污染物感知双流框架，通过统计引导的归一化路由机制为每种污染物选择适配的归一化路径，并结合分层双流状态模型融合多尺度状态传播与可学习响应系数，在多个城市真实数据上以极低的计算开销（0.0483M参数、0.0215G FLOPs）实现了34/36项指标最优，RMSE最高降低11.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空气质量预测方法虽在空间依赖学习和气象协变量建模上有所改进，但所有污染物通道仍共享相同的归一化规则、时序骨干网络和潜在表示，无法有效捕捉不同污染物在周期性和分布漂移上的差异，以及浓度轨迹中多尺度依赖与快速变化的复杂性。

Method: AirFlow包含两个核心模块：(1) 统计引导的归一化路由机制——根据每种污染物的24小时自相关和分布漂移特征选择最优归一化路径；(2) 分层双流状态模型——结合多尺度状态空间传播与可学习响应系数，通过门控双向交叉注意力实现信息交换和表示的自适应融合。该框架直接在站点多变量观测上运行，无需额外图传播或预定义信号分解。

Result: 在多个城市的真实数据实验中，AirFlow在36项指标比较中取得34项最佳性能，相比最先进基线RMSE最高降低11.11%；同时仅需0.0483M参数和0.0215G FLOPs，在保持高预测精度的同时实现极低计算开销。

Conclusion: AirFlow通过污染物感知的归一化路由和分层双流状态建模，有效解决了不同污染物在分布、周期性和变化速率上的异构性问题，在不依赖额外图传播或分解模块的前提下，以极低计算成本实现了卓越的空气质量预测精度。

Abstract: Accurate air quality forecasting is essential for public health and urban environmental management, but remains challenging because pollutant channels differ in periodicity and distribution drift, while their concentration trajectories contain both multi-scale dependencies and rapid changes. Recent methods have improved spatial dependency learning and meteorological covariate modeling. However, pollutant channels are still passed through the same normalization rule and temporal backbone, using a shared latent representation for channel-specific distributions and changes at different rates. To address this limitation, we propose AirFlow, a pollutant-aware dual-stream framework that operates on station multivariate observations without additional graph propagation or predefined signal decomposition. Specifically, AirFlow designs two novel blocks: (1) a statistic-guided normalization routing mechanism that selects a normalization path for each pollutant according to its 24-hour autocorrelation and distribution drift; and (2) a hierarchical dual-stream state model that combines multi-scale state space propagation with learnable response coefficients, where gated bidirectional cross-attention exchanges information and adaptively fuses the resulting representations. Experiments on real-world data from multiple cities show that AirFlow achieves the best performance in 34 of 36 metrics comparisons, with reductions of up to 11.11% root mean square error over the state-of-the-art baseline. AirFlow also requires only 0.0483M parameters and 0.0215G FLOPs, achieving high forecasting accuracy with low computational overhead.

</details>


### [221] [GRACE: LLM-Grounded Semantic Metric Spaces for Scalable Mixed-Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07881)
*Zihua Yang,Zhencheng Xie,Junyang Chen,Liang Xie,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRACE是一个基于LLM的混合型表格数据聚类框架。它通过多视角LLM查询策略在属性-值级别获取语义描述，一次性（one-shot）将异构属性映射到统一语义空间，同时用数据内部统计证据交叉验证外部语义以确保与数据集特定聚类结构一致，在不牺牲可扩展性的前提下实现了优越的聚类精度和概念可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合型表格数据的聚类需要统一的度量空间来弥合连续数值和离散分类符号之间的异构性。传统算法仅依赖数据内部统计来估计类别关系，忽略概念上明显但统计上未观察到的关联。虽然LLM提供外部世界知识，但将其文本中心推理应用于抽象表格概念面临挑战，且将LLM嵌入迭代度量学习循环会产生难以承受的计算开销，迫使在语义丰富性和可扩展性之间妥协。

Method: GRACE将语义获取转移到属性-值级别，采用多视角LLM查询策略将异构值映射为知识丰富的描述。关键设计是一步式的语义基础化，提取通用的语义表示，将异构属性嵌入统一空间，将昂贵的LLM调用与迭代优化解耦。同时通过数据内部统计证据交叉验证外部语义，确保与数据集特定的聚类结构对齐。

Result: GRACE在11个竞争方法上实现了更优的聚类精度和概念可解释性，同时保持了与基于统计的传统基线相当的可扩展性。

Conclusion: GRACE证明了通过在属性-值级别进行一次性LLM语义基础化，并将其与数据内部统计证据相结合，可以同时实现语义丰富性和可扩展性，解决了混合数据聚类中长期存在的语义获取与计算成本之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Clustering mixed tabular data requires a unified metric space to bridge the inherent heterogeneity between continuous numerical measurements and discrete categorical symbols. Traditionally, algorithms rely entirely on dataset-internal statistics to estimate categorical relationships, which confines the learned metric to empirical co-occurrences and ignores conceptually obvious yet statistically unobserved affinities. Although LLMs offer external world knowledge, applying their text-centric reasoning to highly abstract tabular concepts presents significant challenges. Bridging this modality gap to construct a semantically complete metric typically requires embedding LLMs into iterative metric learning loops to dynamically optimize cross-modality representations. This incurs intractable computational overhead, forcing a compromise between semantic enrichment and scalability. Therefore, we propose GRACE, an LLM-grounded framework for scalable mixed-data clustering. GRACE shifts semantic acquisition to the attribute-value level via a multi-perspective LLM querying strategy, mapping heterogeneous values into knowledge-informed descriptions. Crucially, this one-shot grounding extracts general-purpose semantic representations that embed heterogeneous attributes into a unified space, decoupling expensive LLM invocation from iterative optimization. Furthermore, GRACE cross-validates these external semantics against dataset-internal statistical evidence to ensure alignment with the dataset-specific cluster structure. Ultimately, GRACE matches the scalability of conventional statistics-driven baselines while achieving superior clustering accuracy and conceptual interpretability over 11 competing methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/develop-yang/GRACE-GRACE-A

</details>


### [222] [The Knowing-Saying Gap: When Probes See Errors that Confidence Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07528)
*Jyotin Goel,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 线性探针能以接近完美的准确率检测语言模型中的上下文损坏，但无法可靠预测最终答案的正确性，形成"知道但不言说"的分离现象。该模式在多种模型家族（含推理模型）中普遍存在；探针干预作为实时监控手段存在严重的模型和错误类型依赖性，没有单一干预策略占优，可部署方案需采用模型感知、错误类型感知的路由策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针虽能高精度检测上下文损坏，但检测能力与最终答案正确性预测之间存在解离（dissociation），直接影响部署监控的可靠性。作者试图验证"持久性胜过峰值"的预注册假设，探索探针能否作为更可靠的失败预测信号。

Method: 在多跳算术链任务上，使用线性探针检测上下文损坏；对比探针检测与最终答案正确性的关联性；测试结构化置信度格式下的模型行为；分析探针在多跳间的持久性；并评估三种干预策略（branch-and-pick、reprompt、replace-prior）在不同模型家族上的效果。

Result: 探针检测上下文损坏的准确率接近完美，但对答案正确性无预测力；模型在结构化置信度格式下坍缩为两个值且错误率无差异；探针持久性不能区分正确与错误结果，拒绝了"持久性胜过峰值"假设；branch-and-pick在跨模型中净收益为正且在Llama-3.1-8B上唯一不破坏正确轨迹（4个挽救，0个破坏），而reprompt和replace-prior破坏正确轨迹的比例与挽救错误轨迹的比例大致相当。

Conclusion: 探针监测是口头化置信度（verbalised confidence）的必要补充，但不存在任何单一干预策略在所有条件下占优；可部署的解决方案是结合模型特性和错误类型的自适应路由策略。

Abstract: Linear probes detect corrupted context in language models with near-perfect accuracy, yet this does not translate into reliable failure prediction. The result is a dissociation with direct implications for deployment monitoring. Across multi-hop arithmetic chains, probes that detect corruption turn out to be uninformative about final answer correctness; models forced into structured confidence formats collapse to two values with indistinguishable error rates; and probe persistence across hops fails to separate correct from incorrect outcomes, refuting our pre-registered "persistence beats peak" hypothesis. This pattern of knowing but not saying generalises across model families including reasoning models. As a real-time monitor, probe-based interventions are sharply model and error-type dependent: branch-and-pick is net-positive across models and uniquely non-breaking on Llama-3.1-8B (4 rescued, 0 broken), while reprompt and replace-prior break correct traces at roughly the rate they rescue wrong ones. Probe-based monitoring is a necessary complement to verbalised confidence, but no single intervention dominates, and the deployable answer is model-aware, error-type-aware routing.

</details>


### [223] [NL2SHACL-Bench: A Benchmark Suite for Natural Language to SHACL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07530)
*Yuchen Zhou,Niels Bobet,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NL2SHACL-Bench，一个用于评估自然语言到SHACL翻译的基准测试套件，并基于该基准评估了四种先进的LLM，发现它们能生成语法有效的SHACL但在复杂逻辑和结构模式的语义等价约束生成上仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHACL是验证RDF知识图谱一致性的核心技术，但编写SHACL形状需要领域专家通常不具备的技术专业知识。将自然语言需求翻译为SHACL（NL2SHACL）可以降低这一门槛，但目前缺少专门的基准测试来评估该任务，且评估生成形状不能仅依靠字符串比较，因为语义等价的形状可能在序列化和结构上不同。

Method: 作者构建了NL2SHACL-Bench基准套件，用于自然语言到SHACL翻译任务。该基准支持超越字符串比较的语义等价评估方法。作者使用该基准评估了四种最先进的LLM在NL2SHACL任务上的表现。

Result: 当前LLM非常擅长生成语法有效的SHACL，但在生成复杂逻辑和结构模式的语义等价约束方面仍然存在困难。这表明NL2SHACL-Bench为衡量NL2SHACL技术进展提供了有意义的基础。

Conclusion: NL2SHACL-Bench填补了NL2SHACL领域缺少专门基准的空白，为评估和推动NL2SHACL研究提供了有价值的工具；当前LLM在该任务上仍有提升空间，尤其是在复杂语义等价约束的生成上。

Abstract: SHACL is a core technology for validating the conformance of RDF knowledge graphs (KGs). Yet, authoring SHACL shapes requires technical expertise that most domain experts lack. Translating natural language requirements into SHACL (NL2SHACL) would lower this barrier. However, there is no dedicated benchmark for NL2SHACL, and evaluating generated shapes requires methods beyond string comparison, as semantically equivalent shapes can differ in serialisation and structure. To tackle these challenges, we present NL2SHACL-Bench, a benchmark suite for natural language to SHACL translation. Using NL2SHACL-Bench, we evaluate four state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) for this task. Our results show that current LLMs are highly capable of generating syntactically valid SHACL, but still struggle to produce semantically equivalent constraints for complex logical and structural patterns. This indicates that NL2SHACL-Bench provides a meaningful basis for measuring advances in the NL2SHACL state of the art.

</details>


### [224] [From Single Chatbots to Governed Agent Ecosystems: An Agentic AI Pattern Catalogue and Orchestration Framework for Mission-Critical Hospital Information Management Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07627)
*Manideep Dhar,Ritwik Singh,Sharat Chandra Kumar Manikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向医疗信息管理系统（HIMS）的合规优先的Agentic AI模式目录与编排框架，通过角色分类学、风险分层模型和统一编排运行时，旨在将碎片化的AI试点转化为受治理的自主智能体生态系统，同时满足HIPAA、GDPR、EU AI Act等多法规合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院在将AI嵌入分诊管理、文档记录、排程和收入周期等工作流时，大多数部署仍是碎片化试点，停滞在生产边缘，导致运营脆弱性、不受管控的风险和不断增加的技术债务。同时全球AI医疗市场预计到2034年将超过近1万亿美元，架构失误和扩展策略失败的财务后果被放大。

Method: 提出一个合规优先的Agentic AI模式目录和编排框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) Agentic角色分类学；(ii) 形式化风险分层模型，将每个模式映射到风险层级、人在环检查点和治理挂钩；(iii) 统一编排运行时，协调跨Epic、Cerner、MEDITECH等EHR/HIMS的多智能体工作流。技术上结合vLLM推理、优化分页内存、机密计算和基于MCP的本地部署，执行端到端加密和策略即代码控制。

Result: 所提出的架构能够减少文档记录时间、集成工作量和AI试点流失率，同时加强治理和审计能力，为医院领导者和监管机构提供将AI投资转化为可持续临床、运营和财务ROI的蓝图。

Conclusion: 该框架为医疗AI从碎片化试点向受治理的生产级智能体生态系统过渡提供了实用路径，将合规性作为架构设计的核心而非事后补充，帮助机构将AI投资转化为可持续的临床、运营和财务回报。

Abstract: Hospitals are racing to embed AI, while coping with the surge in adaptation of the technology in other industries, into the triage management, documentation, scheduling, and revenue-cycle workflows, yet most deployments remain as fragmented pilots that stall at the edge of production, exposing patients and institutions to operational fragility, ungoverned risk, and mounting technical debt. At the same time, the global AI-in-healthcare market is projected to exceed nearly USD 1 trillion by 2034, according to the report of Fortune Business Insights, amplifying the financial consequences of architectural missteps and failed scaling strategies. This research proposes a compliance-first Agentic AI pattern catalogue and orchestration framework, purposely built for HIMS, moving beyond the single LLM chatbots and towards a governed ecosystem of autonomous and semi-autonomous agents. The framework extends by adding (i) a taxonomy of Agentic roles, (ii) a formal risk-stratification model that maps each pattern to risk tiers, human-in-the-loop checkpoints, and governance hooks, and (iii) a unified orchestration runtime capable of coordinating multi-agent workflows across EHR/HIMS landscapes such as Epic, Cerner, and MEDITECH. Technically the framework combines vLLM-based inference, optimized paging memory, confidential computing, and MCP based on-premise deployment, enforcing end-to-end encryption and policy-as-code controls aligned with HIPAA, GDPR, the EU AI Act, India's DPDP and DISHA Acts, ISO 27001, ISO 27002, ISO 14971 and IEC 62304. We exhibit how the proposed architecture is capable and efficient to reduce the documentation time, integration effort, and AI pilot attrition while constricting the governance and auditability, offering hospital leaders and governing authorities an urgently needed blueprint to convert AI investment into sustainable clinical, operational, and financial ROI

</details>


### [225] [IntelliAudit: Using Large Language Models to Evaluate Audit Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07688)
*Allison Wilson,Sina Moradi Sabet,Diar Shakimov,Panteha Shahrivar,Mohammad Reza Bagheri,Dean Konenkamp,Mohammad A. Tayebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntelliAudit，一个基于检索的多智能体系统，用于IT审计证据评估。系统能检索相关证据、生成基于证据的评估、挑战不利发现、裁决分歧并生成面向审计师的建议。在ISO/IEC 27001上的评估表明其能支持控制解读、证据推理和审计准备工作流，但强调需要人工监督来校准充分性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT审计要求审计师判断异构组织证据是否满足语义安全和合规控制，这一判断难以自动化：相关证据分散在政策、记录、电子表格和操作工件中，且审计结论依赖证据充分性而非关键词匹配。

Method: 提出IntelliAudit检索增强多智能体系统，工作流程为：给定控制项和证据语料库后，检索相关工件、生成基于证据的评估、挑战不利发现、裁决分歧，并产生面向审计师的建议（含引用证据、理由、缺失证据分析和补救指引）。系统在ISO/IEC 27001上实例化，并通过专家审计师评审和审计准备用户反馈在多个模拟组织上评估。

Result: 评估表明IntelliAudit能支持控制解释、证据推理和审计准备工作流，同时揭示人工监督在校准充分性判断和纠正过度宽容建议方面的重要性。

Conclusion: 检索增强多智能体系统可以辅助审计证据审查，但应作为决策支持工具而非自主认证系统。

Abstract: IT audits require auditors to judge whether heterogeneous organizational evidence satisfies semantic security and compliance controls. This judgment is difficult to automate because relevant evidence is distributed across policies, records, spreadsheets, and operational artifacts, and because audit conclusions depend on evidentiary sufficiency rather than keyword matching. We present IntelliAudit, a retrieval-grounded multi-agent system for IT audit evidence evaluation. Given a control and an evidence corpus, IntelliAudit retrieves relevant artifacts, generates an evidence-grounded assessment, challenges adverse findings, adjudicates disagreements, and produces an auditor-facing recommendation with cited evidence, rationale, missing-evidence analysis, and remediation guidance. We instantiate IntelliAudit on ISO/IEC 27001 and evaluate it across multiple simulated organizations using expert auditor review and audit-readiness user feedback. The evaluation shows that IntelliAudit can support control interpretation, evidence-grounded reasoning, and audit-preparation workflows, while also revealing the importance of human oversight for calibrating sufficiency judgments and correcting overly permissive recommendations. These results suggest that retrieval-grounded multi-agent systems can assist audit evidence review, but should remain decision-support tools rather than autonomous certification systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [EBGT: Epistemology-aided Bayesian Game Theory for Uplink Power Control in Stochastically Distributed IoT Tiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07745)
*Nirmal D. Wickramasinghe,Indrakshi Dey,Dirk Pesch,John Dooley*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出EBGT框架，一种基于认识论辅助的贝叶斯博弈方法，用于随机分布式IoT网络中分散式上行功率最小化。设备通过两层信念层级推理对手行为，无需重复节点间反馈即可达到发射功率均衡，在保持目标覆盖率的同时降低发射功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集异质IoT网络中的上行功率控制受限于不完整的信道状态信息（CSI）和相互干扰；低SWaP（尺寸、重量、功耗）设备无法承担传统分布式方案的反馈与计算开销。因此需要一种去中心化的、无需重复反馈的功率控制方案。

Method: 利用Poisson点过程（PPP）将干扰用户建模为空间随机分布；设计两层信念层级（关于对手的层间认识信念和关于自身的层内自我评估）使各设备推理竞争对手行为；通过随机几何推导出闭式形式的覆盖率收益函数；使用Jensen-Shannon散度（JSD）量化信念向均衡的收敛过程。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真验证了分析覆盖率表达式的准确性，并表明EBGT在维持目标覆盖率的同时，相比固定功率控制（FPC）和随机非合作功率控制（SNCPC）基线降低了发射功率，尤其在严格SINR和高密度场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: EBGT为密集IoT网络提供了一种无需重复节点间反馈的去中心化功率控制方法，通过贝叶斯博弈与信念层级机制在降低发射功率的同时保持覆盖率性能，适用于SWaP受限设备的场景。

Abstract: Uplink power control in dense, heterogeneous Internet-of-Things (IoT) tiers is fundamentally limited by incomplete channel-state information (CSI) and mutual interference, while low size, weight, and power (SWaP) devices cannot afford the feedback and computation of conventional distributed schemes. This paper proposes EBGT, an epistemology-aided Bayesian game-theoretic framework for decentralized uplink power minimization in stochastically distributed IoT networks. Interfering users are modeled as spatially random through a Poisson point process (PPP), and each device reasons about its rivals through a two-layer belief hierarchy of inter-epistemic beliefs about opponents and intra-epistemic self-assessment, so that the transmit-power equilibrium is reached without repeated inter-node feedback. We derive a closed-form coverage-probability payoff via stochastic geometry and quantify belief convergence toward equilibrium using the Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) of the resulting power distributions. Monte-Carlo simulations validate the analytical coverage expressions and show that EBGT sustains the target coverage probability while reducing transmit power relative to fixed power control (FPC) and stochastic non-cooperative power control (SNCPC) baselines, particularly under stringent SINR and high-density regimes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Myelin Distribution at the Optic Nerve Myelination Transition Zone Influences Axonal Biomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07831)
*Tingting Liu,Xiaofei Wang,C. Ross Ethier,Martin Buist,Tin Aung,Michael J. A. Girard*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究通过多尺度有限元（FE）建模，系统地分析了巩膜筛板（LC）中髓鞘转化区（MTZ）的几何构型对视网膜神经节细胞（RGC）轴突在眼压（IOP）加载下机械应力和应变的影响。结果显示，髓鞘边界处存在显著的力学不连续性，且未髓鞘区域比髓鞘区域承受更高的有效应力和应变，该不连续性随MTZ后移偏移量的增大而加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巩膜筛板被认为是青光眼性RGC损伤的初始部位，也是无髓鞘RGC轴突转变为有髓鞘的关键区域。此前尚无研究系统探讨MTZ构型如何影响RGC轴突的机械负荷，理解这一机制对于阐明青光眼轴突损伤的病理生理过程至关重要。

Method: 采用多尺度有限元框架：1）基于解剖结构的宏观FE眼模型计算15和45 mmHg眼压下的LC变形；2）将宏观变形施加到由轴突、髓鞘和周围基质组成的后部LC微观模型中；3）模拟四种MTZ边界构型（一个平坦型和三种随机后移3、6、9 μm的类型），量化不同构型下IOP诱导的轴突有效应变和应力。

Result: 在眼压加载下，轴突表现为纵向压缩和横向拉伸，在髓鞘边界处出现明显的有效应力和应变不连续性。所有模型中，未髓鞘区域的有效应力和应变均高于髓鞘区域，且这种力学不连续性随MTZ后移偏移量的增加而增大。

Conclusion: MTZ构型直接影响RGC轴突的力学特性。研究提示青光眼相关脱髓鞘可能先于RNFL变薄，但不同MTZ轮廓能否触发青光眼损伤、脱髓鞘是否会加速疾病进展，或两种机制是否共同参与，仍有待进一步研究明确。

Abstract: Purpose: The lamina cribrosa (LC) is considered the initial site of glaucomatous retinal ganglion cell (RGC) injury, and is also the region where unmyelinated RGC axons become myelinated. Here we sought to use finite element (FE) modeling to investigate how the configuration of the myelination transition zone (MTZ) influences the mechanical insult to RGC axons. Methods: A multiscale FE framework was developed to investigate the biomechanical effect of myelin distribution on IOP-induced axonal stress and strain at the MTZ. An anatomically based macro-scale FE eye model was used to compute LC deformations under 15 and 45 mmHg IOP. These deformations were then applied to micro-scale models of the posterior LC, consisting of axons, myelin sheaths, and surrounding matrix. Four distinct MTZ boundary configurations were simulated: one flat and three with random posterior offsets of 3, 6, or 9 μm, representing potential physiological variations. IOP-induced effective axonal strains and stresses were quantified across the different MTZ configurations. Results: Under IOP loading, axons exhibited longitudinal compression and transverse stretch, with marked effective stress and strain discontinuities at the myelin boundary. Across all models, the unmyelinated region exhibited higher effective stress and strain than the myelinated region, and this mechanical discontinuity increased with larger MTZ offsets. Conclusions: Glaucoma-associated demyelination has been previously suggested to precede RNFL thinning. Here we have shown that the MTZ configuration directly influences RGC axonal mechanics. Whether different MTZ profiles can initiate glaucomatous injury, whether demyelination accelerates disease progression, or whether both mechanisms contribute, remains to be determined.

</details>


### [228] [Tools to Explain Neural Networks for Power System Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08048)
*Petros Ellinas,Johanna Vorwerk,Spyros Chatzivasileiadis*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文首次在电力系统文献中提出利用神经正切核（NTK）方法解析电力系统动力学机器学习代理模型的训练性能，将物理刚性和时间尺度分离与神经网络优化刚度联系起来，并据此开发自适应损失加权策略以改善训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统仿真因变流器接口资源和快速控制回路面临刚性和多时间尺度动力学挑战，机器学习代理模型虽被寄予厚望以加速动态仿真，但其训练性能难以解释，限制了实际采用。缺乏分析工具来理解训练过程中的误差模式与收敛行为。

Method: 基于电力系统小信号特征值分析，引入神经正切核（NTK）方法对神经网络训练进行模态解释，识别快速衰减与缓慢收敛的误差模式。在此基础上，提出自适应损失加权策略，并分析结构感知神经架构（如ActNet）优于普通NN的内在原因，在物理信息机器学习代理模型（同步电机和电力电子变换器）上进行评估。

Result: 建立了电力系统物理刚度与时间尺度分离同NN训练优化刚度之间的映射关系，验证了NTK模态分析能够解释训练动态和失败模式；开发的自适应损失加权策略有效改善了训练性能，并解释了ActNet等结构感知架构的优势机制。

Conclusion: 本文提供的分析工具使机器学习代理模型的设计从试错式开发转向系统化的物理感知设计，能够揭示训练动力学和失败模式，支持更可靠的设计决策，增强工程应用中机器学习代理模型的可信度。

Abstract: This paper presents, for the first time in power systems literature to our knowledge, analytical tools to explain the training performance of machine learning surrogate models for power system dynamics. Power system simulations are increasingly challenged by stiff and multi-timescale dynamics arising from converter-interfaced resources and fast control loops. Machine learning surrogates emerge as promising tools to handle this complexity and accelerate dynamic simulations. However, their performance remains difficult to interpret, which limits their adoption. Building on the small-signal eigenvalue analysis in power systems, this paper uses the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) method. NTK delivers a modal interpretation of the learning performance, identifying error modes that decay rapidly versus others that converge slowly. This connection explains how physical stiffness and timescale separation in power system dynamic models appear as optimization stiffness during Neural Network (NN) training. Based on this analysis, we develop adaptive loss-weighting strategies to improve and explain why structure-aware neural architectures, such as ActNet, perform better than vanilla NNs. We assess the proposed approach on physics-informed machine learning surrogate models of \acp{SM} and power electronic converters. The methods introduced in this paper can deliver the necessary analytical tools to interpret and improve the performance of machine learning surrogates, paving the way for the systematic, physics-aware design of NN architectures and training strategies. By moving beyond trial-and-error development, these tools reveal training dynamics and failure modes, support more reliable design decisions, and strengthen confidence in machine-learning surrogates for engineering applications.

</details>


### [229] [Test-Time Scaling for CAD Generation via Verifier-Free Consensus Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09706)
*Aaron Haag,Altay Kaçan,Bertram Fuchs,Oliver Lohse*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需验证器的3D CAD共识选择方法：从自然语言描述生成多个参数化CAD程序后，编译为3D模型，选择与候选池中其他候选一致性最高的样本作为最终输出。该方法无需训练、兼容现有CAD智能体，在多个几何与拓扑指标上优于现有验证器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到CAD生成任务中，单次采样往往不可靠，增加测试时计算通过多次采样来提升效果的前提是能够从候选池中选出好的样本。然而生成时没有ground-truth模型可用，现有系统通常依赖额外的验证器（如视觉-语言评判模型）来挑选候选。作者希望探究是否可以通过候选池本身提供的信息实现无需验证器的有效选择，从而简化系统并降低成本。

Method: 提出共识选择（consensus selection）方法：采样N个参数化CAD程序，编译为3D模型，然后基于候选池内的成对一致性（agreement）分数，选择与其余候选最一致的样本作为最终结果。该方法完全无需训练，可直接集成到现有CAD智能体中。论文探讨了两种一致性的定义：几何一致性（基于几何度量）和拓扑一致性（基于拓扑度量），每种一致性分别优化对应的评估指标。

Result: 在SOTA CAD生成方法的精确候选池上，几何共识改进了该方法验证器的所有三项几何指标，拓扑共识在拓扑指标上与验证器持平。在测试的所有LLM和提示变体上，几何共识相比随机选择均提升了几何精度，将Chamfer距离降低了1-10%。

Conclusion: 候选池本身提供了足够的信号用于有效选择，共识选择是一种训练免费、广泛兼容的候选选择方案，可替代或媲美依赖额外验证器的现有方法，在几何指标上一致优于随机选择和SOTA方法的验证器。

Abstract: Large language models can write parametric CAD programs from a natural-language description (text-to-CAD generation), but a single sample is often wrong. Increasing test-time compute by sampling multiple candidates only helps if a good candidate can be identified, yet no ground-truth model is available at generation time. Existing systems often require a separate verifier, such as a vision-language judge, to select among candidates. We investigate whether the candidate pool itself provides enough signal for effective selection and a verifier-free alternative. We introduce 3D CAD consensus selection, hereafter consensus selection: sample $N$ parametric CAD programs, compile them to 3D models, and return the candidate that agrees most with the rest of the pool. The method is training-free and compatible with existing CAD agents. We investigate geometric and topological notions of agreement, each of which improves its corresponding evaluation metric.
