Then, after establishing embedding between metaplectic Barron spaces and Sobolev spaces we consider a neural metaplectic dictionary and we prove Monte-Carlo approximation bounds for metaplectic Barron functions using finite linear combinations of atoms of the dictionary. Finally, we validate the introduction of the neural metaplectic dictionary by devising a deep neural network architecture that uses as building blocks the atoms of the dictionary. We test it to approximate solutions of time-dependent Schrödinger equations, demonstrating better performance compared to classical phyisics informed neural networks architectures.

</details>


### [332] [DistillCache: KL-Guided Adaptive KV-Cache Eviction for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08878)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DistillCache，一个基于强化学习的KV缓存驱逐框架，将KV缓存驱逐建模为序贯决策问题，通过轻量策略网络利用模型内部信号（注意力统计量、值范数、熵、位置）和REINFORCE算法训练，以每步KL散度奖励保持全缓存输出分布。在Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3上，25%缓存预算下保留94.2%的LongBench全缓存准确率，优于H₂O、SnapKV等启发式方法以及ForesightKV、RLKV等基于RL的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长造成严重内存瓶颈。现有启发式驱逐方法（如H₂O、SnapKV）依赖静态注意力或位置信号，无法捕捉token对未来的预测影响力，因此需要更智能、分布感知的缓存驱逐策略。

Method: 将KV缓存驱逐建模为序贯决策问题（MDP），学习一个轻量策略网络，输入为丰富的模型内部信号（注意力统计量、值范数、熵和位置），使用REINFORCE算法训练，奖励函数为每步KL散度，以最小化驱逐后与全缓存的输出分布差异。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3上：25%缓存预算下LongBench保留94.2%全缓存准确率；相比H₂O和SnapKV最高提升2.7个绝对点；相比ForesightKV和RLKV等RL方法最高提升1.4点；推理基准上与最佳方法持平并在激进压缩下超越；最高实现2.1倍全缓存吞吐量。

Conclusion: 学习驱动的、分布感知的缓存驱逐策略在长上下文LLM推理中兼具高准确率和高效率，证明了基于RL的KV缓存管理比静态启发式方法更有效。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks, but their Key-Value (KV) cache grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck for long-context inference. Existing heuristic eviction methods (e.g., H$_2$O and SnapKV) rely on static attention or positional signals that often fail to capture a token's future predictive influence. We propose DistillCache, a reinforcement learning framework that formulates KV-cache eviction as a sequential decision problem. DistillCache learns a lightweight policy network using rich internal model signals (attention statistics, value norms, entropy, and position) and trains it with REINFORCE via a per-step KL-divergence reward to preserve the full-cache output distribution. On a 7B-parameter instruction-tuned Transformer (Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3), DistillCache retains 94.2% of full-cache accuracy on LongBench at a 25% cache budget, outperforming both strong heuristic baselines (H$_2$O, SnapKV) by up to 2.7 absolute points and, under our re-implementations, concurrent RL-based methods (ForesightKV, RLKV) by up to 1.4 points on long-context tasks. On reasoning benchmarks, DistillCache is competitive with the best concurrent method and surpasses it under aggressive compression. It also delivers up to 2.1x full-cache throughput while maintaining competitive practical efficiency. These results highlight the effectiveness of learned, distribution-aware policies for memory-efficient long-context LLM inference.

</details>


### [333] [Federated Attention Autoencoders with a Stochastic Aggregation Scheme for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08906)
*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种针对基于注意力机制的自编码器网络的聚合函数，用于联邦学习场景下的异常检测，在KDDCUP10数据集上相比传统自编码器F1分数提升2.9%、AUC ROC提升5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化数据环境中进行异常检测具有挑战性，尤其是数据无法共享的场景。虽然已有基于自编码器的联邦异常检测方法，注意力机制的引入能提升自编码器效率，但基于注意力机制的模型在联邦学习中因缺乏合适的聚合函数而发展不足。

Method: 提出两种新颖的、专门针对基于注意力机制的自编码器设计的聚合函数，这些聚合函数能够更好地保留网络中记忆模块内存储的学习信息。

Result: 在KDDCUP10数据集上验证，与传统自编码器相比，F1分数最多提升2.9%，AUC ROC最多提升5.1%。

Conclusion: 所提出的针对注意力自编码器的聚合函数能够有效提升联邦异常检测的性能，为注意力模型在联邦学习中的应用提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Outlier detection in decentralized data environments is a challenging task for many machine learning implementations, particularly in settings where data cannot be shared. Recently, there have been advances in federated outlier detection, some of which are based on the use of autoencoder networks. The introduction of attention mechanisms to autoencoders boosts their efficiency. However, the application of attention-based models in federated learning remains underdeveloped due to the absence of proper aggregation functions for these types of networks. In our work, we propose two novel aggregation functions tailored for attention-based autoencoders, which better preserve the learned information stored within the memory modules of these networks. We evaluated our approach on the KDDCUP10 dataset, and we showed that the proposed methods achieve up to 2.9\% and 5.1\% better results for F1 score and AUC ROC respectively when compared to traditional autoencoders.

</details>


### [334] [A Domain-Structured Ensemble Framework for Perioperative Outcome Prediction Using Electronic Health Record Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08920)
*Shikhar Shukla,Cristina Barboi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于域结构集成框架的围手术期风险预测模型，将预测变量划分为患者、手术和麻醉相关三个领域，通过逻辑回归元学习器整合各领域梯度提升模型的独立风险估计。在包含5,386例手术事件的术后谵妄（POD）案例对照研究中，该框架取得了AUROC 0.899、Brier得分0.126的优异性能，优于最佳单阶段模型（AUROC 0.849），且校准性良好、决策曲线分析显示临床净获益，为可解释、校准感知的围手术期临床决策支持提供了模块化、可扩展的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有围手术期风险预测模型受限于狭窄的手术人群、不完整的术中数据、校准不佳以及可解释性有限等问题，亟需一种能够整合多维数据且性能稳健的预测框架。

Method: 采用域结构化集成框架：（1）将预测变量划分为患者相关、手术相关和麻醉相关三个领域；（2）每个领域独立训练梯度提升模型生成风险估计；（3）通过逻辑回归元学习器整合各领域输出。使用5,386例手术事件（2,693例POD病例，2,693例对照）进行案例对照验证，并采用时间验证（2017年后数据）和领域消融实验评估模型。

Result: 堆叠元学习器取得AUROC 0.899（95% CI: 0.891-0.906），PR-AUC 0.881，Brier得分0.126；优于最佳单阶段模型（AUROC 0.849）。领域消融显示较手术单一模型（AUROC 0.879, Brier 0.140）有改进。时间验证AUROC达0.915。校准优秀（截距-0.006，斜率1.035）。决策曲线分析在临床合理阈值内显示正净获益。

Conclusion: 该模块化域结构化框架支持替代结局、扩展预测领域及动态风险更新，为可解释、校准感知的围手术期临床决策支持提供了可扩展的基础，具有良好的泛化性和临床实用性。

Abstract: Perioperative risk prediction models are often limited by narrow surgical populations, incomplete intraoperative data, poor calibration, and limited interpretability. We present a domain-structured ensemble framework for perioperative outcome prediction using routinely collected electronic health record (EHR) data. Predictors are organized into patient-related, surgery-related, and anesthetics-related domains. Domain-specific gradient boosting models generate independent risk estimates that are integrated through a logistic regression meta-learner. We demonstrate the framework using postoperative delirium (POD) in a case-control sample of 5,386 surgical encounters (2,693 cases, 2,693 controls) from a statewide health information exchange. POD required both delirium-related ICD codes and a positive Confusion Assessment Method screening within seven postoperative days; patients with preexisting dementia were excluded. The stacked meta-learner achieved AUROC 0.899 (95% CI: 0.891-0.906), precision-recall AUC 0.881, and Brier score 0.126, compared with AUROC 0.849 for the best single-stage model. Domain ablation showed improved discrimination and calibration over a surgery-only model (AUROC 0.879, Brier 0.140). Temporal validation on held-out post-2017 data yielded AUROC 0.915. Calibration was excellent, with intercept -0.006 (95% CI: -0.083 to 0.070) and slope 1.035 (95% CI: 0.982 to 1.088). Decision curve analysis, corrected for case-control sampling, showed positive net benefit across clinically plausible thresholds. The modular framework supports alternative outcomes, extension of predictor domains, and dynamic risk updating, providing a scalable foundation for interpretable, calibration-aware perioperative clinical decision support.

</details>


### [335] [Idea Search: Guiding Tree Search with Ideas to Explore Diverse Scientific Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08958)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Hao Cui,Michael P. Brenner,Subhashini Venugopalan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Idea Search框架，将动态"思想库"（Idea Bank）集成到树搜索中，通过将现有方法分解为原子思想、采样指导代码变异分支、并动态更新思想库，来破解纯树搜索在科学编码中陷入局部最优或无效循环的局限。在scRNA-seq批次整合任务上，该方法将强基线分数从0.678提升至0.697，最佳分数达0.728。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯树搜索（Tree Search）虽在LLM测试时扩展中表现强大，但在科学方法这一巨大搜索空间中常面临系统性探索不足、陷入局部最优或产生无效循环的问题，需要一种更结构化的探索机制来突破性能瓶颈。

Method: Idea Search包含三个步骤：(1) 将现有方法分解为原子化的思想（atomic ideas）；(2) 从思想库中采样以指导代码变异的分支生成；(3) 根据代码执行结果动态发现新思想并更新思想库。关键设计包括库增强（bank augmentation）、"Exploratory"提示策略（优先探索新思想）以及不同采样级别的探索控制。

Result: 在单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq）批次整合任务上，Idea Search突破强纯树搜索基线的性能平台期，将平均分数从0.678提升至0.697，最佳分数达到0.728。消融实验揭示：库增强对bandit采样有效但对随机采样无效；"Exploratory"提示能有效发现稀有最优解；增加采样级探索反而适得其反。

Conclusion: Idea Search证明了系统化的思想管理（而非仅依赖盲目搜索）是提升LLM科学编码测试时扩展的关键。具体设计选择（如库增强策略与提示方式）对探索效率有显著的差异化影响，为后续研究指明了可优化的方向。

Abstract: Tree Search-based test-time scaling of LLMs is a powerful tool for automated scientific coding. However, pure Tree Search sometimes struggles with systematic exploration, becoming trapped in local optima, or unproductive loops, especially in the vast search space of scientific methods. To address this limitation, we propose Idea Search, a framework that systematically integrates a dynamic "Idea Bank" into Tree Search. Idea Search involves three steps: (1) decomposing existing methods into atomic ideas, (2) sampling from this bank of ideas to guide branches of code mutations, and (3) dynamically updating the bank with new ideas discovered through execution. On single-cell RNA-sequencing (scRNA-seq) batch integration, Idea Search reliably breaks the plateau of a strong pure Tree Search baseline, improving the mean score from 0.678 to 0.697 and reaching a best score of 0.728. We then characterize which design choices drive these gains: bank augmentation helps bandit sampling but not random sampling, "Exploratory" prompting that prioritizes new ideas surfaces the rare best-performing solutions, while increasing sampling-level exploration is counterproductive.

</details>


### [336] [Gradient Under Microscope: Benchmarking Resource Utilization of Memory-Efficient Gradient Computation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08961)
*Sarthak Mahapatra,Zihan Zhou,Khatoon Khedri,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,Reza Rawassizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受限硬件条件下，系统性地基准测试了五种梯度优化器（SGD、Adam、Adagrad、Adadelta、共轭梯度下降）搭配三种内存策略（标准训练、梯度检查点、梯度累积）在四种Transformer架构（ViT、ModernBERT、Llama 3.1 1B、NanoVLM）上的表现。研究发现梯度累积是最可靠的内存策略，Adadelta和SGD在部分架构上优于Adam，梯度检查点的效果高度依赖架构，GPU利用率主要受架构而非训练策略主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练的算力和资源消耗日益加剧，对电力供应和碳预算造成巨大压力。作者旨在为资源受限硬件上的内存高效训练提供系统性的实证指导，解决当前训练策略选择缺乏全面基准比较的问题。

Method: 在四种Transformer架构（ViT、ModernBERT、Llama 3.1 1B、NanoVLM）上，对五种梯度优化器（SGD、Adam、Adagrad、Adadelta、共轭梯度下降）与三种内存策略（标准训练、梯度检查点、梯度累积）进行全组合基准测试，测量训练损失、GPU利用率、训练时间和内存使用四个关键指标。

Result: 梯度累积是最可靠策略，在视觉语言模型上训练损失降低约一个数量级，在语言模型上降低约四倍且无需额外GPU内存。Adam并非普遍最优，Adadelta和SGD在编码器和自回归架构上表现更佳。梯度检查点对视觉Transformer有益但严重损害编码器模型，并使内存受限模型的训练时间增加高达60%。GPU利用率主要由架构决定，内存受限语言模型仅8-15%，而计算受限视觉模型达96-99%。

Conclusion: 该研究为资源高效模型训练和部署提供了实用的优化器与梯度策略选择指南，挑战了Adam普遍最优的常见假设，强调了架构与训练策略之间的交互作用对训练效率和性能的关键影响。

Abstract: AI training's rising resource intensity is straining electricity supplies and carbon budgets, motivating systematic study of memory-efficient training on constrained hardware. We benchmark five gradient optimizers (SGD, Adam, Adagrad, Adadelta, and Conjugate Gradient Descent) under three memory strategies (standard training, gradient checkpointing, and gradient accumulation) across four transformer architectures (ViT, ModernBERT, Llama 3.1 1B, and NanoVLM), measuring training loss, GPU utilization, training time, and memory usage. Gradient accumulation emerges as the most reliable strategy, cutting training loss by roughly an order of magnitude on the vision-language model and about four-fold on the language model without additional GPU memory. Contrary to common practice, Adam is not universally superior: Adadelta and SGD outperform it on the encoder and autoregressive architectures. Gradient checkpointing's effect is strongly architecture-dependent, improving vision transformer loss while severely degrading the encoder model, and it increases training time by up to 60% on memory-bound models. GPU utilization is governed primarily by architecture, ranging from 8-15% for the memory-bound language model to 96-99% for compute-bound vision models. These findings provide practical guidelines for optimizer and gradient-strategy selection in resource-efficient model training and deployment.

</details>


### [337] [Math-Vision Diagrams: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Mathematical Diagram Generation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08964)
*Harish Kashyap,Kiran Byadarhaly,Sriram Chakaravarthy,Sanyukta Tuti,Aryan Mistry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Math-Vision Diagrams，这是首个专门用于评估LLM数学图表生成能力的基准，统一评估text-to-code和text-to-image两种范式，构建了2920张高质量数学竞赛题图表的数据集，并发现现有领先LLM在该任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学图表生成对LLM而言是重要但未被充分探索的能力，涉及空间推理、数学推理和渲染系统的协同。现有基准（如MathVision、MathVista、MermaidSeqBench）或关注数学推理，或关注通用图表生成，缺乏专门针对数学图表生成的标准化prompt-图像对数据集。

Method: 基于Math-Vision基准，从3040张图像中筛选出2920张来自高质量竞赛题且具有必需视觉上下文的图像，构建统一的数据集。采用LLM集成与领域专家（SME）人工校验结合的pipeline进行数据标注，并设计了一套评估指标。数据集设计为与底层编码语言或模型类型无关，同时覆盖text-to-code和text-to-image两种生成范式。

Result: 对多个领先LLM进行测试，实验表明LLM在数学图表生成任务上仍然存在明显困难，能力不足。代码、数据、curation pipeline和评估脚本将全部开源。

Conclusion: Math-Vision Diagrams填补了数学图表生成评估基准的空白，是首个在同一统一设置下同时评估text-to-code和text-to-image两种范式的基准。研究揭示了LLM在数学图表生成方面的当前局限性，为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台和开源资源。

Abstract: The generation of mathematically precise diagrams from tex- tual prompts has emerged as a critical yet underexplored capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). This has been of interest to researchers in the areas of curriculum preparation, automated ranking of problem sets, and scientific publishing. For LLMs to achieve this, it requires per- fect coordination between Spatial Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, and Rendering systems. While existing benchmarks such as MathVision, MathVista are built for Math Reasoning or DiagramGenBenchmark, Mer- maidSeqBench on general purpose diagram generation, no prior work provides a standardized set of prompt, image pairs that can be used to evaluate the LLMs specifically on math diagram generation. This includes fields that span both both text-to-code and text-to-image paradigms. We introduce Math-Vision Diagrams, the first benchmark specifically designed to evaluate LLMs on mathematical diagram generation, and the first to assess text-to-code and text-to-image generation paradigms together in a single unified setting, agnostic of the underlying coding lan- guage or model type. Building on the Math-Vision benchmark, we select a subset of 2920 images out of 3040 from high-quality competition problems with essential visual context. A novel pipeline combining an ensemble of LLMs with Subject Matter Expert (SME) curation is presented, together with a suite of evaluation metrics. Testing several leading models against this benchmark, we demonstrate that LLMs struggle with math diagram generation. All code, data, curation pipeline, and evaluation scripts will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [338] [Label-Free Parkinson's Disease Screening from Face and Voice through Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08976)
*Jiaheng Su,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种完全无标签的帕金森病(PD)人脸+语音筛查方法，仅使用冻结的预训练编码器（ViT和HuBERT），无需任何PD标签参与训练；语音模态采用合成构音障碍对比激活添加(CAA)方向，人脸模态采用k近邻异常检测；核心提出"对齐原则"以解释CAA检测器何时有效。在YouTubePD基准上，语音CAA的AUROC为0.765，人脸异常检测AUROC为0.751，等权晚期融合达到AUROC 0.802（NPV 0.95），支持排除性分诊应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是第二大常见神经退行性疾病，传统机器学习筛查依赖PD标签，但数据受隐私限制且需要专家标注，导致可用数据稀缺。需要一种不依赖PD标签的筛查方法。

Method: 1) 语音模态：通过时间拉伸和气息化降解健康语音生成合成构音障碍数据，从中提取CAA方向，利用冻结的HuBERT编码器进行检测。2) 人脸模态：基于冻结的ViT，计算测试样本到对照组嵌入聚类的k近邻异常分数。3) 引入"对齐原则"：当合成退化方向与真实疾病方向的余弦相似度>0时，CAA检测器有效。4) 等权晚期融合两模态结果。

Result: YouTubePD基准上：语音方向余弦相似度为+0.37（CAA有效，AUROC 0.765）；人脸方向余弦相似度为-0.48（CAA失效，异常检测奏效，AUROC 0.751）；等权晚期融合AUROC 0.802（95% CI [0.70, 0.89]），NPV为0.95。过拟合审计显示语音检测器泛化干净，但人脸侧及融合AUROC可能偏乐观，需外部验证。

Conclusion: 完全无标签的PD筛查在语音+人脸多模态上可行，语音侧CAA方法可干净迁移，融合模型支持"排除性分诊"场景；但人脸侧结果存在乐观偏差风险，需要外部数据集验证以确认普适性。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is the second most common neurodegenerative disorder. Typical machine learning screening methods require PD labels, but the available data is limited by privacy concerns and the need for expert annotation. We propose a label-free face-plus-voice PD screen built entirely on frozen pretrained encoders--a face-expression Vision Transformer and HuBERT--in which no PD label touches any fit; the reference is training controls only. The voice modality uses a synthetic-dysarthria contrastive activation addition (CAA) direction built from time-stretch and breathy degradation of healthy speech; the face modality uses a k-nearest-neighbor anomaly score to the control embedding cluster. We introduce the alignment principle, a post-hoc analysis showing that a synthetic-degradation CAA detector works when the cosine similarity between the synthetic and real disease directions exceeds zero. Measured on the YouTubePD benchmark, this cosine is +0.37 for voice (CAA works, AUROC 0.765) and -0.48 for face (CAA fails; anomaly succeeds, AUROC 0.751). Equal-weight late fusion reaches AUROC 0.802 (95% CI [0.70,0.89]) with NPV 0.95, supporting a rule-out triage interpretation. An overfitting audit shows the voice detector transfers cleanly, while the face-side--and thus fused--AUROC is potentially optimistic pending external validation.

</details>


### [339] [Twin Rollouts: Noise-Coupled Counterfactual Branching in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08982)
*Yu Ma,Hongli Shi,Xinran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在交互式视频世界模型中进行反事实生成的形式化框架：利用噪声耦合的双胞胎轨迹（事实分支与反事实分支共享生成前缀和未来外生噪声，仅在干预点后动作流不同），由于事实分支是模型自生成的，其外生噪声精确已知，使得Pearl反事实程序中的abduction步骤精确可构造，避免了基于编辑的管线所面临的近似反演问题；同时将最小变更原则转化为逐样本可验证的属性，并提出时空局部性度量，可基于模拟器真实值计算可验证奖励用于后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式视频世界模型几乎仅在事实预测上训练和评估，缺乏对反事实生成的研究。给定模型自身生成的轨迹，需要回答"若从第t*步起动作不同会发生什么"这一关键问题。此外，传统的反事实编辑方法面临外生噪声近似反演的难题，且最小变更原则难以逐样本验证。

Method: 本文提出了噪声耦合双胞胎轨迹的形式化框架：事实分支与反事实分支共享已生成的prefix及未来的外生噪声序列，仅在干预点的动作流处分叉。由于事实分支由模型自生成，其外生噪声精确已知，abduction步骤通过构造精确完成。噪声耦合将最小变更原则转化为逐样本可验证的属性，定义了惩罚干预因果后裔之外分歧的时空局部性度量，可在模拟器真实值下计算，无需学习判别器。通过在t*处分叉模拟器状态获得真实反事实重渲染，作为可验证奖励用于后训练。

Result: 本文目前建立了形式化框架、度量定义和定位（positioning），实验将在后续工作中进行。

Conclusion: 该框架通过精确abduction绕过了传统编辑管线中噪声反演的困难，并将反事实的合理性转化为可验证的、基于模拟器真实值的性质，为视频世界模型的反事实能力提供了理论基础和训练信号来源。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate rollouts autoregressively under an action stream, yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on factual prediction. We study counterfactual generation inside the rollout: given a trajectory the model has itself generated, what would have happened had the actions differed from step t* onward? We formalize noise-coupled twin rollouts --- a factual and a counterfactual branch sharing the generated prefix and the future exogenous noise sequence, diverging only in the action stream at an intervention point. Because the factual branch is self-generated, its exogenous noise is known exactly: the abduction step of Pearl's counterfactual procedure is exact by construction, sidestepping the approximate-inversion problem faced by editing-based pipelines. Noise coupling further turns the minimal-change principle into a per-sample verifiable property: we define a spatiotemporal locality metric that penalizes divergence outside the causal descendants of the intervention, computable against simulator ground truth without a learned judge. Forking the simulator state at t* yields ground-truth counterfactual re-renders, which we use as verifiable rewards for post-training. This note establishes the formal framework, metric definitions, and positioning; experiments are forthcoming.

</details>


### [340] [SoftMCC: An MCC-Brier Calibration Bridge for Threshold-Free Model Selection under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08984)
*Özkan Canay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftMCC，一种基于后训练概率混淆计数的MCC验证框架，通过校准恒等式和共享池选择协议，在18种设置下获得最优稳定性（平均秩2.31，Kendall's W=0.659），但模型选择效用无优势，且对标签置换和温度缩放敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不平衡二分类中MCC广泛用于模型选择，但基于阈值的MCC使验证排名依赖阈值选择，缺乏阈值无关的稳定评估方法。

Method: 提出SoftMCC框架：在概率级混淆计数上计算协方差归一化的概率-标签关联分数，耦合MCC特定校准恒等式与平局感知的共享池选择协议；分数对硬预测精确退化为MCC，且受Pearson界约束。通过18种设置、12次去重分组重复实验，结合Friedman检验、Nemenyi事后分析、标签置换和温度缩放扰动进行验证。

Result: SoftMCC在稳定性上表现最优（平均秩2.31，Kendall's W=0.659，Friedman p=0.007），Nemenyi分析将其与AUPRC和MCC@0.5区分开；但选中模型效用无优势，3/6预设比较中测试MCC差异为负，仅F1@best通过Holm校正（p=0.014），数据集级检验不显著（p=0.117）。标签置换使W降至0.092，温度缩放改变SoftMCC排名（Spearman=0.851），而基于秩和阈值优化的指标保持不变。

Conclusion: SoftMCC是一个对校准敏感的MCC族模型选择器，具有有界的稳定性优势，但在模型选择效用上缺乏显著优势；其性质需要在校准边界内理解，超出该范围的表现差异不能归因于校准误差。

Abstract: Model selection for imbalanced binary classification often uses the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), but thresholding makes validation rankings threshold-dependent. SoftMCC is a post-training MCC validation framework on established probability-valued confusion counts, coupling an MCC-specific calibrated identity with a tie-aware, shared-pool selection protocol. Its core score is a covariance-normalized probability-label association, reduces exactly to MCC for hard predictions, and is Pearson-bounded. Under perfect population calibration it equals the Brier skill score with identical candidate ordering; outside that regime the gap does not identify calibration error. Across 18 settings with 12 duplicate-safe grouped repeats, SoftMCC attains the best stability mean rank (2.31) and highest mean tie-corrected Kendall's W (0.659), with a significant Friedman test (p=0.007); Nemenyi analysis separates it from AUPRC and MCC@0.5, while 14-source-family sensitivity retains only the latter. Selected-model utility shows no advantage. Three of six prespecified comparisons have negative mean test-MCC differences, only F1@best survives Holm correction (p=0.014), and the dataset-level test is not significant (p=0.117). Label permutation lowers mean W to 0.092; temperature scaling shifts SoftMCC rankings (mean Spearman 0.851) whereas rank-based and threshold-optimized metrics remain invariant. SoftMCC is a calibration-sensitive MCC-family selector with bounded stability and utility evidence.

</details>


### [341] [Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Tracking in Vision-Language Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09011)
*Ao Zhou,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Cong Wang,Shufan Yang,Haoru Chen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种动态分布感知的不确定性量化框架（DDA-UQ），通过高斯混合模型建模VLM嵌入空间来动态估计不确定性，以解决后验方法只能适应源域失败模式、无法应对测试分布动态变化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验不确定性量化方法虽然轻量高效，但本质上只能拟合源域的失败模式，忽略了测试分布动态变化的特性，导致在安全关键场景下部署VLM时可靠性不足。

Method: 训练阶段使用高斯混合模型（GMM）对VLM的嵌入空间进行建模并提取分布证据，动态推导不确定性估计；推理阶段动态响应数据分布的变化。

Result: 大量实验表明DDA-UQ在多个基准上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性量化的范式从静态映射转变为动态分布感知过程，DDA-UQ有效应对了测试分布漂移问题，提升了VLM在安全关键场景中的可靠性评估能力。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) aims to measure the reliability of model predictions, serving as a critical safeguard for deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in safety-critical scenarios. Post-hoc approaches are widely adopted due to their lightweight nature, mapping the outputs of VLMs to uncertainty measures through learnable modules or inductive summarization. However, Post-hoc approaches remain inherently confined to fitting the failure patterns of the source domain, ignoring the dynamic nature of test distributions. To address this challenge, we propose a Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Quantification framework (DDA-UQ) that shifts the paradigm from static mapping to a dynamic distribution-aware process. During training, we leverage a Gaussian Mixture Model to model the VVLMs'embedding space and extract distributional evidence, thereby dynamically deriving uncertainty estimates. During inference, the design dynamically responds to changes in the data distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [342] [Space-Time Information Interchangeability in Dynamical Systems: Conditions and Bounds for Replacing Spatial Sensors with Temporal Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07728)
*Maryam Reza,Farbod Faraji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架，用于比较时间测量历史与瞬时空间传感器阵列在动态状态重建中的信息内容。通过引入先验白化的信息算子，建立了时空信息交换定理，给出在每一状态方向上保留指定比例空间参考信息的充要条件，并回答了所需测量数量、信息天花板和充分历史长度三个实际问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，只能从少量空间位置获取测量数据，但需要重建高维空间状态。由于动力学在空间和时间上耦合演化，时间测量中包含关于非传感器位置状态分量的信息。作者希望建立一个理论框架，明确回答"何时可以用时间测量替代（或减少）空间传感器覆盖"这一核心问题。

Method: 该方法将时间测量历史与空间参考阵列分别表示为先验白化的信息算子，这些算子考虑了先验变异性、传感器几何和测量噪声，同时保持不确定性减少的方向分布。论文推导了一个通用的时空信息交换定理，提供在每一状态方向保留指定空间参考信息比例的充要条件。针对不同动力学类型——收缩型、谱分离酉型、可同时对角化的耗散线性动力学，以及高斯过程与测量噪声下的随机线性估计——推导了显式的历史长度界。对非线性系统，则结合全局延迟映射单射性与均匀局部Fisher信息条件。

Result: 论文得到了关于三个实际问题的确定性答案：(1)所需时间测量数量与总信息量；(2)指定信息比例是否可达，或是否存在信息天花板排除所有有限历史；(3)可达时所需的历史长度。数值实验测试了方向性交换长度、信息天花板和有限记忆界，并评估了非线性延迟映射的可区分性和局部Fisher信息预测。

Conclusion: 该框架为判断时间测量能否减少空间传感器覆盖以进行全状态重建提供了明确的理论基础，为传感器部署优化和动态状态估计提供了指导性工具。

Abstract: Many dynamical-state reconstruction problems seek to infer a high-dimensional spatial state from measurements at only a few locations. Because the governing dynamics couple evolution across space and time, temporal measurements can contain information about state components beyond the sensor locations. This work develops a theoretical framework comparing the information content of a temporal measurement history with that of a specified instantaneous spatial sensor array. The temporal history and spatial reference array are represented by prior-whitened information operators that account for prior variability, sensor geometry, and measurement noise while preserving the directional distribution of uncertainty reduction. A general space-time information-exchange theorem gives necessary and sufficient conditions for retaining a prescribed fraction of the spatial-reference information in every state direction. It answers three practical questions. First, how many temporal measurements and how much total information are required? Second, is the prescribed fraction attainable, or does a limiting information ceiling rule out every finite history? Third, when attainable, what history length is sufficient? Explicit history-length bounds are derived for contractive, spectrally separated unitary, and simultaneously diagonalizable dissipative linear dynamics, and for stochastic linear estimation under Gaussian process and measurement noise. For nonlinear systems, global delay-map injectivity is combined with a uniform local Fisher-information condition. Numerical experiments test directional exchange lengths, information ceilings, and finite-memory bounds, and assess nonlinear delay-map distinguishability and local Fisher-information predictions. The framework provides an explicit basis for determining when temporal measurements can reduce spatial sensor coverage for full-state reconstruction.

</details>


### [343] [Semantic-Aided Iterative Decoding for Uplink Non-Orthogonal Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07897)
*Wenjing Wei,Chentao Yue,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向共享自然语言源的上行非正交传输的语义辅助迭代译码（Sem-IR）方案。K个用户各自持有共同句子的一段，通过LDPC编码叠加传输于AWGN信道。基站端利用迭代基本信号估计器（ESE）和K个并行LDPC译码器逐步消除用户间干扰。高功率用户先通过奇偶校验和语言合理性检查后，其译码字节构成可靠的语言前缀，供微调的ByT5字节级语言模型预测未收敛用户的字节后验概率。这些后验被边缘化为比特级对数似然比，并与LDPC后验在迭代循环内凸组合，实现语言模型与物理层迭代的闭环反馈。仿真表明Sem-IR在BLER性能上优于正交时分接入和无语义反馈的NOMA方案，在8 dB时相比NOMA实现了一个数量级的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非正交多址（NOMA）系统仅依赖物理层奇偶校验信息进行干扰消除和译码，未利用自然语言源本身的语义冗余和语言结构约束。在共享自然语言源的场景下，用户可以相互利用语言上下文来辅助译码，将语言模型的先验知识融入迭代译码循环中，从而提升整体的块错误率性能。

Method: 提出Sem-IR架构：1）K个用户各自持有共同句子的一个片段，经LDPC编码后在AWGN信道上叠加传输；2）基站端采用迭代基本信号估计器（ESE）与K个并行LDPC译码器进行逐用户干扰消除；3）高功率用户先收敛后，其译码结果形成语言前缀；4）微调的ByT5字节级语言模型利用该前缀预测未收敛用户的字节后验分布；5）字节后验经边缘化转换为比特级LLR，与LDPC译码后验凸组合后反馈回迭代循环，形成语义与物理层的闭环。

Result: 仿真结果表明，Sem-IR在块错误率（BLER）上优于传统的正交时分接入（TDMA）以及无语义反馈的NOMA接收机。在8 dB信噪比下，Sem-IR相比NOMA实现了约一个数量级（order-of-magnitude）的BLER降低。

Conclusion: 将语言模型的语义先验嵌入NOMA迭代译码框架是可行且有效的。通过语言前缀引导的字节级预测反馈，可以显著加速未收敛用户的译码过程并降低错误率，证明了在物理层通信中利用自然语言语义信息的巨大潜力。该工作为语义通信与非正交传输的深度融合提供了新的范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes semantic-aided iterative decoding (Sem-IR) for uplink non-orthogonal transmission of a shared natural-language source. K users each hold one segment of a common sentence and superimpose low-density parity-check (LDPC) coded transmissions over an additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel. At the base station, an iterative elementary signal estimator (ESE) and K parallel LDPC decoders progressively cancel inter-user interference. As high-power users pass both parity and language-plausibility checks earlier, their decoded bytes form a reliable linguistic prefix for the remaining users; a fine-tuned ByT5 byte-level language model exploits this prefix to predict byte posteriors for the unconverged user. The byte posteriors are marginalized to bit-level log-likelihood ratios and convex-combined with the LDPC posteriors inside the iterative loop. The resulting feedback closes the loop between the language model and the physical-layer iteration. Simulations show that Sem-IR outperforms orthogonal time-division access (TDMA) and the same NOMA receiver without semantic feedback in block error rate (BLER), yielding an order-of-magnitude reduction over NOMA at 8 dB.

</details>


### [344] [Error characterization and error correction approaches in combinatorial DNA-based storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07991)
*Inbal Preuss,Omer Sabary,Ryan Gabrys,Zohar Yakhini,Eitan Yaakobi,Leon Anavy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统地表征了组合DNA存储系统中的非对称组合擦除错误，并提出了一种基于张量积编码的非对称纠错方案，该方案将标准擦除/替换纠错码（如RS码）与非对称Varshamov-Tenengolts码相结合，在低覆盖度条件下显著优于传统的2D RS方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合DNA编码通过短聚体组合提高逻辑密度，但其引入的独特合成和测序错误尚未被充分表征，尤其是非对称组合擦除错误（组合字母集合中单个短聚体缺失）在大规模低覆盖度系统中十分普遍，严重阻碍了组合字母的重建，亟需针对这类非对称错误的专门纠错方案。

Method: 首先在两组已有数据集上表征了组合DNA存储系统中的错误类型，发现非对称组合擦除错误是高发错误类型。随后开展大规模实验验证，确认在每条序列低于50次读取覆盖时擦除错误急剧增加。在此基础上开发了非对称纠错码，采用张量积码结构，将标准擦除/替换纠错码（如Reed-Solomon码）与非对称Varshamov-Tenengolts码集成。

Result: 在模拟实验和第二次大规模实验中，与直接使用2D RS方案相比，该方法在擦除主导场景下始终表现更优，在低覆盖度条件下（2D RS无法解码时）展现出优越的解码准确性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，针对组合DNA存储系统中错误的非对称性定制纠错方案至关重要，所提出的方法为大规模组合DNA存储系统的可靠性提供了有效保障。

Abstract: Data storage in DNA has recently emerged as a promising archival solution, offering space-efficient and long-lasting digital storage. Combinatorial DNA encoding enhances this potential by increasing the logical density through combinations of DNA shortmers, where each sequence position is represented by a set of predefined short DNA fragments, allowing more data to be encoded using fewer synthesis cycles. However, this method introduces unique synthesis and sequencing errors. In this study, we characterize errors in combinatorial DNA-based storage systems. We reveal that asymmetric combinatorial erasure errors, defined as the omission of a single shortmer from the set defining the combinatorial letter, are a prevalent error type, particularly in large-scale systems where read coverage is limited. In two previously published datasets, we observed a high frequency of erasure errors, where missing sequences obstruct the reconstruction of combinatorial letters. We conducted a large-scale experimental proof-of-concept and confirmed that erasure errors become increasingly prominent with reduced sequencing depth: below 50 reads per sequence, their frequency sharply increased. We developed an asymmetric error-correcting code for these errors, utilizing tensor-product codes to integrate standard erasure and substitution-correcting codes (such as Reed-Solomon (RS) codes) with asymmetric Varshamov-Tenengolts codes. We validated its performance in simulations and in a second large-scale experiment directly comparing it with the more straightforward 2D RS scheme. Our method consistently outperformed 2D RS, particularly in erasure-dominated scenarios, and demonstrated superior decoding accuracy under low coverage conditions where 2D RS struggled to decode the data. Our findings demonstrate the importance of error correction schemes tailored to the asymmetric nature of errors in combinatorial DNA.

</details>


### [345] [More on Codes for Combinatorial Composite DNA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08018)
*Zuo Ye,Omer Sabary,Ryan Gabrys,Eitan Yaakobi,Ohad Elishco*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(t,e)$-组合非对称纠错码（CAECCs）的唯一可译码和列表可译码构造。针对一般参数$m,n,w,t,e$，提出了冗余度至多为$(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$的新构造，并特别给出了冗余度最优的$(2,e)$-CAECCs。当$m$为素数幂时，冗余度可进一步降至$(t-1)\log(m)-O(\log m)$。引入弱$B_e$-集合来增大码的规模，并证明了在某些条件下列表可译码的CAECCs可达到常数冗余度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合非对称错误模型（即多个行中发生$1\to0$错误）是近年新提出的纠错场景，现有构造在冗余度方面尚不理想。作者希望为一般参数的$(t,e)$-CAECCs提供冗余度更低甚至最优的构造，并探索从唯一可译码放宽到列表可译码时能获得的性能收益。

Method: 作者提出多种构造方案：1) 针对一般参数设计冗余度至多$(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$的$(t,e)$-CAECCs，其中$(2,e)$-CAECCs在冗余度意义上达到最优；2) 当$m$为素数幂时，利用有限域结构进一步降低冗余度至$(t-1)\log(m)-O(\log m)$；3) 引入弱$B_e$-集合这一组合对象以增大码的规模，并在$e=w$时给出高效的编解码算法；4) 在列表可译码框架下，证明当列表大小为$t!$或$t$的指数函数时存在常数冗余度的构造，当列表大小为$2$时给出冗余度$\log(m)+O(1)$的$(3,2)$-CAECCs。

Result: 获得了针对一般参数的$(t,e)$-CAECCs新构造，冗余度至多$(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$；$(2,e)$-CAECCs的冗余度达到最优；素数幂条件下冗余度进一步降低；通过弱$B_e$-集合增大了码的大小；证明了列表可译码下存在常数冗余度的CAECCs构造（列表大小为$t!$或$t$的指数函数），以及列表大小为$2$时$(3,2)$-CAECCs的冗余度为$\log(m)+O(1)$。

Conclusion: 本文系统地推进了$(t,e)$-CAECCs的构造理论：在唯一可译码情形下实现了近最优或最优冗余度，在列表可译码情形下证明了常数冗余度的可行性，并通过弱$B_e$-集合和高效编解码算法增强了构造的实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we focus on constructions of unique-decodable/list-decodable on the recently studied $(t,e)$-composite-asymmetric error-correcting codes ($(t,e)$-CAECCs). Let $X$ be an $m\times n$ binary matrix, in which each row has Hamming weight $w$. When at most $t$ rows of $X$ suffer from errors and in each of these erroneous rows, there are at most $e$ $1 \to 0$ errors, we say that a $(t,e)$-composite-asymmetric-error occurs in $X$. For general $m,n,w,t,e$, we propose new constructions of $(t,e)$-CAECCs with redundancy at most $(t-1)\log(m)+O(1)$, where $O(1)$ is a number independent of the code-length $m$. In particular, this gives a class of $(2,e)$-CAECCs that are optimal in terms of their redundancy. %(in terms of redundancy, regarded as a function of the number of rows $m$) s. When $m$ is a prime power, the redundancy can be further reduced to $(t-1)\log(m)-O(\log(m))$. To further increase the size of these codes, we introduce a combinatorial object called a weak $B_e$-sets. When $e=w$, we show an efficient way to encode/decode our codes. At last, we investigate how much we can gain if we relax the requirement of uniquely decoding to list-decoding. It is shown that when the list size is $t!$ or an exponential function of $t$, there are list-decodable $(t,e)$-CAECCs with constant redundancy. When the list size is two, we show that there are list-decodable $(3,2)$-CAECCs with redundancy $\log(m)+O(1)$.

</details>


### [346] [Shapes and Norms of Random Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08039)
*Rostislav Matveev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文进一步发展了有限值随机变量对形状函数的理论，证明了当(X,Y)均匀支撑在双正则二分图的边上时，形状函数S(X,Y)(α,β)等于图关联矩阵在由(α,β)确定的Lebesgue指数下的算子范数的对数，并建立了形状函数满足的一系列关系与不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状函数在先前的工作中已用于推导随机变量对的纠缠谱界，但该理论仍不完善。本文旨在深入发展形状函数的理论基础，特别是将其与图关联矩阵的算子范数建立联系，从而为数值逼近和后续应用提供工具，并系统建立形状函数的性质体系。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与数学证明的方法：(1) 对于均匀支撑在双正则二分图边上的随机变量对(X,Y)，证明形状函数等于关联矩阵在相应Lebesgue指数下的算子范数的对数；(2) 利用该识别结果，通过算子范数实现形状函数的数值逼近；(3) 借助对偶性，推广到扩展轮廓（张力区域）的近似计算；(4) 建立形状函数的凸性、单调性、复合不等式，以及条件作用和添加变量下行为的关系。

Result: (1) 证明了双正则二分图边均匀支撑下形状函数与图关联矩阵算子范数对数的等价性；(2) 该等价性使得形状函数及扩展轮廓（张力区域）可通过数值方法近似求解；(3) 建立了一系列形状函数的性质，包括凸性、单调性、复合不等式、条件化及变量添加下的行为描述。

Conclusion: 本文系统推进了随机变量对形状函数理论的发展，通过算子范数的桥梁实现了理论与数值计算的结合，并建立了完备的性质框架，为形状函数在信息论和量子纠缠等领域的进一步应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The shape function of a pair of finite-valued random variables was introduced in arXiv:2606.23849, where it was used to derive a spectral bound on the entanglement of the pair, a quantity measuring the extent to which their mutual information can be extracted. In this article, we further develop the theory of shape functions for pairs of random variables. We prove that, when $(X,Y)$ is uniformly supported on the edges of a biregular bipartite graph, the value of the shape function $\mathcal{S}(X,Y)(α,β)$ equals the logarithm of the operator norm of the graph's incidence matrix with respect to Lebesgue exponents determined by $(α,β)$.This identification, in particular, enables the numerical approximation of the shape function and, by duality, of the extension profile, also known as the tension region, of the pair. We also establish a collection of relations and inequalities satisfied by shape functions, including convexity and monotonicity properties, composition inequalities, and relations describing their behavior under conditioning and the adjoining of variables.

</details>


### [347] [Probabilistic Temporal Shaping for Level-Constrained Signaling on Bandlimited Additive White Gaussian Noise Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08044)
*Mahdi Mahvari,Gerhard Kramer,Shlomo Shamai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文针对实数字母连续时间信道中的一比特量化信令，提出了一种新的容量下界。通过概率时间整形（PTS）方法，在带限加性白高斯噪声信道中导出了最优整形密度，使得每个奈奎斯特速率样本具有一个数据相关的符号变化。计算表明，在高信噪比下，PTS方案比现有最优下界改善至少1.94 dB，而更简单的顺序PTS方案可实现1.64 dB的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一比特量化信令在带限连续时间信道中的容量下界仍有改进空间，需要新的方法（如概率时间整形）来收紧现有下界。

Method: 采用概率时间整形（PTS）方法，对每个奈奎斯特速率样本施加一个数据相关的符号变化，并推导满足可逆性要求的最优整形密度。同时提出一个更简单的顺序PTS方案作为对比。

Result: 在高信噪比下，PTS方案比现有最佳下界提升至少1.94 dB；顺序PTS方案实现1.64 dB的增益。

Conclusion: 概率时间整形能显著改善带限AWGN信道中一比特量化信令的容量下界，且顺序PTS方案在复杂度较低的情况下仍能提供有效的性能增益。

Abstract: Level-constraints model one-bit-quantized signaling over real-alphabet continuous-time channels. New lower bounds on the capacity of bandlimited, additive white Gaussian noise channels with level-constrained inputs are derived by using probabilistic temporal shaping (PTS). The optimal shaping density is derived for signals with one data-dependent sign change per Nyquist-rate sample, which ensures they satisfy an invertibility requirement. Calculations show that PTS improves the best existing lower bound by at least 1.94 dB at high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). A simpler sequential PTS scheme achieves a gain of 1.64 dB at high SNR.

</details>


### [348] [Dependency-Aware Reliability Allocation for Open-Vocabulary Scene-Graph Semantic Packets over Latency- and Energy-Constrained Wireless Visual Uplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08110)
*Yuli Liu,Jiacheng Ruan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无线视觉感知上行链路中开放词汇场景图（open-vocabulary scene graph）语义数据包（subject-relation-object三元组及词汇增量包）的依赖感知可靠性分配问题。在帧延迟与帧能量约束下，联合优化每个数据包的调制编码方案、发射功率和最大HARQ深度。提出语义依赖拉格朗日块方法，利用多仿射失真结构进行精确有限候选最小化，在30个缩减菜单实例的穷举审计中28次达到全局最优，平均相对间隙0.052%，最差1.10%，在多项验证中优于依赖无关HARQ、语义优先HARQ、优先级UEP、纯UEP和统一HARQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线视觉感知上行链路日益传输结构化语义数据包而非原始图像或不透明特征张量。在开放词汇场景图中，索引化的主语-关系-宾语三元组包只有在其自身数据包及所有前置词汇增量包都被成功恢复时才是可解释的，这形成了传统丢包、层优先级或语义特征HARQ规则无法捕获的依赖结构。

Method: 模型区分词汇增量包与索引三元组包，构建其前置图（prerequisite graph），推导Chase合并HARQ残差失败概率、反馈感知的延迟与能量以及依赖感知的语义失真。将问题建模为有限离散优化，联合选择每个包的调制编码方案（MCS）、发射功率和最大HARQ深度。关键创新在于揭示词汇包共享的下游启用价值取决于其他前置包和三元组包的当前可靠性，从而产生多仿射失真结构，据此提出语义依赖拉格朗日块方法，对每个包更新执行精确的有限候选最小化。

Result: 在30个缩减菜单实例的穷举审计中，该方法28次达到全局最优，平均相对间隙0.052%，最差1.10%。通过分析链路预算扫描、公开场景图轨迹、多会话与多种子验证及表驱动BLER测试，验证了相比依赖无关HARQ、语义优先HARQ、优先级UEP、纯UEP和统一HARQ，该方法降低了词汇诱导的语义失败率并提高了关键查询成功率。

Conclusion: 本文开创性地将场景图语义包的依赖结构纳入无线可靠传输分配决策，证明了利用多仿射结构可实现对依赖感知HARQ分配的高效精确求解，为开放词汇语义通信的跨层设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Wireless visual perception uplinks increasingly transmit structured semantic packets rather than raw images or opaque feature tensors. In an open-vocabulary scene graph, an indexed subject-relation-object triplet is interpretable only if its own packet and all prerequisite vocabulary-increment packets are recovered, creating a dependency structure not captured by conventional packet-loss, layer-priority, or semantic-feature HARQ rules. We study dependency-aware reliability allocation for such packets under expected frame-delay and frame-energy constraints. The model separates vocabulary-increment and indexed-triplet packets, builds their prerequisite graph, and derives Chase-combining HARQ residual failure, feedback-aware delay and energy, and dependency-aware semantic distortion. The resulting finite discrete problem jointly selects each packet's modulation and coding scheme, transmit power, and maximum HARQ depth. Unlike fixed-priority UEP, a vocabulary packet receives a shared downstream enabling value that depends on the current reliabilities of other prerequisite and triplet packets. Exploiting the resulting multi-affine distortion structure, the proposed semantic-dependency Lagrangian block method performs exact finite-candidate minimization for each packet update. In a small-scale exhaustive audit over 30 reduced-menu instances, the method reaches the global optimum in 28 cases, with mean and worst observed relative gaps of 0.052% and 1.10%. Analytical link-budget sweeps, public scene-graph traces, multi-session and multi-seed validation, and table-driven BLER tests show lower vocabulary-induced semantic failure and higher critical-query success than dependency-agnostic HARQ, semantic-priority HARQ, priority UEP, UEP-only, and uniform HARQ.

</details>


### [349] [Optimal Exponent of the Single-Error Correction Threshold with Fixed Redundancy for Analog Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08171)
*Zhengyi Jiang,Wenhao Liu,Zhongyi Huang,Hanxu Hou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了模拟纠错码（Analog ECCs）单纠错阈值Γ₂(C)的匹配下界，对于固定冗余r的实数[n,k]线性码，证明Γ₂(C)≥Ω(n^{1+1/(r-1)})，结合已有上界，完整刻画了这一阈值的渐近最优阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Roth引入的模拟纠错码用于应对存内计算中向量-矩阵乘法面临的模拟噪声和稀疏异常值。一个基础开放问题是为固定冗余r≥2的实数[n,k]线性码建立单纠错阈值Γ₂(C)的下界。Li等人在r=2时解决了该问题，并在一般r下构造了满足Γ₂(C)≤O(n^{1+1/(r-1)})的码类，但缺少匹配的下界来确认该上界的渐近最优性。

Method: 本文通过精细的几何与解析论证，对任意固定冗余r的[n,k]实数线性码推导出Γ₂(C)的通用下界。利用球面面积、Gamma函数及常数a_r和β_d，建立了显式下界Γ₂(C)≥a_r/(√r·β_{r-1}·2^{1/(r-1)})·n^{1+1/(r-1)}，并进一步简化为Γ₂(C)≥1/(4π√3r)·n^{1+1/(r-1)}。

Result: 证明了对于所有固定冗余2≤r<n的[n,k]实数线性码，Γ₂(C)≥Ω(n^{1+1/(r-1)})，给出了两个显式常数下界。该下界与文献[2]中的上界O(n^{1+1/(r-1)})相匹配，确认了指数n^{1+1/(r-1)}的最优性。

Conclusion: 这项研究完成了模拟纠错码单纠错阈值的渐近完整刻画，解决了文献[2]中提出的匹配逆问题，证实了n^{1+1/(r-1)}是最优指数，为存内计算中模拟纠错码的设计提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: Analog error-correcting codes (Analog ECCs), introduced by Roth [1], address errors in vector-matrix multiplication arising from analog noise and sparse outliers in in-memory computing. A fundamental open problem concerns the lower bound on the single-error correction threshold $Γ_2(\mathcal C)$ for real $[n,k]$ linear codes with fixed redundancy $r=n-k\geq 2$. Li et al. [2] recently established that for redundancy $r=2$, every real $[n,n-2]$ linear code $\mathcal{C}$ satisfies $Γ_2(\mathcal C)\geq \csc^2(\fracπ{2n})$, resolving an open problem in [1], and showed that, for every fixed $r \geq 2$, there exists a class of $[n,k]$ linear code $\mathcal{C}$ over $\mathbb{R}$ such that $Γ_2(\mathcal{C}) \leq O(n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}})$.
  This paper proves the matching converse in [2]. For every $[n,k]$ linear code $\mathcal{C}\subseteq \mathbb R^n$ with fixed redundancy $2\leq r<n$, we show that \[ Γ_2(\mathcal C)\ge \frac{a_r}{\sqrt{r}\,β_{r-1}\,2^{\frac{1}{r-1}}}\cdot n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}}, \] where $a_r =\frac{Γ(\frac{r}{2})}{\sqrtπ\,Γ(\frac{r+1}{2})}$ and $β_d = \left(\frac{dπ^{d-1}|\mathbb S^d|}{|\mathbb S^{d-1}|}\right)^{\frac{1}{d}}$ for positive integer $d$. Here $\mathbb S^d$ denotes the unit sphere in $\mathbb R^{d+1}$, $|\mathbb S^d|$ its surface area, and $Γ(\cdot)$ the Gamma function. In particular, we further show that $Γ_2(\mathcal C)\geq \frac{1}{4π\sqrt{3}r}\cdot n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}}$. Together with the upper bound in [2], this confirms that the exponent $n^{1+\frac{1}{r-1}}$ is optimal, completing the asymptotic characterization of the single-error correction threshold for Analog ECCs.

</details>


### [350] [A counterexample to the Etzion-Silberstein conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08478)
*Jitendra Prajapati*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造二进制Ferrers图秩度量码，证明Etzion-Silberstein猜想不成立。对于列高为(5,5,5,5,1,1)的Ferrers图E，最小秩距离为3时，Singleton型上界为12，但所有二进制线性码的维数至多为11，并给出显式的维数11的码，从而反证该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Etzion-Silberstein猜想声称Singleton型上界对所有Ferrers图、最小秩距离和有限域都是可达的。验证或反驳这一猜想对于理解线性Ferrers图秩度量码的结构和极限至关重要。

Method: 先将假设存在的维数12的码约化为三类二进[4×4,12,2] MRD码的等价类；利用秩分布论证排除其中两类，对剩余域类中的四个核轨道逐一证明其精确升降系统不可满足。独立编写的验证器（包括对全部8,382,465个核进行无轨道约化的穷举枚举）确认结果。此外证明了一个精确的行锥传播恒等式，迭代该恒等式可在每个最小秩距离d≥3下产生界为12、最优值为11的二进制反例。

Result: 证明了Ferrers图E上最小秩距离3的二进制线性码最大维数为11（而非上界12），给出了显式的维数11的码，从而否定了Etzion-Silberstein猜想。同时通过行锥传播恒等式，将反例推广到所有d≥3的情形。

Conclusion: 该工作通过具体反例彻底否证了Etzion-Silberstein猜想，表明Singleton型上界并非对所有Ferrers图都可达，并提供了可验证的计算证明方法。

Abstract: The Etzion-Silberstein conjecture asserts that the Singleton-type upper bound for linear Ferrers-diagram rank-metric codes is attained for every Ferrers diagram, minimum rank distance, and finite field. Let $E$ be the Ferrers diagram with column heights $(5,5,5,5,1,1)$. The bound for minimum rank distance $3$ is $12$. We prove that every binary linear code supported on $E$ with minimum rank distance $3$ has dimension at most $11$, and we give an explicit code of dimension $11$. Thus the optimum is exactly $11$, disproving the conjecture. The nonexistence proof reduces a hypothetical dimension-$12$ code to one of the three equivalence classes of binary $[4\times 4,12,2]$ MRD codes. A rank-distribution argument eliminates two classes and leaves four kernel orbits in the field class; all four exact lift systems are unsatisfiable. Independently written verifiers reproduce the result, including a raw enumeration of all $8,382,465$ kernels without orbit reduction. We also prove an exact row-cone propagation identity. Iterating it produces binary counterexamples with bound $12$ and optimum $11$ at every minimum rank distance $d \geq 3$.

</details>


### [351] [Memory AMP: Overflow Avoidance, Complexity Reduction, and Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08513)
*Shunqi Huang,Lei Liu,Brian M. Kurkoski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了内存近似消息传递（MAMP）类算法在高维噪声线性系统中的信号恢复问题，针对梯度下降MAMP（GD-MAMP）提出overflow问题的解决方案和两种低复杂度变体，并发展出通用的基于梯度的MAMP算法设计框架，该框架包含WS-CG-VAMP作为特例。作者证明WS-CG-VAMP的有限精度不稳定性源于正交化参数计算中的灾难性抵消，并提出等效重构WS-CG-VAMP(r)，将矩阵向量乘积次数减少最多50%。按矩阵向量乘积为衡量标准，GD-MAMP在条件数较小时收敛更快，而WS-CG-VAMP(r)在条件数较大时收敛更快，但在IEEE双精度下可能因灾难性抵消而发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMP类算法在高维信号恢复中广泛使用，MAMP框架为AMP算法引入记忆项提供了新途径。然而GD-MAMP在大条件数场景下存在中间变量溢出（overflow）问题，且现有算法的计算复杂度和数值稳定性仍待改善。本文旨在解决这些问题，并建立统一的基于梯度的MAMP设计框架。

Method: 首先针对GD-MAMP在大条件数下的溢出问题提出解决方案；其次提出两个低复杂度变体：部分记忆替代全长记忆、将每次迭代的矩阵向量乘积从三次减至两次；随后开发通用梯度基MAMP算法设计框架，表明其包含WS-CG-VAMP为特例，并揭示其正交化参数计算存在灾难性抵消导致有限精度不稳定；最后提出等效重构WS-CG-VAMP(r)，将矩阵向量乘积次数减少最多50%。

Result: 两个GD-MAMP变体在收敛速度上没有明显退化；WS-CG-VAMP的灾难性抵消被理论解释；WS-CG-VAMP(r)将矩阵向量乘积减少最多50%。以矩阵向量乘积次数为指标，GD-MAMP在小条件数下收敛更快，WS-CG-VAMP(r)在大条件数高精度运算下收敛更快，但在IEEE双精度下可能因灾难性抵消而发散。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了GD-MAMP在实际计算中的溢出问题，提出了计算效率更高的变体，并使WS-CG-VAMP的数值不稳定性得到理论解释和缓解。研究揭示了不同算法在条件数维度上的收敛性能权衡，为高维线性系统信号恢复中的AMP算法设计提供了新的理论工具和实用指导。

Abstract: Approximate message passing (AMP)-type algorithms are widely used for signal recovery in high-dimensional noisy linear systems. Recently, a framework called memory AMP (MAMP) was introduced, offering a new approach to incorporating memory terms within AMP algorithms. Building on this, a low-complexity gradient descent MAMP (GD-MAMP) was proposed for right-unitarily invariant matrices. In this paper, we first address an overflow problem in GD-MAMP caused by intermediate variables exceeding the floating-point range, which typically occurs when the condition number is large. Second, we propose two low-complexity variants of GD-MAMP: one replaces full-length memory with partial memory, while the other reduces the number of matrix-vector products per iteration by $1/3$ (from three to two). Neither degrades the convergence speed notably. Third, we develop a general gradient-based formulation for designing MAMP algorithms. This formulation recovers warm-started conjugate gradient VAMP (WS-CG-VAMP) as a special case. Furthermore, we show that the computation of the orthogonalization parameters in this formulation can suffer from catastrophic cancellation, which explains the finite-precision instability of WS-CG-VAMP. Finally, we derive an equivalent reformulation, termed WS-CG-VAMP(r), which reduces the number of matrix-vector products by up to $50\%$. Measured by matrix-vector products, GD-MAMP converges faster for small condition numbers, whereas WS-CG-VAMP(r) converges faster for large ones under high-precision arithmetic but may diverge in IEEE double precision due to catastrophic cancellation.

</details>


### [352] [Fluid Antenna System-Enabled Interference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08584)
*Jialu Zheng,Quanzhong Li,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流体系天线（FAS）的干扰对齐方案，应用于多用户干扰信道。在发送端无信道状态信息的情况下，利用FAS提供的额外空间自由度来消除有效组合干扰的实部分量，从而保留无干扰的期望信号检测。理论上推导了干扰对齐条件和相应的中断概率，仿真验证了理论推导与数值结果的一致性，且所提方案的平均和速率优于传统单用户传输方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干扰对齐是提高多用户无线网络频谱利用效率的关键技术，但传统方案通常需要发送端的信道状态信息（CSI）。在实际系统中获取发送端CSI代价高昂且不现实，因此需要探索在无发送端CSI条件下实现干扰对齐的方法，以提升频谱效率。

Method: 利用流体天线系统（FAS）提供的额外空间自由度，在接收端通过天线位置切换来置零有效组合干扰的实部分量，从而创造无干扰的期望信号检测空间。在无发送端CSI的假设下，理论推导了干扰对齐的实现条件以及对应的中断概率表达式。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真验证了理论推导的中断概率与数值结果高度吻合。此外，所提出的FAS辅助干扰对齐方案在平均和速率方面优于传统非干扰信道上的单用户传输方案。

Conclusion: FAS与干扰对齐的结合能够在无发送端CSI条件下有效消除多用户干扰，显著提升系统吞吐量，为实际无CSI场景下的干扰管理提供了可行且高效的新方案。

Abstract: Interference alignment is an efficient spectrum reuse scheme for multiuser wireless networks. In this letter, assuming no channel state information at the transmitters, we investigate a fluid antenna system (FAS)-enabled interference alignment scheme over a multiuser interference channel. In the proposed system, the additional spatial degrees of freedom provided by the FAS are exploited to nullify the real components of the effective combined interference such that those are reserved for interference-free desired signal detection. The interference alignment conditions and corresponding outage probabilities are theoretically derived. Simulations verify that the theoretically derived outage probabilities agree well with the numerical results. Furthermore, the proposed system is able to achieve a higher average sum rate than conventional single-user transmission schemes over non-interfering channels.

</details>


### [353] [Mean Gain Is Not a Guarantee: Risk-Controlled Open-Loop Scheduling for Ka-Band LEO Semantic Downlinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08635)
*Milad Bafarassat*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对低轨卫星在下行链路中调度图像传输的问题，指出传统的基于平均收益和统计可靠性的评估标准是错误的。通过分析真实Sentinel-2影像数据，作者发现即使平均上能多传1-2张图像，在个别过境中仍有25-33%的概率比均匀功率方案更差；即使是完全知道天气的"精灵"也无济于事。作者提出基于"回火频率"来设置安全裕度的方法，只保留被证明极少回火的配置，该方法能拒绝学习型编解码器的调度，并为具有更"陡峭悬崖"性能曲线的编解码器提供认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星仅有几分钟时间在有限的功率预算内下行传输图像，不同时刻信道质量差异巨大。现有方法基于天气预报统计和平均性能来规划调度，但这对每次过境只有一次尝试机会的卫星来说是不合适的——平均表现好不代表单次表现好，现有目标函数设定是错误的。

Method: 作者在真实Sentinel-2影像上评估了调度器性能，对比均匀功率分配方案，测量了个别过境中的失败比例。然后提出通过历史记录数据来量化调度计划"回火"（即比均匀功率更差）的频率，仅保留能证明极少回火的调度设置，从而更合理地设置安全裕度。

Result: 实验表明：平均上每个过境多获得1-2张图像的学习型调度器，在四分之一到三分之一的个别过境中仍然输给均匀功率方案；即使是拥有完美天气信息的"精灵"调度器也无法解决这一问题。所提出的认证方法会拒绝学习型编解码器的调度，而能为性能曲线有更陡峭"悬崖"的编解码器提供调度认证。

Conclusion: 对于单次尝试的卫星下行任务，平均性能目标函数具有误导性，评估标准应转向衡量回火频率。只有通过历史数据验证回火率极低的调度方案才应被采用，这为可靠卫星调度提供了新的认证思路。

Abstract: A low-Earth-orbit satellite has only minutes to downlink imagery on a limited power budget, and some moments in a pass carry far better channels than others. Spending more power on the good moments and skipping hopeless ones should deliver more images, but the channel cannot be measured in time, so the schedule is planned in advance from weather statistics using a safety margin. That margin is sized so the forecast is statistically reliable, and the scheduler is judged by whether it beats uniform power on average. Both targets are wrong for a satellite that gets one attempt per pass: on real Sentinel-2 imagery, a scheduler gaining one to two images per pass on average still delivers fewer than uniform power on a quarter to a third of individual passes, and a genie knowing the weather exactly does no better. We instead size the margin by measuring, on recorded passes, how often it would have backfired, keeping only settings that provably backfire rarely. The procedure declines to schedule our learned codec and certifies scheduling for codecs with a sharper cliff.

</details>


### [354] [SheafIQ: Sheaf-Theoretic Information Quantification of Vector Fields on Geometric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08728)
*Cong Shen,Guancen Lin,Chuan-Shen Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出SheafIQ框架，利用层论（sheaf theory）将图上节点向量映射到边关联坐标系中，度量局部不兼容性的残差能量分布，并通过熵来量化其全局组织性，拓展了传统图分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要刻画图拓扑结构或单个信号特征，缺乏对图结构中节点向量间局部交互如何全局组织的量化手段，需要一种能同时结合图拓扑与向量信号的新框架。

Method: 提出SheafIQ框架：采用层论表示将相邻向量映射至共享的边关联坐标系，将局部不兼容性转化为残差能量分布，再利用熵来量化该分布的全局组织性。

Result: 在蛋白质、功能性脑网络、城市交通系统和电网等多种系统中，SheafIQ均揭示了超越传统图与信号描述符的互补性组织信息，验证了其广泛适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: SheafIQ建立了统一的信息论框架，用于量化几何图上向量值状态的全局组织结构，将网络分析从单纯的图拓扑层面扩展到更丰富的向量信号-拓扑耦合维度。

Abstract: Vector fields on graph structures naturally arise in diverse biological and engineered systems, where vector-valued states are defined on the nodes and evolve through the network interactions. Existing methods primarily characterize either the graph topology or individual signals, but generally do not quantify how local interactions among node-associated vectors are organized across the graph. To address this limitation, a sheaf-theoretic framework, termed SheafIQ, is proposed to represent neighboring vectors in a common edge-associated coordinate system, map local incompatibilities to a residual energy distribution, and quantify its global organization through entropy. Across proteins, functional brain networks, urban traffic systems, and power grids, SheafIQ consistently reveals complementary organizational information beyond conventional graph- and signal-based descriptors. More broadly, it establishes a unified information-theoretic framework for quantifying the organization of vector-valued states on geometric graphs, extending network analysis beyond graph topology alone.

</details>


### [355] [Sensing-Induced Embodied Communication in the Near Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08731)
*Jingreng Lei,Yulin Shao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了近场条件下"传感诱发具身通信"（sensing-induced embodied communication）的基本通信极限，其中智能体通过将消息映射到三维有界区域内可控散射体的位置来实现通信，基站通过多快照单站感知回波解码位置。近场球面波前同时提供角度和距离分辨，将符号空间从2D平面扩展到3D体积，但引入了位置依赖的各向异性可靠性几何。作者通过逐对Bhattacharyya距离刻画该几何，推导出局部椭球表示，并将有限快照ε-容量转化为3D装箱问题，通过面心立方构造提供可达速率与几何上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通信感知一体化（ISAC）将蜂窝基础设施转变为物理世界的主动观测者，但当基础设施与能够主动改变自身状态及周围环境的具身智能体交互时，物理世界本身可以成为通信媒介。已有研究主要关注远场2D平面上的这种传感诱发通信，而近场球面波前带来的3D容量增益及其各向异性可靠性几何尚未被系统刻画，缺少统一的理论框架。

Method: 本文提出一个数学分析框架：(1) 利用逐对Bhattacharyya距离刻画近场3D符号空间中位置依赖的各向异性混淆区域，推导出局部椭球表示，其主轴量化方向性感知分辨率；(2) 证明该椭球在远场极限下退化为2D横向椭圆，统一近场与远场两种体制；(3) 将有限快照ε-容量下的可靠码本设计转化为3D装箱问题，采用面心立方（FCC）晶格构造实现可达速率，同时通过几何对偶推导互补上界。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提出的几何框架的准确性，并展示了近场三维体积装箱相对于远场二维平面装箱的容量增益，确立了通信感知一体化中"通过刻意配置的物理状态进行通信"的统一几何与信息论框架。

Conclusion: 本文为近场传感诱发具身通信建立了统一的几何与信息论分析框架，揭示了从2D平面到3D体积的符号空间扩展及其代价（各向异性可靠性几何），为设计物理状态编码通信系统提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication is turning the cellular infrastructure into an active observer of the physical world. When such infrastructure interacts with embodied agents capable of deliberately changing their states and surroundings, the physical world itself can become a communication medium. This paper studies the fundamental communication limits of this sensing-induced embodied communication paradigm in the near field. We consider an agent that maps messages to the positions of a controllable scatterer within a bounded three-dimensional (3D) region, while a base station decodes the selected position from multi-snapshot monostatic sensing echoes. Near-field spherical wavefronts resolve both angle and range, expanding the embodied-symbol space from a 2D plane to a 3D volume. This gain, however, comes with a position-dependent and anisotropic reliability geometry. We characterize this geometry through the pairwise Bhattacharyya distance and derive a local ellipsoidal representation of the resulting 3D confusability regions, whose principal axes quantify directional sensing resolution. The ellipsoid further degenerates into the 2D transverse ellipse in the far-field limit, unifying the two regimes. We then formulate the finite-snapshot $ε$-capacity and translate reliable codebook design into a 3D packing problem. A face-centered cubic construction provides an achievable rate, while a geometric converse yields a complementary upper bound. Numerical results validate the proposed geometry and demonstrate the capacity gain of near-field volumetric packing over far-field planar packing. These results establish a unified geometric and information-theoretic framework for communication through deliberately configured physical states.

</details>


### [356] [Bounds and constructions for constant/low-power error-correcting cooling codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08742)
*Shuangqing Liu,Tingting Tong,Menglong Zhang,Binwei Zhao,Yuhao Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了片上总线通信中用于同时控制峰值温度与平均功耗的低功耗纠错冷却（LPECC）码和恒定功率纠错冷却（CPECC）码。利用图论方法建立了(n,1,w,w-2)-CPECC码和(n,t,w,w-2)-CPECC码的新上界，并通过组合构型构造了多族最优CPECC码，完全解决了已有文献中关于CPECC码的猜想。此外，通过概率方法推导了LPECC码的新上界，构造了新的最优族，并建立了最优LPECC码与最优CPECC码之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 片上总线在传输信息时面临峰值温度过高和平均功耗过大的问题，同时还需要提供纠错能力。LPECC码和CPECC码是两种兼顾温度控制、功率控制和纠错能力的编码方案。当前研究中，这两类码的最优性上界尚不够紧，且已有若干构造问题与猜想未解决，因此需要建立更紧的界并构造最优码族。

Method: 本文采用图论技术（图论中的独立数、覆盖数等参数来刻画码的参数界）建立CPECC码的新上界；利用组合构型（combinatorial configurations）构造新的最优CPECC码族；采用概率方法（probabilistic method）推导(n,t,w,w-2)-LPECC码的新上界；并通过构造最优LPECC码与最优CPECC码之间的映射关系建立两者间的等价联系，即证明最优(n,t,w,w-2)-LPECC码与最优(n+1,t,w,w-2)-CPECC码之间的对应关系。

Result: 建立了(n,1,w,w-2)-CPECC码和(n,t,w,w-2)-CPECC码的新的图论上界；构造了若干新的最优CPECC码族；完全解决并证实了已有论文中关于CPECC码的猜想；通过概率方法获得了(n,t,w,w-2)-LPECC码的新上界并构造了新的最优族；证明了最优(n,t,w,w-2)-LPECC码与最优(n+1,t,w,w-2)-CPECC码之间的等价关系。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上完善了LPECC码和CPECC码的码字体积上界研究，解决了公开猜想，并通过揭示两类码之间的结构联系，为实际片上总线设计中同时兼顾温度、功耗与纠错的编码方案提供了更优的理论基础和最优构造。

Abstract: The low-power error-correcting cooling (LPECC) codes and constant-power error-correcting cooling (CPECC) codes, introduced in [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, 64 (2018), 3062--3085; 66 (2020), 4804--4818], respectively, are two coding schemes designed to simultaneously control the peak temperature and average power consumption of on-chip buses while providing error-correction capability for transmitted information. This paper establishes new upper bounds for both $(n,1,w,w-2)$-CPECC codes and $(n,t,w,w-2)$-CPECC codes using graph-theoretic techniques, and constructs several new families of optimal CPECC codes using combinatorial configurations. Moreover, it completely resolves the conjecture concerning CPECC codes posed in [IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory, DOI: 10.1109/TIT.2026.3721101]. Finally, we derive a new upper bound for $(n,t,w,w-2)$-LPECC codes by probabilistic method, along with new optimal families, and establish the relationship between optimal $(n,t,w,w-2)$-LPECC codes and optimal $(n+1,t,w,w-2)$-CPECC codes.

</details>


### [357] [Universally optimal (wiretap) codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09040)
*Madhura Pathegama,Alexander Barg*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明通用最优（UO）码在无噪声主信道的窃听信道场景中，能够优化α=2时的α-互信息，从而表明UO码在此类窃听信道上具有最优传输性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UO码在2007年由Cohn和Kumar提出，后由Cohn和Zhao扩展到离散场景，它们能在一类势函数下最小化同规模码集合的能量。然而，目前仅有少数特定函数与信息论问题建立了联系，作者希望通过发现新的联系来扩展UO码在信息论中的应用范围。

Method: 作者将UO码与窃听信道中的α-互信息（α=2）建立联系，具体在无噪声主信道的窃听信道场景下，证明UO码能够优化α-互信息。

Result: 证明UO码在无噪声主信道窃听信道中优化α=2的α-互信息，从而推导出UO码在此类窃听信道上具有最优传输性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作为UO码在信息论中的应用新增了一个具体实例，表明UO码在窃听信道传输中具有最优性，进一步扩展了UO码的应用范围。

Abstract: Universally optimal (UO) codes were introduced by H. Cohn and A. Kumar in 2007 and later extended to the discrete setting by Cohn and Y. Zhao. They minimize the ``energy'' among all codes of the same size for a certain class of potential functions. So far only a small number of specific functionals have been linked to information theory problems. We add one more, showing that UO codes optimize $α$-mutual information for $α=2$ in the context of wiretap channels with a noiseless main channel. This implies that UO codes are optimal for transmission over this type of wiretap channels.

</details>


### [358] [Quasi-MSRD Codes and Their Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09085)
*Qingfeng Xia,Fang-Wei Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了和秩度量码中的准MRD（QMSRD）码与对偶准MRD码。由于MSRD码在某些维度下不存在，作者引入QMSRD码概念，进而定义对偶QMSRD码，并刻画了这类码的性质，推导其支撑、秩列和和秩分布，以及计算广义和秩权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 和秩度量码在网络编码、空时码和分布式存储中有重要应用。MSRD码是达到和秩度量Singleton界的优码，但并非所有维度下都存在MSRD码，因此需要引入更广泛的准MRD码概念来填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先引入准MSRD（QMSRD）码的定义并提供其性质；其次发现并非所有QMSRD码的对偶码都是QMSRD码，因此进一步定义对偶QMSRD码；最后通过系统的数学刻画，推导此类码的支撑（support）分布、秩列（rank-list）分布、和秩（sum-rank）分布，并计算其广义和秩权重。

Result: 成功定义并刻画了QMSRD码与对偶QMSRD码，证明了QMSRD码对偶性质并非必然成立，并给出了对偶QMSRD码的完整刻画，包括其支撑、秩列及和秩分布，以及广义和秩权重的计算公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为和秩度量码理论提供了新的码类（QMSRD码与对偶QMSRD码），弥补了MSRD码在某些维度下不存在的缺陷，并为后续关于和秩度量码的结构分析与实际应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Sum-rank-metric codes have recently attracted considerable attention of many researchers, due to their applications in network coding, space-time codes and distributed storage. MSRD codes are those good codes attaining the Singleton bound in the sum-rank metric. However, MSRD codes do not exist for some dimensions. Motivated by this fact, we introduce the notion of quasi-MSRD (QMSRD) codes and provide some properties of them. What is more, we find that not every QMSRD code has a QMSRD dual code, so we give the definition of dually QMSRD codes, whose dual codes and themselves are both QMSRD. Finally, we characterize such codes, derive their support, rank-list and sum-rank distributions as well as compute their generalized sum-rank weights.

</details>


### [359] [Federated Unlearning Over Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09090)
*Yixuan Chen,Zhouxiang Zhao,Mingzhe Chen,Wei Xu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Zhaohui Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了无线联邦学习网络（FUN）中的延迟最小化问题，提出了一个联合建模收敛行为、本地设备计算动态和CSI误差下鲁棒传输的系统模型，并通过基于单调性和凸性分解的迭代算法，利用嵌套二分法和黄金分割搜索优化延迟、带宽、功率和计算频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦遗忘（FU）是满足严格数据隐私法规的关键范式，但其在无线网络中的实现面临迭代校准和物理层信道不确定性带来的严重通信延迟与可靠性挑战，亟需针对性的延迟最小化方案。

Method: 建立联合系统模型（FUN算法收敛行为、本地设备计算动态、有界CSI误差下的最坏情况鲁棒传输模型）；将该非凸联合资源分配问题利用系统约束的单调性和凸性进行分解，通过对本地精度参数的均匀扫描分解问题，并在各子问题中利用嵌套二分法和黄金分割搜索确定最优延迟、带宽、功率和计算频率。

Result: 理论与数值结果均表明，所提算法实现了多项式复杂度，相比常规基线方案显著缩短了整体遗忘完成时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于凸性分解和高效搜索的迭代算法能够在无线联邦遗忘网络中实现最优资源分配，在多项式复杂度内显著降低卸载完成延迟，为FU在无线场景下的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: To comply with stringent data privacy regulations, federated unlearning (FU) has emerged as a critical paradigm. However, its implementation over wireless networks introduces severe communication latency and reliability challenges due to iterative calibration requirements and physical-layer channel uncertainties. In this paper, we investigate the problem of delay minimization for federated unlearning networks (FUN). Specifically, we establish a comprehensive system model that jointly incorporates the convergence behavior of the FUN algorithm, local device computation dynamics, and a worst-case robust transmission model operating under bounded channel state information (CSI) error. To solve the resulting non-convex joint resource allocation problem, we propose an efficient iterative algorithm. By exploiting the monotonicity and convexity properties of the system constraints, the problem is decomposed via a uniform scan over the local accuracy parameter, within which the optimal delay, bandwidth, power, and computation frequency are determined utilizing nested bisection and golden-section searches. Both theoretical analysis and extensive numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieves polynomial complexity and significantly reduces the overall unlearning completion time compared to conventional baseline schemes.

</details>


### [360] [Quantum Bicycle LDPC Codes with High $kd^2/n$ from Divisor-Driven Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09115)
*Liangdong Lu,Guanmin Guo,Yang Liu,Ruipan Yang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将双圈环量子LDPC码（bicycle codes）在循环情形下简化为多项式环F2[x]/(x^l−1)上的代数框架：自正交性自动成立，量子维度由多项式gcd直接读出，最小距离通过Calderbank对应到F4上加法码精确验证。基于此框架的计算机搜索恢复了[[42,12,4]]2和[[62,12,4]]2短码，并产出包括[[66,20,7]]2（kd^2/n=14.85，优于bivariate bicycle码[[144,12,12]]2）在内的一系列高性价比码，同时在n=48处做了穷举普查，证明距离5会导致稳定子秩损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双圈环量子LDPC码是已知最优的小型量子码之一，但传统设计依赖群代数表述，维度与距离只能通过矩阵计算获得，缺乏理论洞见且搜索效率低下。需要一种代数化的简洁框架以系统探索双圈环码的参数空间，特别是现有码表覆盖不足的区域。

Method: 在循环情形下将构造坍缩到多项式环F2[x]/(x^l−1)：利用自正交性的自动成立简化构造条件，通过多项式gcd计算量子维度，并借助Calderbank对应将最小距离的精确验证转化为F4上加法码的验证问题。据此实现代数预过滤的枚举搜索，并在n=48处进行穷举普查以刻画参数边界。

Result: 恢复了[[42,12,4]]2和[[62,12,4]]2短码；获得一系列高性价比码，包括[[66,20,7]]2（kd^2/n=14.85，以不到bivariate bicycle码[[144,12,12]]2一半的块长超越其kd^2/n=12），以及[[46,2,8]]2、[[66,2,9]]2、[[66,4,8]]2、[[66,6,8]]2、[[90,16,6]]2、[[90,18,6]]2、[[90,20,6]]2。在n=48穷举中，用最小48元素群构造出[[48,10,6]]2码（较Aydin-Tamo-Barg的72元素更优），并证明距离5导致稳定子秩损失，排除[[48,10,5]]2。

Conclusion: 该框架为超出群论搜索范围的双圈环型量子LDPC码提供了系统化的代数构造路径，并准确界定了真正需要coset理论现象出现的参数边界。

Abstract: Bicycle (two-block circulant) quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes include some of the best known small quantum codes, yet their design has relied on group-algebra formulations in which the dimension and distance are accessible only through matrix computation. We show that in the cyclic case the construction collapses into the polynomial ring $\F_2[x]/(x^{l}-1)$: self-orthogonality is automatic, the quantum dimension is read off from a polynomial gcd, and the minimum distance is certified exactly through the Calderbank correspondence to additive codes over $\F_4$, turning code search into an algebraically pre-filtered enumeration that reaches parameter regimes poorly covered by existing tables. A computer search based on this framework recovers the short codes $[[42,12,4]]_2$ and $[[62,12,4]]_2$ and produces a family of codes with competitive figure of merit $kd^2/n$, including $[[66,20,7]]_2$ with $kd^2/n=14.85$, above the bivariate bicycle code $[[144,12,12]]_2$ ($kd^2/n=12$) at less than half the block length, together with $[[46,2,8]]_2$, $[[66,2,9]]_2$, $[[66,4,8]]_2$, $[[66,6,8]]_2$ and, at $n=90$, $[[90,16,6]]_2$, $[[90,18,6]]_2$, $[[90,20,6]]_2$. An exhaustive census at $n=48$ delineates the boundary of this picture: we exhibit a $[[48,10,6]]_2$ code from a minimal $48$-element group (the Aydin--Tamo--Barg realization uses $72$ elements), and prove that distance $5$ forces a stabilizer-rank loss, which excludes $[[48,10,5]]_2$ from the weight-$8$ symmetric coset family. The framework thus opens a systematic route to bicycle-type quantum LDPC codes beyond the reach of group-theoretic searches, and identifies exactly where genuinely coset-theoretic phenomena begin.

</details>


### [361] [Demand-Aware Cooperative Transmission Design for Energy-Efficient LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09148)
*Wooseok Cha,Kyeongsoo Kim,Seonghoon Kim,Junil Choi,Jihwan P. Choi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络的协作传输框架，联合考虑非均匀业务需求和全网功耗，通过混合预编码、射频链激活和硬件量化设计，以业务感知的能量效率为优化目标，采用两阶段算法（改进交叉熵法+分数规划）求解混合整数非线性规划问题，数值结果验证了其优于基准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络覆盖范围广导致业务需求在空间上非均匀分布，同时星上功率约束严格，传统高功耗传输架构不适用，亟需能有效利用稀缺星上资源的节能传输策略，实现业务感知与网络功耗的联合优化。

Method: 提出协作传输框架：每颗LEO卫星集成混合预编码（HPC）、射频链激活和硬件量化；基于统计信道状态信息（sCSI）和业务需求组织以用户设备（UE）为中心的卫星簇；构建联合优化协作传输架构与资源分配的问题，最大化业务感知的能量效率（EE），将该问题建模为MINLP，并开发两阶段算法，其中改进的交叉熵（CE）方法搜索离散变量，分数规划用于发射功率分配。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提框架在同时考虑业务需求和能量效率的情况下，性能优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的业务感知协作传输框架能够在满足非均匀业务需求和严格功率约束的条件下实现高能量效率，为LEO卫星网络提供了有效的节能传输方案。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks are envisioned as a promising solution for providing ubiquitous connectivity and narrowing the digital divide. The extensive footprint of LEO satellite constellations enables broad coverage, resulting in spatially non-uniform traffic demand across the serviced areas. Meanwhile, stringent on-board power constraints make power-intensive transmission architectures less attractive and motivate energy-efficient transmission strategies that effectively exploit scarce satellite network resources. To this end, this paper proposes a cooperative transmission framework that jointly accounts for non-uniform traffic demand and network-wide power consumption. Each LEO satellite integrates hybrid precoding (HPC), radio frequency (RF) chain activation, and hardware quantization, while user-equipment (UE)-centric satellite clusters are organized using statistical channel state information (sCSI) and traffic demands. A framework for joint optimization of cooperative transmission architecture and resource allocation is designed to maximize demand-aware energy efficiency (EE), resulting in a mixed-integer nonlinear program (MINLP) for which finding a globally optimal solution is generally intractable. Accordingly, a two-stage algorithm is developed under a distributed linear precoding structure, in which a modified cross-entropy (CE) method searches over discrete variables, while fractional programming is employed for transmit power allocation. Numerical results indicate that the proposed framework outperforms benchmark schemes while accounting for traffic demands and EE.

</details>


### [362] [Optimal Movable-Antenna Control for Multi-Path Sensing Guided by Prior AoA Statistic (extended version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09165)
*Jaehong Kim,Changsheng You,Jihong Park,Seung-Woo Ko*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于先验引导的6G敏捷多路径感知框架，利用弱先验到达角（AoA）统计信息，通过一次三维平板朝向优化和两次线性扫描实现多路径的AoA和到达时间（ToA）估计，大幅降低控制开销和感知延迟，同时达到接近单路径基准的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可移动天线（MA）通过机械运动合成孔径可实现多路径感知，但现有方法依赖对全部可移动区域的穷举扫描，导致巨大的控制开销和感知延迟，限制了其在6G敏捷感知场景中的实用性。需要一个能显著减少扫描次数同时保持多路径感知性能的方案。

Method: 该框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 利用Fisher信息分析，仅优化一次可移动平板的3D朝向，以配置一个机械上可行的扫描区域，增强路径可见性并保持路径间可区分性；(2) 在最优平板朝向下，MA仅执行两次线性扫描，将非共线的空间相位投影与先验AoA统计信息通过最大后验（MAP）估计器融合，恢复多路径的仰角和方位角AoA；随后利用估计的AoA增强目标路径分量并抑制其他路径干扰，提取各路径的ToA。

Result: 仅需一次朝向调整和两次线性扫描，该框架即可实现敏捷多路径感知，显著降低控制开销和延迟；AoA和ToA的估计精度接近单路径基准的性能。

Conclusion: 该先验引导的框架在保持高精度多路径感知的同时，将扫描开销从穷举扫描压缩到两次线性扫描，为6G敏捷感知提供了切实可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Multi-path sensing, which aims to extract the geometric attributes of multiple propagation paths, is expected to be a key functionality of 6G. A movable antenna (MA) can enable this functionality by synthesizing an aperture through mechanical motion. However, existing MA-based sensing methods typically rely on exhaustive scanning over the entire movable region, resulting in significant control overhead and sensing latency, which limit their practicality for agile sensing. To address this challenge, this paper develops a prior-guided agile multi-path sensing framework that leverages weak prior angle-of-arrival (AoA) statistics as side information. The proposed framework is built on two key steps. First, the movable plate's three-dimensional orientation is optimized only once to configure a mechanically feasible scan region that enhances path visibility while preserving inter-path discriminability, guided by Fisher information analysis. Second, given the optimal plate orientation, the MA performs only two linear scans, whose non-collinear spatial phase projections are fused with the prior AoA statistics through a maximum a posteriori (MAP)-based estimator to recover the elevation and azimuth AoAs of multiple paths. The estimated AoAs are subsequently used to extract the times-of-arrival (ToAs) by enhancing the target path component while suppressing interference from other paths. With only one orientation adjustment and two linear scans, the proposed framework enables agile multi-path sensing with significantly reduced control overhead and latency, while achieving AoA and ToA estimation accuracy close to the single-path benchmark.

</details>


### [363] [Modeling and Performance Analysis for Fluid Antenna System Enabled UAV Near-Field Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09179)
*Hao Jiang,Wangqi Shi,Zhentian Zhang,Xusheng Zhu,Kai-Kit Wong,Hyundung Shin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无人机空地通信的动态端口可重构近场信道模型，并设计了基于贪心策略的FAS自适应子阵划分方案，用于准确刻画FAS辅助UAV-移动用户链路的信道特性，同时降低计算复杂度并提升系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信道模型难以捕捉FAS的动态端口配置特性以及无人机通信的近场特性，无法准确描述FAS辅助无人机空地通信链路中信道的非均匀空间分布，需要一种更精确的动态近场信道模型。

Method: 提出动态端口可重构近场信道模型，将视距与非视距分量分解并与无人机运动动态及FAS端口激活状态集成；开发基于贪心策略的FAS自适应子阵划分方案，通过动态更新算法高效处理端口切换时的子阵调整；采用基于信道增益的端口选择策略以避免低有效增益和深衰落端口。

Result: 推导并分析了建模精度与信道容量，研究了FAS尺寸、端口间距、激活端口数量和无人机动态对系统性能的影响；评估了子阵划分方案的计算复杂度，验证了其在实时应用中的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的信道模型和子阵划分方案能够准确刻画近场信道特性、显著降低计算复杂度，为动态场景下FAS的设计与分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FASs) offer a promising solution for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) air-to-ground (A2G) communications by enabling reconfigurable radiation characteristics. Addressing the limitations of traditional models in capturing the dynamic port configuration of FAS and the near-field nature of UAV communications, this paper proposes a dynamic port-reconfigurable near-field channel model for FAS-assisted UAV-to-mobile user (MU) links. Furthermore, we develop a FAS-adaptive subarray partition scheme utilizing a greedy strategy. By decomposing line-of-sight (LoS) and non-line-of-sight (NLoS) components and integrating UAV motion dynamics with FAS port activation states, the proposed model accurately characterizes the non-uniform spatial distribution of near-field channels. The subarray partition scheme dynamically groups active ports to satisfy near-field conditions while significantly reducing computational complexity, supported by a dynamic update algorithm that efficiently handles subarray adjustments during port switching. To avoid low effective gain and deep-fading ports in dense FAS configurations, a channel gain-based selection strategy is employed to prioritize high-gain ports. We derive and analyze the modeling accuracy and channel capacity, investigating the impact of FAS dimensions, port spacing, active port count, and UAV dynamics on system performance. Finally, the computational complexity of the subarray partition scheme is evaluated, verifying its advantages for real-time applications and providing a theoretical foundation for the design and analysis of FAS in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [364] [Binary code rate bounds via classical--quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09347)
*Omar Alrabiah,Venkatesan Guruswami*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文从单一定理"pretty good criterion"推导出二进制码的四个主要渐近率-距离权衡（Plotkin、Elias-Bassalygo及两个MRRW界），证明这些经典界均可由量子测量（PGM）下的后验比特错误率约束导出。随后用新的信道（混合量子比特信道MQC及掩码混合量子比特信道2MQC）在0<δ<1/2区间内严格改进两个MRRW界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典编码理论中的四个主要率-距离上界（Plotkin、Elias-Bassalygo、两个MRRW界）表面上独立推导、彼此无关，缺乏统一的原理性框架。作者希望揭示这些界背后的共同数学结构，并据此寻找超越现有界限的新途径。

Method: 提出"pretty good criterion"定理：对于二元输入输出对称的经典-量子（cq）信道，若在pretty good measurement（PGM，即量子版后验采样）下的比特错误率低于δ，则任意长度n、相对距离δ的二元码（线性或非线性）其码率至多为信道容量，修正项为O(n^{-1/2})。由此将率-距离界问题转化为信道设计问题（最小化容量并满足后验错误率约束）。用BEC得到Plotkin界，BSC得到Elias-Bassalygo界，纯态信道（PSC）得第一个MRRW界，掩码PSC得第二个MRRW界。随后引入新的混合态信道MQC和2MQC来严格改进两个MRRW界。

Result: 证明了四个经典界均能从"pretty good criterion"统一推出；构造了新的混合量子信道MQC和2MQC，在0<δ<1/2整个区间内严格改进了两个MRRW界，实现了几十年来的界改进。

Conclusion: 本文建立了经典编码界与量子测量之间的深层联系，提供了一个统一的框架来推导和超越现有的率-距离权衡。通过设计新的量子信道（MQC和2MQC），成功在对两个MRRW界的关键区间内实现了严格改进，展现了量子信息方法对经典编码理论的强大反向推动力。

Abstract: We derive the four principal asymptotic rate-distance tradeoffs for binary codes---Plotkin, Elias--Bassalygo, and the two McEliece--Rodemich--Rumsey--Welch (MRRW) bounds---from one theorem, the ``pretty good criterion.'' If the bit error rate under the pretty good measurement (PGM)---the quantum analog of posterior sampling---of a binary-input output-symmetric classical--quantum (cq) channel lies below $δ$, then every length-$n$ binary code, linear or nonlinear, of relative distance $δ$ has rate at most the channel's capacity, up to an $O(n^{-1/2})$ correction. Rate--distance bounds thereby reduce to a channel design problem, wherein the task is to minimize channel capacity subject to the posterior bit error rate constraint. Via the pretty good criterion, the binary erasure channel (BEC) yields Plotkin, the binary symmetric channel (BSC) yields Elias--Bassalygo, the pure-state channel (PSC) yields the first MRRW bound, and a masked PSC yields the second MRRW bound exactly.
  This framework is then instantiated with new channels to improve upon the MRRW bounds. Specifically, the mixed-qubit channel (MQC), a mixed-state version of PSC, strictly improves the first MRRW bound at every $0 < δ< \frac{1}{2}$, while the masked mixed-qubit channel (2MQC) strictly improves the second MRRW bound throughout the same interval.

</details>


### [365] [Orbital Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09362)
*Kuranage Roche Rayan Ranasinghe,Giuseppe Thadeu Freitas de Abreu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出轨道检测（OD）框架，通过将离散符号先验松弛为混合离散-连续密度，为数字调制MIMO系统设计渐近最优、低复杂度的消息传递（MP）接收机，将最优去噪器的复杂度从O(M)降至O(1)，并提供五个信息论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMO系统中设计渐近最优且低复杂度的消息传递接收机是一个核心挑战。传统方法需要在最优去噪性能（O(M)复杂度）与简化方案（如线性接收机）之间做出权衡，尚缺乏一种既能保持渐近最优性又能显著降低复杂度的统一框架。

Method: 提出轨道检测（OD）框架：将离散符号先验松弛为混合离散-连续密度，将每个符号的分布因式分解为离散径向分量（仅支持L<<M个幅度环）和连续最大熵相位密度。由此将传播的后验均值和方差无损压缩为3L个实标量，推导出O(L)进而O(1)复杂度的闭式去噪器层级（OBD、OGD、OPD），并通过Jacobi-Anger梯子以几何衰减误差恢复精确检测器。

Result: 五个信息论结果：1) OBD/OGD/OPD具有相同的首阶状态演化（SE）不动点；2) 高信噪比下误差衰减律从指数变为线性但不形成错误平台；3) 欠载系统中速率损失随SNR指数消失，达到星座约束下容量；4) 达到MMSE维度d=1/2；5) 建立了Wasserstein距离下非渐近最优传输界将星座环几何与可达速率联系起来。

Conclusion: OD框架为MP接收机设计提供了从O(M)到O(1)复杂度的无信息损失的压缩路径，同时保持渐近最优性并以明确的信息论代价（高SNR下线性误差衰减、无错误平台）换取极低复杂度，且在任何欠载系统中仍能渐近达到星座约束容量。

Abstract: We introduce orbital detection (OD), a framework for designing asymptotically optimal, low-complexity message passing (MP) receivers for digitally modulated multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, based on relaxing the discrete symbol prior into a mixed discrete-continuous density. The resulting orbital prior factors each symbol's distribution into a discrete radial component, supported on only the \(L << M\) amplitude rings of an arbitrary constellation \(\mathcal{M}\) of cardinality \(M = |\mathcal{M}|\), and a continuous, maximum-entropy phase density on each ring. This compresses the propagated posterior mean and variance losslessly into \(3L\) real scalars, and collapses the optimal \(\mathcal{O}(M)\)-complexity denoiser into a closed-form hierarchy whose per-symbol cost falls to \(\mathcal{O}(L)\) and ultimately \(\mathcal{O}(1)\): the orbital Bessel denoiser (OBD), its Bessel-free variant the orbital Gaussian denoiser (OGD), and the orbital phase denoiser (OPD), proved irreducible on the ring manifold. A Jacobi-Anger ladder recovers the exact detector with geometrically vanishing error. Five information-theoretic results follow. First, the OBD, OGD, and OPD share an identical leading-order state evolution (SE) fixed point. Second, the sole price is a change in the high-SNR error-decay law, from exponential to linear, which never hardens into an error floor. Third, for any underloaded system the induced rate loss vanishes exponentially in SNR, so every level is asymptotically capacity-achieving in the constellation-constrained sense, attaining \(\log_2 M\). Fourth, OD attains a minimum mean square error (MMSE) dimension \(d=1/2\), halfway between the \(d=0\) Bayes-optimal denoiser (BOD) and the \(d=1\) linear receiver. Fifth, a non-asymptotic optimal-transport bound in Wasserstein distance links constellation ring geometry directly to the achievable rate.

</details>


### [366] [Quantum-Classical Coexistence Network Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09364)
