*Xuchuang Wang,Joseph C. Chapman,Aneesh Ramaswamy,Matheus Guedes de Andrade,Yu-Zhen Janice Chen,Joseph M. Lukens,Gayane Vardoyan,Don Towsley*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于量子-经典共存网络（QCN）的层析成像框架，仅凭端到端测量即可推断每链路信道参数。框架将信号演化分解为光子损耗、成功传输和三个方向相关的去极化分量，并为星型拓扑推导了闭式链路级估计器，配合简单的信号方向切换协议解决未知量。在实验数据上验证了单链路估计精度，并通过仿真验证了任意拓扑（树和网格）的泛化扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCN通过在现有电信基础设施上实现量子-经典信号共享光纤来进行量子通信，但同向和反向传播的经典流量会引入不同的去极化噪声，导致信道表征复杂化。已有的端到端测量无法直接获得每个链路的信道参数，需要一种能够从端到端测量中推断每链路参数的框架。

Method: (1) 将共存的每根光纤建模为光子损耗、成功传输和三个方向依赖的去极化分量的分解；(2) 推导链路级闭式估计器；(3) 对星型拓扑网络，通过端节点对的乘法方程组系统扩展，配合经典信号方向切换协议解析剩余未知量；(4) 扩展为两种方向：一是将共光纤信道因子化为去极化损耗信号信道与拉曼噪声注入信道的完全正迹保持张量积；二是通过剥皮算法（树拓扑）和最小二乘估计器（网格拓扑）泛化到任意拓扑。

Result: 在单链路实验测试台上，去极化概率估计准确，估计的过程保真度在不同光纤长度和波长下紧密跟踪贝叶斯过程层析成像基线；残差差距源于去极化近似。星型网络估计器在由实测单链路信道构建的仿真路径上验证。蒙特卡洛仿真在树和循环网格网络上验证了拓扑泛化方法。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种从端到端测量推断QCN每链路信道参数的有效手段，在实验和仿真层面均得到验证，并可灵活扩展到星型、树和网格等不同拓扑结构，为实际QCN的信道监测和量子通信部署提供了支持。

Abstract: Quantum-classical coexistence networks (QCNs) share optical fiber between quantum and classical signals via wavelength-division multiplexing, offering a practical path to quantum communication over existing telecom infrastructure. However, co- and counter-propagating classical traffic introduce distinct depolarization noise, complicating channel characterization. We develop a tomography framework that infers per-link channel parameters of a QCN from end-to-end measurements alone. We first model each coexisting fiber by decomposing the signal evolution into photon loss, successful transmission, and three direction-dependent depolarization components. We then derive closed-form link-level estimators, and extend the approach to star-topology networks through a system of multiplicative equations across end-node pairs, together with a simple classical-signal-direction-switching protocol that resolves the remaining unknowns. On single-link experimental testbed data, we recover per-link depolarization probabilities accurately, with estimated process fidelities closely tracking the Bayesian-process-tomography baseline across multiple fiber lengths and wavelengths; residual gaps reflect the depolarization-only approximation. Absent a multi-link coexistence testbed, we validate the star-network estimators on emulated paths built from measured single-link channels. We further extend the framework in two directions: (i) a channel model that factorizes the coexisting fiber into a depolarizing-with-loss signal channel and a Raman-noise-injection channel on separate optical modes -- a completely-positive, trace-preserving tensor product -- whose link observables reduce exactly to our basic model; and (ii) a generalization to arbitrary topologies via a peeling algorithm (trees) and a least-squares estimator (meshes), validated by Monte-Carlo simulations on tree and cyclic-mesh networks.

</details>


### [367] [Intelligent Wiretap Code Design: Exploiting Wireless Endogenous Security via Information Theory and Deep Learning Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09387)
*Haibin Zhang,Xiangnan Zhou,Chao Wang,Liang Jin,Hao Xu,Yao Sun,Chonghua Wang,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在语义通信框架内提出了一种利用无线信道固有随机性的窃听编码方案，通过离散语义表示实现与传统数字调制兼容的联合安全和可靠性增强，并针对两种窃听场景分别采用互信息和广义互信息指导编码优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加密方法在无线通信中面临密钥分发和管理等挑战，而无线信道的固有随机性为通信安全提供了新的天然途径。本文旨在将这一思想引入语义通信框架，利用语义离散表示实现通信安全与可靠性的联合优化。

Method: 提出一种基于语义通信框架的窃听编码方案，将语义信息离散化以兼容传统数字调制。针对两种窃听场景分别建模：(i) 窃听者使用MAP解码器时，以互信息为指标设计优化编码策略以最小化信息泄露；(ii) 窃听者拥有与合法接收者相同的解码器但受解码能力限制时，使用广义互信息GMI刻画其可恢复性并指导可靠性感知的编码优化。

Result: 通过在不同窃听能力假设下分别采用互信息和GMI作为设计准则，实现了有效的编码优化，在保障合法通信可靠性的同时最小化了信息泄露。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在语义通信框架内利用无线信道随机性设计窃听编码方案的可行性，通过针对不同窃听场景选择合适的度量指标（MI或GMI），能够实现通信安全性与可靠性的联合优化。

Abstract: Recent advancements in wireless endogenous security have explored leveraging the inherent randomness of wireless channels to enhance communication security, providing an effective alternative to traditional encryption methods. This paper proposes a wiretap coding scheme within the semantic communication framework, which leverages discrete semantic representations compatible with conventional digital modulation to jointly enhance communication security and reliability. We investigate two eavesdropping scenarios: (i) the eavesdropper employs a maximum a posteriori (MAP) decoder, and (ii) the eavesdropper has access to a decoder identical to that of the legitimate receiver. In the first scenario, we exploit mutual information as a metric to guide the design of an optimized coding strategy, minimizing information leakage while enhancing communication reliability. In the second scenario, considering the limitations of the eavesdropper's decoding capability, we employ generalized mutual information (GMI) to characterize recoverability under the prescribed decoding rule and guide reliability-aware code optimization.

</details>


### [368] [Joint Age-of-Latent and Resource Minimization for Wireless Multi-Camera Perception With Temporal Window Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09411)
*Chih-Yu Lin,Wanjiun Liao,Sumudu Samarakoon,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出Age-of-Latent (AoL) 指标以量化多摄像头无线感知系统中任务相关潜在表征的新鲜度，并设计CoLA框架，联合优化任务级时间积分窗口(TWI)与槽级编码器选择、调度及NOMA功率分配，在预测可靠性约束下实现AoL与资源消耗的折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Age-of-Information (AoI)仅衡量最新接收更新的年龄，无法反映任务相关的潜在内容新鲜度。在多摄像头无线感知场景中，基站需要在有限上行资源下从分布式摄像头维持及时可靠的潜在信念，现有指标和方案未联合考虑更新机会、预测时长、提交时间AoL、预测可靠性与累积资源成本之间的权衡。

Method: 提出CoLA (Correlation-aware Latent prediction for AoL minimization) 框架：1) 定义AoL指标量化每个摄像头最新解码潜在表征的新鲜度；2) 利用有限时间积分窗口(TWI)确定任务承诺时间和可用上行时隙，利用冗余或重叠视角实现相关预测；3) 将问题建模为联合AoL-资源最小化问题，耦合任务级TWI选择与槽级编码器选择、调度和NOMA功率分配；4) 采用Lyapunov优化进行任务级TWI选择（基于AoL-资源代价和预测不确定性），采用近端策略优化(PPO)进行槽级资源控制。

Result: 在仓库多摄像头RF数据集上的实验结果表明，CoLA能够自适应调整TWI以适应摄像头更新可用性，在所有基线方法中实现了最优的AoL-资源折中，同时在长时间、严重摄像头中断情况下保持预测可靠性。

Conclusion: CoLA通过任务级和槽级的分层优化策略，有效解决了多摄像头无线感知中AoL与资源消耗的联合优化问题，验证了相关感知和自适应TWI机制在资源受限条件下的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-camera wireless perception requires a base station (BS) to maintain timely and reliable latent beliefs from distributed cameras under limited uplink resources. Conventional Age-of-Information (AoI) measures the age of the latest received update but not task-relevant latent content. We introduce Age-of-Latent (AoL) to quantify the freshness of each camera's latest decoded latent representation. A finite temporal window of integration (TWI) determines the task-commitment time and available uplink slots, creating a tradeoff among update opportunities, prediction duration, commit-time AoL, prediction reliability, and accumulated resource cost. Within this horizon, redundant or overlapping views enable correlated prediction, reducing reliance on the highest-cost communication and encoding configuration to maintain BS-side latent beliefs. We formulate a joint AoL-resource minimization problem coupling task-level TWI selection with slot-level encoder selection, scheduling, and NOMA power allocation under prediction-reliability constraints. We propose correlation-aware latent prediction for AoL minimization (CoLA), which uses Lyapunov optimization for task-level TWI selection based on AoL-resource cost and prediction uncertainty, and proximal policy optimization for slot-level resource control. Results on a warehouse multi-camera RF dataset show that CoLA adapts the TWI to camera-update availability and achieves the most favorable AoL-resource tradeoff among the benchmarks while maintaining prediction reliability, particularly under prolonged and severe camera outages.

</details>


### [369] [CSI Reconstruction in Fluid Antenna Systems Without Spatial Covariance Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09499)
*Zhentian Zhang,Kaitao Meng,Tuo Wu,Kai-Kit Wong,Hao Xu,Liang Liu,Pei Xiao,Chao Wang,Kin-Fai Tong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了流体天线系统（FAS）中在无需先验信道统计信息的情况下，从少量激活端口恢复全端口信道状态信息（CSI）的可行性问题。作者证明在Clarke各向同性散射模型下，信道位于由散射环境决定的低维空间模态子空间中，并建立了恢复可行性的尖锐阈值：当观测端口数M达到模态维度r时即可实现可靠恢复（即使M远小于总端口数N），低于该阈值则无论信噪比多高都无法可靠恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体天线系统（FAS）利用大量候选端口获得空间分集增益，但硬件约束使其只能观测少数激活端口上的信道。能否在不预先获取信道统计信息的情况下恢复全端口CSI，此前仍是一个悬而未决的问题。解决这一问题对于突破FAS的硬件瓶颈、实现实际部署至关重要。

Method: 基于Clarke各向同性散射模型，将信道分解到由散射环境决定的空间模态子空间，证明该子空间的维度r远小于总端口数N。通过将恢复误差分解为模态截断误差、估计误差和学习误差三个分量，推导出射频链数量、导频开销、发射功率和训练数据之间的显式权衡关系，并建立恢复可行性的理论下界。

Result: 证明了当观测端口数满足M≥r时，无需先验统计信息即可实现全端口CSI恢复（即使M远小于N）；确立了尖锐的可行性阈值：低于模态维度r时可靠恢复在任意SNR下都不可能，高于该阈值则增加观测数可提升精度；给出了各类资源之间的显式权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了可扩展的先验无关全端口CSI恢复方案，仅需少量激活端口即可完成，为流体天线系统的实际部署提供了理论基础，突破了传统方法对信道统计先验的依赖。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FASs) exploit many candidate ports for spatial diversity, but hardware constraints allow channel observations at only a few active ports. Whether full-port CSI can be recovered without pre-acquired channel statistics remains open. Under the Clarke isotropic scattering model, we show that the channel lies in a low-dimensional spatial modal subspace determined by the scattering environment rather than the total port count. Consequently, recovery becomes feasible when the number of observed ports reaches the modal dimension (i.e., $M\geq r$), even when $M\ll N$. We further establish a sharp feasibility threshold: reliable recovery is impossible below this dimension regardless of SNR, whereas accuracy improves with additional observations above it. By decomposing the recovery error into modal truncation, estimation, and learning components, we derive explicit tradeoffs among RF chains, pilot overhead, transmit power, and training data. These results enable scalable prior-free full-port CSI recovery with few active ports.

</details>


### [370] [Separating Abelian and Homomorphic Entropy Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09543)
*Shahram Khazaei*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了阿贝尔熵锥严格包含于同态熵锥（16变量时），并确定了两者首次出现差异的变量数范围在6到16之间。分离函数的构造基于Pálfy–Szabó六交叉恒等式的序对偶提升，并通过构造2类2-群的16个正规子群来消除所有连接误差，最终实现了阿贝尔与混合线性熵锥的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chan和Yeung已证明有限群足以判定齐次线性信息不等式是否为普适有效。本文旨在比较两种受限的群特征化熵锥——阿贝尔锥和同态锥，以深入理解群代数结构对信息不等式有效性的影响，并找到能将两者分离的具体不等式。

Method: 本文定义了阿贝尔熵锥（由阿贝尔群的特征化系统生成）和同态熵锥（由正规子群的陪集系统生成）。通过构造16个变量的分离泛函，利用Pálfy–Szabó六交叉恒等式的序对偶进行提升，同时量化非精确子群连接的误差。最终构造了一个阶为2^43的类二2-群的16个正规子群，使所有连接误差消失而端点包含关系恰好失败一个比特。

Result: 证明了$\widetilde{\Gamma}^{\mathrm{Abl}}_{16}\subsetneq\widetilde{\Gamma}^{\mathrm{Hom}}_{16}$，即阿贝尔熵锥严格包含于同态熵锥；确定两锥首次不同的最小变量数满足$6\le n_{\mathrm{AH}}\le16$。该示例同时分离了混合线性熵锥与同态熵锥。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔群与一般群的同态系统在生成熵锥上确实存在本质差异，这种差异最早出现在6到16个变量的范围内。类受限的信息不等式（在阿贝尔锥上有效而非普适）确实存在，且混合线性随机变量（阿贝尔情况下）与一般同态系统在熵锥层面可以被严格区分。

Abstract: Chan and Yeung showed that finite groups suffice to determine which homogeneous linear information inequalities are universally valid. We compare two restricted group-characterizable entropy cones: the Abelian cone $\widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Abl}}_n$ and the homomorphic cone $\widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Hom}}_n$, the latter generated by coset systems of normal subgroups. We prove \[
  \widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Abl}}_{16}\subsetneq\widetildeΓ^{\mathrm{Hom}}_{16}, \] and, if $n_{\rm AH}$ is the least number of variables for which these cones differ, we show $6\le n_{\rm AH}\le16$. The separating functional is a class-restricted entropy inequality: it is valid on the Abelian cone but is not a universal information inequality. It is obtained by lifting the order dual of the Pálfy--Szabó six-cross identity while quantifying errors at inexact subgroup joins. We then construct sixteen normal subgroups of a class-two $2$-group of order $2^{43}$ for which every join error vanishes while the endpoint containment fails by one bit. Since mixed-linear random variables are Abelian, the same example also separates the mixed-linear and homomorphic entropy cones.

</details>


### [371] [Row-Boosted Ensemble Belief Propagation for Short LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09560)
*Paul Bezner,Felix Krieg,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出行增强集成（RBE）解码方法，作为最小化解码器侧多样性机制：所有集成成员共享相同的校验矩阵和BP核，仅在小部分校验行的传出消息上做增强（boosted）。在5G NR BG1 (144,96)码上，32个成员的RBE将BP-20的帧错误率从1.6×10⁻²降至1.6×10⁻³，优于饱和最小和及仿射子码集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短和中长LDPC码的BP解码受限于有限长度图效应，单一解码轨迹可能陷入陷阱或振荡，即使另一条轨迹可以成功解码。现有集成BP解码器通过多个校验矩阵、自同构、修改调度、子码或改变更新规则来创造多样性，但这些方法复杂度较高。作者希望找到一种最小化的解码器侧多样性机制。

Method: RBE解码的核心思想是：所有集成成员共享同一校验矩阵和同一BP核，仅在一小组校验行的传出消息上有差异（被增强）。通过这种最小化改动创造解码多样性，避免修改矩阵结构或更新规则。实验在5G NR BG1码、非5G短LDPC码上进行，并测试了洪泛和分层两种调度方式，以及不同块长度和码率。

Result: 在5G NR BG1 (144,96)码上，32成员的RBE将BP-20的帧错误率从1.6×10⁻²降至1.6×10⁻³（E_b/N_0=4.0 dB），性能优于相同规模的饱和最小和与仿射子码集成。增大集成规模可获得额外增益，表明RBE提供了可扩展的性能-复杂度折中。增益可迁移到不同5G NR块长度和码率、非5G短LDPC码，以及洪泛和分层调度。

Conclusion: RBE是一种简单而有效的集成BP解码方法，通过仅增强少量校验行的传出消息即可显著提升短和中长LDPC码的解码性能。其增益具有跨码率、块长度、码构造和调度方式的良好通用性，且性能-复杂度折中可通过调整集成规模灵活控制。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding of short and moderate-length low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes is limited by finite-length graph effects: a single decoder trajectory can become trapped or oscillatory even when an alternative trajectory would decode the received word. Existing ensemble-BP decoders create the required diversity through multiple parity-check matrices, automorphisms, modified schedules, subcodes, or altered update rules. We introduce row-boosted ensemble (RBE) decoding as a minimal decoder-side diversity mechanism: all ensemble members share the same parity-check matrix and the same BP kernel, and differ only in a small set of parity-check rows whose outgoing messages are boosted. On the 5G~NR BG1 \((144,96)\) code, RBE with \(32\) members lowers the frame-error rate of BP with \(20\) iterations (BP-20) from \(1.6\times10^{-2}\) to \(1.6\times10^{-3}\) at \(E_\mathrm{b}/N_0=4.0\,\mathrm{dB}\), outperforming saturated-min-sum and affine subcode ensembles of equal size. Increasing the ensemble size yields additional gains, indicating that RBE provides a scalable performance-complexity tradeoff. The gains transfer across 5G~NR block lengths and rates, to non-5G short LDPC codes, and across flooding and layered schedules.

</details>


### [372] [SECS: A Soft Ensemble-Combining Stage for Low-Latency Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09563)
*Felix Krieg,Paul Bezner,Stephan ten Brink*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软集成合并阶段（SECS），利用浅层多样化的置信传播（BP）解码器作为"排序器"而非"纠错器"，通过少量迭代后合并外信息得到高可靠性的位置排序，再用有序统计解码（OSD）等重编码阶段将排序转化为接近最大似然（ML）的候选码字，在显著减少BP迭代次数的同时缩小了与ML解码的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集成解码（ensemble decoding）通过并行运行M个多样化的BP解码器来换取解码延迟，但在选择输出时，只要没有一个成员找到正确码字，解码就会失败。作者观察到浅层多样化的BP成员虽然不是好的纠错器，但却是优秀的排序器，这一发现促使他们设计新的方案来利用这一特性。

Method: 提出软集成合并阶段（SECS），在少量迭代后将各个BP成员的外信息（extrinsic messages）进行合并，产生一个可靠性排序（reliability ordering）。该排序中最高可靠性的位置几乎无错误，随后通过重编码阶段（如有序统计解码OSD）将这些高可靠性位置转换为接近ML的候选码字。

Result: 在三种短码上进行验证：(63,30)BCH码、过完备PG(2,8)码和搜索设计的(105,53)循环码。实验表明，SECS配合OSD后处理在所有情况下都能缩小与ML解码的大部分性能差距，同时显著减少了所需的BP迭代次数。

Conclusion: 浅层多样化的BP解码器在少量迭代后提供的高可靠性位置排序可以作为有效的软信息来源，通过结合OSD重编码，可以在大幅降低BP解码延迟的同时接近ML解码性能，为超可靠低延迟通信提供了一种高效的解码方案。

Abstract: Ensemble decoding is a promising technique for ultra-reliable low-latency communication, as it trades hardware parallelism for decoding latency by running M diverse belief propagation (BP) decoders in parallel. Conventional ensembles select their output from the candidates. Hence, decoding fails whenever no member finds the correct codeword. In this work, we show that shallow, diverse BP members are poor correctors, but excellent sorters. Based on this observation, we propose a soft ensemble-combining stage (SECS) that combines extrinsic messages after only a few iterations, yielding a reliability ordering whose most reliable positions are nearly error-free. A subsequent re-encoding stage (like ordered-statistics decoding (OSD)) converts this ordering into a near-maximum likelihood (ML) candidate codeword, at a fraction of the BP latency of a fully converged ensemble. We demonstrate the proposed scheme on three short codes: a (63,30) Bose-Ray-Chaudhuri-Hocquenghem code, an overcomplete PG(2,8) code, and a search-designed (105,53) cyclic code. In all cases, the SECS with OSD post-processing closes most of the gap to ML decoding, while significantly reducing the number of required BP iterations.

</details>


### [373] [Improved Acceptance Criteria for Speculative Successive Cancellation Decoding of Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09660)
*Marvin Rübenacke,Ryan Seah,Warren J. Gross*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出基于汉明距离和椭球距离的新型接受准则，并结合码成员测试，提升投机连续消除（Spec-SC）解码中右分支投机执行的接受率，从而在高吞吐低延迟的极化码解码场景中获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代数据通道应用中的极化码需要高吞吐量、低延迟的解码算法。已有的投机连续消除（Spec-SC）解码通过并行执行SC解码的右分支来降低平均解码延迟，但其接受率有限，尤其对低速率节点和高速率节点的加速效果不足。

Method: 1）提出基于汉明距离和椭球距离的新型接受准则，提高投机解码的接受率，从而对低速率节点实现更大加速；2）将码成员测试与投机解码相结合，加速高速率节点；3）在g函数计算完成后验证投机结果。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的两种方法在延迟方面均大幅优于快速简化SC（FSSC）解码和采用原始接受条件的Spec-SC解码，且错误率性能几乎相同。

Conclusion: 通过改进接受准则并引入码成员测试，可在不牺牲错误率性能的前提下显著降低极化码解码延迟，为高吞吐低延迟的数据通道应用提供更优方案。

Abstract: Next-generation data-channel applications of polar codes demand decoding algorithms with high throughput and low latency. The recently proposed speculative successive cancellation (Spec-SC) decoding reduces average decoding latency by speculatively executing the right branch of successive cancellation (SC) decoding in parallel with the left branch, verifying the result once the g-function is computed. In this paper, we propose new acceptance criteria based on Hamming distance and ellipsoidal distance that improve acceptance rates, enabling greater speed-ups for lower-rate nodes. We further show that combining code membership testing with speculative decoding accelerates high-rate nodes as well. Numerical results confirm that both approaches outperform fast simplified SC (FSSC) decoding and Spec-SC with the original acceptance condition in terms latency by a wide margin, with virtually identical error-rate performance.

</details>


### [374] [Entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes: bounds and constructions with availability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09886)
*Gretchen L. Matthews,Julia Shapiro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文定义了具有可用性的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码（EA-QLRC），允许多达δ-1个擦除可以从t个局部恢复集中任意一个恢复，每个恢复集大小不超过r+δ-1，且恢复集仅在擦除坐标处相交。文章证明了共享纠缠使得t>1成为可能，建立了该码族的Singleton型界，并基于Vandermonde校验矩阵的经典线性码给出了随机构造，此外还从Tamo-Barg码、纤维积码以及单点Hermitian码和Suzuki码等代数几何码及其折叠版本提供了显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局部可恢复码在实际存储和容错量子计算中具有重要意义，但传统量子LRC往往受限于只有单一恢复集（t=1）。作者希望利用纠缠辅助（EA）框架突破这一限制，使得同一组擦除可以拥有多个可用的局部恢复集，从而提高量子存储系统的容错性和恢复灵活性。

Method: 本文将纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码的可用性概念形式化，给出其参数定义（恢复集大小、相交条件等）；推导了该码族的Singleton型上界；利用Vandermonde奇偶校验矩阵构造经典随机线性码，再通过纠缠辅助CSS构造将其转化为量子码；并从Tamo-Barg码、纤维积码、Hermitian码和Suzuki码等经典码族及其折叠版本出发，给出显式的量子局部可恢复码构造。

Result: 证明了在有共享纠缠辅助的条件下，量子局部可恢复码可以实现t>1的多个可用性恢复集；建立了该码族的Singleton型界；通过随机与显式两类构造方法，获得了多种达到或接近该界参数的纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码，覆盖了从代数结构到几何结构的多种码族。

Conclusion: 纠缠辅助量子局部可恢复码可突破传统量子LRC中多恢复集不可用的限制，具有良好的理论界和多种构造方案，为进一步研究量子存储容错和量子局部可恢复码设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: In this work, we define entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes with availability, in which any set of up to $δ-1$ erased qudits can be recovered from any one of $t$ local recovery sets, each of size at most $r+δ-1$, with the recovery sets intersecting exactly in the erased coordinates, where $r$ is a (small) positive integer. We show that shared entanglement permits $t>1$, meaning that multiple local recovery sets can be available for the same set of up to $δ-1$ erasures. We establish a Singleton-like bound for this family of codes and present random constructions based on classical linear codes with Vandermonde parity-check matrices. We also provide explicit constructions of entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes with availability from several classical code families and their folded versions, including Tamo-Barg codes, fiber-product codes, and algebraic-geometry codes such as one-point Hermitian and Suzuki codes.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [375] [Differentiate the Solver, Not the Equation: Reverse-Sweep Adjoints for Block Implicit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08559)
*Lei Shu,Ying Jiang,Kui Wu,Yin Yang,Leonidas Guibas,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出求解器级微分（solver-level differentiation），通过对求解器本身执行的有限计算进行反向传播，而非对收敛方程或展开计算图求导。在Vertex Block Descent上实例化后，其反向彩色Gauss-Seidel扫描仅由局部3×3伴随求解组成，比展开自动微分快33倍、省71倍内存，并可将可微弹性动力学扩展到单GPU上的10^6个接触耦合软体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真中通过非线性隐式求解器求梯度是学习、控制和反问题中的关键，但现有方法存在明显缺陷：展开自动微分的内存随求解器深度增长，而方程级隐式微分需要组装全局雅可比矩阵并求解大规模稀疏伴随系统，丢弃了前向求解器的局部性。更重要的是，它微分的是收敛后的方程而非实际执行的有限计算，导致梯度不准确。

Method: 提出求解器级微分范式：当求解器由块隐式更新组成时，其离散伴随通过按逆序应用对应的伴随更新获得，形成反向扫描公式，使反向传播与前向求解器一一镜像。从算子视角看，前向通过有序局部求解实现近似逆，反向则通过反向局部伴随求解应用其转置，不构造任何全局系统。将该框架实例化到Vertex Block Descent上，得到由纯局部3×3伴随求解组成的反向彩色Gauss-Seidel扫描。

Result: 反向梯度与穿过完全相同前向计算的自动微分在机器精度上一致；而方程级伴随在一个扫描后偏差达37%。在同代码库、同GPU的受控对比中，比展开自动微分快33倍、省71倍内存。同一构造在投影动力学和扩展位置动力学上同样精确。成功将可微弹性动力学扩展到单GPU上的10^6个接触耦合软体（800万顶点）。

Conclusion: 求解器自身的结构可以作为可微仿真的实用组织原则，通过利用前向求解器的结构性局部性，可以同时获得梯度精度、计算效率和内存效益，为大规模可微物理仿真提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation is a key component in learning, control, and inverse problems, where gradients through nonlinear implicit solvers are required. Existing approaches either rely on unrolled automatic differentiation, whose memory grows with solver depth, or on equation-level implicit differentiation, which assembles global Jacobians and solves large sparse adjoint systems, discarding the locality of the forward solver -- and differentiating the converged equation rather than the finite computation that actually ran. We propose solver-level differentiation, which differentiates the executed solver itself. When a solver is composed of block implicit updates, its discrete adjoint is obtained by applying the corresponding adjoint updates in reverse order, yielding a reverse-sweep formulation whose backward pass mirrors the forward solver. From an operator perspective, the forward pass realizes an approximate inverse through ordered local solves, and the backward applies its transpose through reverse local adjoint solves, constructing no global system. We instantiate this idea on Vertex Block Descent, yielding a differentiable solver whose reverse colored Gauss-Seidel sweeps are composed entirely of local $3\times 3$ adjoint solves. The backward matches automatic differentiation through the identical executed forward to machine precision at every solver depth, where the equation-level adjoint is off by 37% after one sweep; in a controlled same-codebase, same-GPU comparison it is 33x faster and uses 71x less memory than unrolled automatic differentiation; and the same construction is exact on projective dynamics and extended position-based dynamics. We scale differentiable elastodynamics to $10^6$ contact-coupled soft bodies (8M vertices) on one GPU. Overall, this work highlights solver structure as a practical organizing principle for efficient differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [376] [Adaptive Volumetric Parameterization of Simply Connected 3-Manifolds with Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08672)
*Zhiyuan Lyu,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单连通三维流形的体参数化框架，该框架在优化过程中联合控制局部形状和质量畸变，并自适应调整目标域，支持三种不同灵活度的目标域设置（固定椭球、自适应椭球、自由边界域），通过三维拟共形形状更新、扩散密度均衡更新和几何修正三步骤实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体参数化方法大多仅使用标准化的目标域（如实心球），忽略了给定三维流形的整体形状，导致显著了几何畸变，影响后续的形状处理与分析任务。

Method: 提出一个针对单连通三维流形的体参数化框架，包含三种渐进灵活的目标域设置：预设实心椭球、变半径的体积归一化自适应椭球、以及海中嵌入的自由边界域。每种设置下的算法由三部分组成：三维拟共形性形状更新、基于扩散的密度均衡更新、以及去除单元折叠的几何修正过程。

Result: 实验结果表明该框架能有效实现不同期望效果的体参数化。该框架易于应用于多分辨率与局部自适应体网格重构、体配准和体变形等任务。

Conclusion: 本文为三维流形的表示、处理和分析提供了一种新途径，通过自适应目标域和联合控制形状/质量畸变，克服了传统标准化域带来的几何畸变问题。

Abstract: Volumetric parameterization, the process of mapping a 3-manifold onto a simplified volumetric domain, is important for many tasks in computer graphics and imaging science. However, most prior volumetric parameterization approaches have only utilized standardized domains such as a solid ball regardless of the overall shape of the given 3-manifolds, which introduces significant geometric distortion and affects the subsequent shape processing and analysis tasks. To overcome this issue, in this work we propose a novel volumetric parameterization framework for simply connected 3-manifolds. Specifically, the proposed framework jointly controls local shape and mass distortions, while adapting the target domain during the optimization process. It enables three progressively more flexible target-domain settings for the parameterization: a prescribed solid ellipsoid, a volume-normalized adaptive ellipsoid with variable radii, and a sea-embedded free-boundary domain. For each setting, the parameterization algorithm consists of a 3D quasi-conformality shape update, a diffusion-based density-equalizing update, and a geometric correction procedure for removing element foldings, thereby allowing for volumetric parameterizations with different desired effects. Experimental results are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed framework. Moreover, our framework can be easily applied to multiresolution and localized adaptive volumetric remeshing, volumetric registration, and volumetric morphing. Altogether, our work provides a new way for the representation, processing, and analysis of 3-manifolds.

</details>


### [377] [Smooth Motion Stitching via Laplacian Optimization in Rodrigues Vector Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08986)
*Ryosuke Higasayama,Hideki Todo,Jongseong Gwak*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Rodrigues向量空间中拉普拉斯优化的运动编辑框架，用于平滑运动拼接。通过将关节旋转表示为连续的Rodrigues向量，将运动拼接形式化为时间拉普拉斯优化问题，在保持参考运动特征性时间变化的同时实现运动片段间的平滑过渡，支持类别内替换和跨类别拼接，不依赖学习模型且计算高效。实验表明该框架在不同运动差异下均能产生稳定、视觉连贯的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动拼接方法在处理平滑过渡时面临挑战：基于学习的方法需要大量训练数据且泛化能力有限，传统线性插值在运动差异较大时会产生不自然的过渡。同时，旋转表示的选取对时间优化的稳定性至关重要，但此前缺乏对旋转连续性及其对运动拼接影响的分析。

Method: 将关节旋转表示为连续的Rodrigues向量（避免欧拉角奇异性），在此基础上将运动拼接建模为时间域上的拉普拉斯优化问题。该方法保留参考运动的二阶时间变化特征以维持运动风格，同时支持类别内替换和跨类别拼接，无需学习模型或手动调参。此外，论文还从旋转连续性角度分析了旋转轴反转在实际运动数据中的稀有性，解释了合成轴翻转场景中的数值不稳定问题为何在实际拼接中不出现。

Result: 通过一系列拼接实验及与线性插值的对比，证明拉普拉斯编辑在不同运动差异条件下都能产生稳定且视觉连贯的过渡。旋转连续性分析表明旋转轴反转在实际运动数据中极罕见，从而解释了框架在实际场景中的数值稳定性。

Conclusion: 旋转表示的选择对于稳定时间优化至关重要；所提出的框架不仅适用于动画创作，还可扩展至运动分析以及融合感知或生理线索的未来应用。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion editing framework for smooth motion stitching based on Laplacian optimization in Rodrigues vector space. By representing joint rotations as continuous Rodrigues vectors, motion stitching is formulated as a temporal Laplacian optimization problem, enabling smooth transitions between motion segments while preserving characteristic temporal variations of reference motions. The proposed approach supports both intra-category replacement and cross-category motion stitching without relying on learning-based models or complex manual tuning, and is computationally efficient for interactive editing. Through a series of stitching experiments and comparisons with linear interpolation, we demonstrate that Laplacian editing produces stable and visually coherent transitions under a wide range of motion differences. Furthermore, an analysis of rotational continuity clarifies that rotation-axis inversions are rare in real motion data and explains why numerical instabilities observed in synthetic axis-flipping scenarios do not arise in practical motion stitching. These results highlight the importance of rotational representation in stabilizing temporal optimization and suggest that the proposed framework is well suited not only for animation authoring but also for motion analysis and future extensions incorporating perceptual or physiological cues.

</details>


### [378] [A Hybrid Neural-Microfacet BRDF Model for Real-Time Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09604)
*Louis De Oliveira,Anastasia Karpova,Georges Nader,Antoine Houdard,Pierre Mezieres,Damien Rioux-Lavoie,Romain Pacanowski*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合BRDF模型，将GGX型微表面模型与神经网络模型相结合，利用微表面模型提供基础外观，神经网络仅负责修正差异，从而在保持低计算开销和可编辑性的同时，以更小的网络规模实现优于现有神经模型的测量数据拟合精度，适用于实时和离线渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微表面BRDF模型虽广泛用于实时渲染，但无法精确再现复杂光-表面交互产生的细微外观效果；而专门针对特定光学现象（如衍射、虹彩、多层结构）的物理模型虽更精确，却缺乏通用性且难以满足实时渲染的性能要求。现有神经BRDF模型虽然能逼近参考数据，但网络规模较大，实时渲染成本过高。

Method: 提出一个混合模型，将GGX型微表面模型与神经模型结合：微表面组件提供基础外观近似，神经网络组件负责修正微表面模型与实际BRDF数据之间的残差。由于神经部分只需学习微表面模型未能捕捉的差异，网络规模可大幅缩小，同时保留了微表面模型的可编辑性和重要性采样优势。

Result: 在相同内存成本下，该混合模型对测量数据的逼近精度优于现有的先进神经BRDF模型，且评估开销仅略高于纯微表面模型，远低于现有神经模型；同时保持了艺术家可编辑性和重要性采样能力，适用于离线与实时渲染场景。

Conclusion: 混合微表面-神经网络BRDF模型能有效兼顾微表面模型的轻量高效、可编辑特性与神经网络的表达力，以较小的网络规模实现高精度测量数据拟合，为实时渲染中复杂外观的再现提供了一条实用且高效的路径。

Abstract: Over the past decade, microfacet-based BRDF models have formed the foundation of real-time rendering pipelines. Despite their widespread use, they often fail to reproduce subtle appearance effects arising from complex light-surface interactions, which have led to the emergence of specialized physics-based models for specific optical phenomena (e.g., diffraction, iridescence, multilayers). Although more accurate, these models lose versatility and lack performance for real-time rendering. Recently introduced, neural models have demonstrated their ability to approximate BRDF reference data coming from measurements, simulations, or even complex shading networks. However, most current neural models require relatively large networks, making them costly for real-time rendering. In this paper, we introduce a hybrid model that combines a GGX-type microfacet model and a neural model to leverage the best features of both representations. The neural component corrects the appearance approximated by the microfacet component, allowing much smaller network than in existing neural models. We show that, at identical memory cost, our model approximates measurements better than state-of-the-art neural models for a low evaluation overhead compared to a microfacet-based model. Furthermore, our hybrid model remains easily editable by artists and benefits from an important sampling scheme, making it attractive for both offline and real-time rendering.

</details>
